class MainApp extends StatelessWidget {
  const MainApp({super.key});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return MaterialApp(
      title: 'Pals',
      debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
      theme: buildAppTheme(),
      home: const SplashPage(),
      routes: {
        '/name-preview': (_) => NamePage(onContinue: (_) async {}),
        '/interests-preview': (_) => InterestsPage(
          onStart: (_) async => true,
        ),
        '/plaza-preview': (_) => const PlazaPage(),
        '/login-preview': (_) => PhoneLoginPage(
          service: account,
          preview: true,
          onAuthenticated: (_) {},
        ),
        '/login-code-preview': (_) => PhoneLoginPage(
          service: account,
          preview: true,
          initialCodeStep: true,
          initialPhone: '12345678911',
          onAuthenticated: (_) {},
        ),
        '/chat-returning-preview': (_) =>
            const ChatPreviewPage(state: ChatPreviewState.returning),
        '/chat-preview': (_) => const ChatPreviewPage(),
        '/chat-conversation-preview': (_) =>
            const ChatPreviewPage(state: ChatPreviewState.conversation),
        '/chat-recording-preview': (_) =>
            const ChatPreviewPage(state: ChatPreviewState.recording),
        '/splash': (_) => const SplashArtwork(),
        '/welcome-preview': (_) => const OnboardingPreviewPage(),
        '/character-preview': (_) => const CharacterPreviewPage(),
        '/splash-preview': (_) => const SplashPreviewPage(),
      },
    );
  }
}
/// 由 --dart-define-from-file 注入，修改配置后需要停止并重新运行。
abstract final class AppEnv {
  static const enableTestTools = bool.fromEnvironment('ENABLE_TEST_TOOLS');
  static const name = String.fromEnvironment(
    'APP_ENV',
    defaultValue: 'development',
  );
  static const apiProduct = String.fromEnvironment('API_PRODUCT');
  static const apiPlatform = String.fromEnvironment('API_PLATFORM');
  static const apiSecretKey = String.fromEnvironment('API_SECRET_KEY');
  static const accountBaseUrl = String.fromEnvironment(
    'ACCOUNT_BASE_URL',
    defaultValue: 'https://account-api.ilivedata.com',
  );
  static const googleIosClientId = String.fromEnvironment(
    'GOOGLE_IOS_CLIENT_ID',
  );
  static const googleServerClientId = String.fromEnvironment(
    'GOOGLE_SERVER_CLIENT_ID',
  );
  static const apiBaseUrl = String.fromEnvironment('API_BASE_URL');
}
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import '../config/app_env.dart';
import 'request_signer.dart';
export 'request_signer.dart';
export 'package:dio/dio.dart'
    show
        BaseOptions,
        CancelToken,
        Dio,
        DioException,
        DioExceptionType,
        FormData,
        Headers,
        InterceptorsWrapper,
        MultipartFile,
        Options,
        ProgressCallback,
        RequestOptions,
        Response;
/// 在 [Options.extra] 中设置该键为 `true`，可跳过全局 401 处理。
/// 典型场景：登录接口本身返回 401 时，不应触发“登录已过期”。
const kSkipUnauthorizedHandler = 'skipUnauthorizedHandler';
/// 全项目共用的客户端，地址由环境配置注入。
final api = ApiClient(
  baseUrl: AppEnv.apiBaseUrl,
  signer: RequestSigner(
    product: AppEnv.apiProduct,
    platform: AppEnv.apiPlatform,
    secretKey: AppEnv.apiSecretKey,
  ),
);
class ApiClient {
  ApiClient({
    required String baseUrl,
    Dio? dio,
    this.signer,
    this.onUnauthorized,
  }) : _dio = dio ?? Dio() {
    _dio.options = _dio.options.copyWith(
      baseUrl: baseUrl,
      connectTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 15),
      sendTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 30),
      receiveTimeout: const Duration(seconds: 30),
    );
    _dio.interceptors.add(
      InterceptorsWrapper(
        onRequest: (options, handler) {
          final signing = signer;
          if (signing == null) {
            handler.next(options);
            return;
          }
          try {
            if (options.method.toUpperCase() != 'POST') {
              throw ArgumentError('签名接口仅支持 POST 请求');
            }
            final body = options.data is String
                ? options.data as String
                : jsonEncode(options.data ?? <String, dynamic>{});
            // 校验传入的字符串确实是 JSON；保留其原始空白和字段顺序。
            jsonDecode(body);
            final headers = signing.headers(uri: options.uri, jsonBody: body);
            options.headers = {
              for (final entry in options.headers.entries)
                if (!headers.keys.any(
                  (name) => name.toLowerCase() == entry.key.toLowerCase(),
                ))
                  entry.key: entry.value,
              ...headers,
            };
            options.contentType = headers['Content-Type'];
            // 发送已签名的字节，避免 Dio 再次序列化或编码。
            options.data = utf8.encode(body);
            options.followRedirects = false;
            handler.next(options);
          } catch (error, stackTrace) {
            handler.reject(
              DioException(
                requestOptions: options,
                error: error,
                stackTrace: stackTrace,
                message: '请求签名失败：$error',
              ),
            );
          }
        },
        onError: (error, handler) {
          final isUnauthorized = error.response?.statusCode == 401;
          final skip =
              error.requestOptions.extra[kSkipUnauthorizedHandler] == true;
          if (signer == null && isUnauthorized && !skip) {
            setToken(null);
            onUnauthorized?.call();
          }
          handler.next(error);
        },
      ),
    );
    if (kDebugMode) {
      // 不输出原始异常、完整 URI、请求头或响应体，避免凭证进入日志。
      _dio.interceptors.add(
        InterceptorsWrapper(
          onRequest: (options, handler) {
            debugPrint('[HTTP] ${options.method} ${options.uri.path}');
            handler.next(options);
          },
          onResponse: (response, handler) {
            debugPrint(
              '[HTTP] ${response.requestOptions.method} '
              '${response.requestOptions.uri.path} HTTP=${response.statusCode}',
            );
            handler.next(response);
          },
          onError: (error, handler) {
            debugPrint(
              '[HTTP] ${error.requestOptions.method} '
              '${error.requestOptions.uri.path} FAILED '
              'HTTP=${error.response?.statusCode ?? "无响应"} type=${error.type.name}',
            );
            handler.next(error);
          },
        ),
      );
    }
  }
  final Dio _dio;
  final RequestSigner? signer;
  /// token 失效（HTTP 401）时回调，可用于清理登录态、跳转登录页。
  /// 单个请求可通过 [kSkipUnauthorizedHandler] 跳过。
  final void Function()? onUnauthorized;
  /// 底层 Dio，供测试替换或进阶配置（如刷新 token 拦截器）。
  Dio get dio => _dio;
  /// 登录后设置，退出登录传 null。只保存在内存，不负责持久化。
  void setToken(String? token) {
    if (signer != null) {
      throw StateError('Authorization 用于请求签名，登录凭证字段需按业务接口文档配置');
    }
    if (token == null || token.isEmpty) {
      _dio.options.headers.remove('Authorization');
    } else {
      _dio.options.headers['Authorization'] = 'Bearer $token';
    }
  }
  /// 返回完整响应体；不自动剥离 data 或判断业务 code。
  /// 网络和 HTTP 错误保留为 DioException，模型转换错误原样抛出。
  /// [method] 为空时沿用 [options] 中的 method（默认 GET），不会被静默覆盖。
  Future<T> request<T>(
    String path, {
    String? method,
    Map<String, dynamic>? query,
    Object? data,
    T Function(dynamic body)? decode,
    Options? options,
    CancelToken? cancelToken,
    ProgressCallback? onSendProgress,
    ProgressCallback? onReceiveProgress,
  }) async {
    final response = await _send(
      path,
      method: method,
      query: query,
      data: data,
      options: options,
      cancelToken: cancelToken,
      onSendProgress: onSendProgress,
      onReceiveProgress: onReceiveProgress,
    );
    if (decode != null) return decode(response.data);
    final body = response.data;
    if (body is T) return body;
    if (body == null) {
      throw ApiDecodeException('接口 $path 返回空响应体，无法转换为 $T');
    }
    throw ApiDecodeException('接口 $path 返回 ${body.runtimeType}，无法转换为 $T');
  }
  /// 适用于 204 等无响应体的接口，忽略返回内容。
  Future<void> requestVoid(
    String path, {
    String? method,
    Map<String, dynamic>? query,
    Object? data,
    Options? options,
    CancelToken? cancelToken,
    ProgressCallback? onSendProgress,
    ProgressCallback? onReceiveProgress,
  }) async {
    await _send(
      path,
      method: method,
      query: query,
      data: data,
      options: options,
      cancelToken: cancelToken,
      onSendProgress: onSendProgress,
      onReceiveProgress: onReceiveProgress,
    );
  }
  Future<Response<dynamic>> _send(
    String path, {
    String? method,
    Map<String, dynamic>? query,
    Object? data,
    Options? options,
    CancelToken? cancelToken,
    ProgressCallback? onSendProgress,
    ProgressCallback? onReceiveProgress,
  }) {
    if (_dio.options.baseUrl.isEmpty && !Uri.parse(path).hasScheme) {
      throw StateError('请在环境配置文件中填写 API_BASE_URL，并停止后重新运行应用');
    }
    final base = options ?? Options();
    return _dio.request<dynamic>(
      path,
      data: data,
      queryParameters: query,
      options: method == null ? base : base.copyWith(method: method),
      cancelToken: cancelToken,
      onSendProgress: onSendProgress,
      onReceiveProgress: onReceiveProgress,
    );
  }
  Future<T> get<T>(
    String path, {
    Map<String, dynamic>? query,
    T Function(dynamic body)? decode,
    Options? options,
    CancelToken? cancelToken,
    ProgressCallback? onReceiveProgress,
  }) => request<T>(
    path,
    query: query,
    decode: decode,
    options: options,
    cancelToken: cancelToken,
    onReceiveProgress: onReceiveProgress,
  );
  Future<T> post<T>(
    String path, {
    Object? data,
    Map<String, dynamic>? query,
    T Function(dynamic body)? decode,
    Options? options,
    CancelToken? cancelToken,
    ProgressCallback? onSendProgress,
    ProgressCallback? onReceiveProgress,
  }) => request<T>(
    path,
    method: 'POST',
    data: data,
    query: query,
    decode: decode,
    options: options,
    cancelToken: cancelToken,
    onSendProgress: onSendProgress,
    onReceiveProgress: onReceiveProgress,
  );
  Future<T> put<T>(
    String path, {
    Object? data,
    Map<String, dynamic>? query,
    T Function(dynamic body)? decode,
    Options? options,
    CancelToken? cancelToken,
    ProgressCallback? onSendProgress,
    ProgressCallback? onReceiveProgress,
  }) => request<T>(
    path,
    method: 'PUT',
    data: data,
    query: query,
    decode: decode,
    options: options,
    cancelToken: cancelToken,
    onSendProgress: onSendProgress,
    onReceiveProgress: onReceiveProgress,
  );
  Future<T> patch<T>(
    String path, {
    Object? data,
    Map<String, dynamic>? query,
    T Function(dynamic body)? decode,
    Options? options,
    CancelToken? cancelToken,
    ProgressCallback? onSendProgress,
    ProgressCallback? onReceiveProgress,
  }) => request<T>(
    path,
    method: 'PATCH',
    data: data,
    query: query,
    decode: decode,
    options: options,
    cancelToken: cancelToken,
    onSendProgress: onSendProgress,
    onReceiveProgress: onReceiveProgress,
  );
  Future<T> delete<T>(
    String path, {
    Object? data,
    Map<String, dynamic>? query,
    T Function(dynamic body)? decode,
    Options? options,
    CancelToken? cancelToken,
  }) => request<T>(
    path,
    method: 'DELETE',
    data: data,
    query: query,
    decode: decode,
    options: options,
    cancelToken: cancelToken,
  );
}
/// 响应体与期望类型不一致时抛出，用于尽早暴露接口契约问题。
class ApiDecodeException implements Exception {
  ApiDecodeException(this.message);
  final String message;
  @override
  String toString() => 'ApiDecodeException: $message';
}
/// 把 [DioException] 转成可直接展示给用户的中文提示。
extension DioExceptionUserMessage on DioException {
  String get userMessage {
    switch (type) {
      case DioExceptionType.connectionTimeout:
      case DioExceptionType.sendTimeout:
      case DioExceptionType.receiveTimeout:
        return '请求超时，请稍后重试';
      case DioExceptionType.transformTimeout:
        return '数据处理超时，请稍后重试';
      case DioExceptionType.connectionError:
        return '网络连接失败，请检查网络后重试';
      case DioExceptionType.badCertificate:
        return '安全证书校验失败';
      case DioExceptionType.cancel:
        return '请求已取消';
      case DioExceptionType.badResponse:
        final status = response?.statusCode;
        if (status != null) {
          if (status == 401) return '登录已过期，请重新登录';
          if (status == 403) return '没有权限访问该资源';
          if (status == 404) return '请求的资源不存在';
          if (status >= 500) return '服务器开小差了，请稍后重试';
          return '请求失败（HTTP $status）';
        }
        return '服务器返回异常';
      case DioExceptionType.unknown:
        return '请求失败，请稍后重试';
    }
  }
}
import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
/// 类似 useRequest：页面持有实例，通过 ListenableBuilder 监听状态。
/// run 捕获错误并存入 error；重新运行时取消旧请求，只采用最后一次结果。
class RequestController<T> extends ChangeNotifier {
  T? _data;
  Object? _error;
  bool _loading = false;
  bool _disposed = false;
  CancelToken? _token;
  T? get data => _data;
  Object? get error => _error;
  bool get loading => _loading;
  Future<void> run(Future<T> Function(CancelToken token) task) async {
    assert(!_disposed, 'RequestController 已 dispose，不能再调用 run');
    if (_disposed) return;
    _token?.cancel('请求已被替换');
    final token = CancelToken();
    _token = token;
    _loading = true;
    _error = null;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      final result = await task(token);
      if (!_disposed && identical(_token, token)) _data = result;
    } catch (error) {
      if (!_disposed && identical(_token, token) && !token.isCancelled) {
        _error = error;
      }
    } finally {
      if (!_disposed && identical(_token, token)) {
        _loading = false;
        _token = null;
        notifyListeners();
      }
    }
  }
  /// 取消时保留已有数据，不作为请求失败处理；上一次的 error 也不清空。
  void cancel() {
    if (_disposed) return;
    _token?.cancel('请求已取消');
    _token = null;
    _loading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _disposed = true;
    _token?.cancel('页面已销毁');
    _token = null;
    super.dispose();
  }
}
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:crypto/crypto.dart';
/// 密钥按 UTF-8 原文使用，不做 Base64 解码。
class RequestSigner {
  const RequestSigner({
    required this.product,
    required this.platform,
    required this.secretKey,
    this.clock = DateTime.now,
  });
  final String product;
  final String platform;
  final String secretKey;
  final DateTime Function() clock;
  Map<String, String> headers({required Uri uri, required String jsonBody}) {
    if (product.isEmpty || platform.isEmpty || secretKey.isEmpty) {
      throw StateError('请配置 API_PRODUCT、API_PLATFORM 和 API_SECRET_KEY');
    }
    final now = clock().toUtc();
    final timestamp = DateTime.utc(
      now.year,
      now.month,
      now.day,
      now.hour,
      now.minute,
      now.second,
    ).toIso8601String().replaceFirst('.000Z', 'Z');
    final host = uri.hasPort
        ? '${uri.host}:${uri.port}'.toLowerCase()
        : uri.host.toLowerCase();
    final stringToSign = [
      'POST',
      host,
      uri.path.isEmpty ? '/' : uri.path,
      sha256.convert(utf8.encode(jsonBody)).toString(),
      'X-Product:$product',
      'X-Platform:$platform',
      'X-TimeStamp:$timestamp',
    ].join('\n');
    final signature = Hmac(
      sha256,
      utf8.encode(secretKey),
    ).convert(utf8.encode(stringToSign));
    return {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json;charset=UTF-8',
      'Accept': 'application/json;charset=UTF-8',
      'X-Product': product,
      'X-Platform': platform,
      'X-TimeStamp': timestamp,
      'Authorization': base64Encode(signature.bytes),
    };
  }
}
/// 响应字段结构需由具体业务接口解析，再传入错误码获取提示。
String? signatureErrorMessage(Object? code) => switch (code.toString()) {
  '1101' => 'HTTP请求头缺少参数',
  '1102' => 'HTTP请求头参数错误',
  '1103' => '请求签名已过期，请检查设备时间后重试',
  '1104' => '请求签名无效',
  _ => null,
};
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
/// Figma controls keep their artwork unchanged while pressed. Selection,
/// loading and disabled states still come from the individual components.
ThemeData buildAppTheme() {
  const quietButton = ButtonStyle(
    splashFactory: NoSplash.splashFactory,
    overlayColor: WidgetStatePropertyAll(Colors.transparent),
    elevation: WidgetStatePropertyAll(0),
    shadowColor: WidgetStatePropertyAll(Colors.transparent),
  );
  return ThemeData(
    colorScheme: ColorScheme.fromSeed(seedColor: const Color(0xFF3659C9)),
    useMaterial3: true,
    splashFactory: NoSplash.splashFactory,
    splashColor: Colors.transparent,
    highlightColor: Colors.transparent,
    hoverColor: Colors.transparent,
    textButtonTheme: const TextButtonThemeData(style: quietButton),
    filledButtonTheme: const FilledButtonThemeData(style: quietButton),
    elevatedButtonTheme: const ElevatedButtonThemeData(style: quietButton),
    outlinedButtonTheme: const OutlinedButtonThemeData(style: quietButton),
    iconButtonTheme: const IconButtonThemeData(style: quietButton),
    floatingActionButtonTheme: const FloatingActionButtonThemeData(
      splashColor: Colors.transparent,
      elevation: 0,
      focusElevation: 0,
      hoverElevation: 0,
      highlightElevation: 0,
      disabledElevation: 0,
    ),
    chipTheme: const ChipThemeData(
      elevation: 0,
      pressElevation: 0,
      shadowColor: Colors.transparent,
    ),
    inputDecorationTheme: const InputDecorationTheme(
      border: OutlineInputBorder(),
    ),
  );
}
import 'dart:convert';
import '../../core/config/app_env.dart';
import '../../core/network/api_client.dart';
import 'models/account_models.dart';
import 'auth_debug_log.dart';
import 'device_uuid.dart';
import 'google_identity.dart';
import 'session_store.dart';
export 'models/account_models.dart';
export 'session_store.dart';
export 'google_identity.dart';
final account = AccountService(
  client: ApiClient(
    baseUrl: AppEnv.accountBaseUrl,
    signer: RequestSigner(
      product: AppEnv.apiProduct,
      platform: AppEnv.apiPlatform,
      secretKey: AppEnv.apiSecretKey,
    ),
  ),
  store: SecureSessionStore(
    namespace:
        '${AppEnv.name}.${AppEnv.accountBaseUrl}.${AppEnv.apiProduct}.${AppEnv.apiPlatform}',
  ),
);
class AccountService {
  AccountService({
    required this.client,
    required this.store,
    DateTime Function()? clock,
    this.deviceUuidProvider = readDeviceUuid,
    GoogleIdentityProvider? googleIdentity,
  }) : _clock = clock ?? DateTime.now,
       googleIdentity = googleIdentity ?? NativeGoogleIdentityProvider();
  final ApiClient client;
  final SessionStore store;
  final GoogleIdentityProvider googleIdentity;
  final Future<String?> Function(String platform) deviceUuidProvider;
  final DateTime Function() _clock;
  final Map<String, DateTime> _nextSend = {};
  final Set<String> _sending = {};
  bool _sessionBusy = false;
  AccountSession? _session;
  bool testNewUser = false;
  Future<void> resetForNewUserTest() => _withSessionLock(() async {
    if (!AppEnv.enableTestTools) throw StateError('测试入口未启用');
    final storage = store;
    if (storage is! SecureSessionStore) throw StateError('不支持重置此存储');
    await storage.resetForNewUserTest();
    _session = null;
    testNewUser = false;
    _nextSend.clear();
  });
  Future<void> _consumeNewUserTest() async {
    final storage = store;
    testNewUser =
        AppEnv.enableTestTools &&
        storage is SecureSessionStore &&
        await storage.consumeNewUserTest();
  }
  /// 本地凭证，不代表服务器已验证 token 仍有效。
  AccountSession? get session => _session;
  /// Registration history survives logout; it must not be used for auth.
  Future<bool> hasCompletedRegistration() async {
    final storage = store;
    if (storage is RegistrationHistoryStore) {
      return (storage as RegistrationHistoryStore).hasCompletedRegistration();
    }
    return await storage.read() != null;
  }
  RequestSigner get _signer {
    final signer = client.signer;
    if (signer == null ||
        signer.secretKey.isEmpty ||
        signer.product.trim().isEmpty ||
        !{'ios', 'android', 'web'}.contains(signer.platform)) {
      throw StateError('请配置账号接口的产品、平台和对应签名密钥');
    }
    return signer;
  }
  static String normalizePhone(String phone) {
    final value = phone.trim();
    if (!RegExp(r'^[1-9][0-9]{0,2}-[0-9]+$').hasMatch(value)) {
      throw ArgumentError('手机号格式应为 国家区号-手机号码');
    }
    return value;
  }
  int resendSeconds(String phone) {
    final until = _nextSend[phone.trim()];
    if (until == null) return 0;
    final milliseconds = until.difference(_clock()).inMilliseconds;
    return milliseconds <= 0 ? 0 : (milliseconds / 1000).ceil();
  }
  Map<String, dynamic> _body(String phone, String ip) {
    final signer = _signer;
    return {
      'phone': phone,
      'ip': ip.trim(),
      'product': signer.product,
      'platform': signer.platform,
    };
  }
  Future<void> sendPhoneVerify({
    required String phone,
    String ip = '',
    String utmSource = '',
    String? lang,
    CancelToken? cancelToken,
  }) async {
    final normalized = normalizePhone(phone);
    final body = _body(normalized, ip);
    if (_sending.contains(normalized) || resendSeconds(normalized) > 0) {
      throw const AccountException(2, AccountOperation.sendCode);
    }
    _sending.add(normalized);
    try {
      await _post(
        '/account/send_phone_verify',
        {...body, 'utm_source': utmSource, 'lang': ?lang},
        AccountOperation.sendCode,
        cancelToken,
      );
      _nextSend[normalized] = _clock().add(const Duration(seconds: 60));
    } on AccountException catch (error) {
      if (error.status == 2) {
        _nextSend[normalized] = _clock().add(const Duration(seconds: 60));
      }
      rethrow;
    } finally {
      _sending.remove(normalized);
    }
  }
  Future<AccountSession> phoneVerifyLogin({
    required String phone,
    required String code,
    String ip = '',
    String? uuid,
    CancelToken? cancelToken,
  }) => _withSessionLock(() async {
    final normalized = normalizePhone(phone);
    final body = _body(normalized, ip);
    if (code.trim().isEmpty) throw ArgumentError('验证码不能为空');
    final deviceId = await _deviceUuid(uuid: uuid);
    final response = await _post(
      '/account/phone_verify_login',
      {
        ...body,
        'code': code.trim(),
        if (deviceId != null && deviceId.isNotEmpty) 'uuid': deviceId,
      },
      AccountOperation.login,
      cancelToken,
    );
    final data = response['data'];
    if (data is! Map<String, dynamic>) {
      throw ApiDecodeException('登录成功响应缺少 data');
    }
    final result = AccountSession.fromJson(data);
    if (result.product != _signer.product ||
        result.platform != _signer.platform ||
        result.phone != normalized) {
      throw ApiDecodeException('登录响应的账号、产品或平台与请求不一致');
    }
    await store.write(result);
    await _consumeNewUserTest();
    _session = result;
    return result;
  });
  Future<String?> _deviceUuid({String? uuid}) async {
    final platform = _signer.platform;
    final value =
        (uuid ??
                (platform == 'web' ? null : await deviceUuidProvider(platform)))
            ?.trim();
    if (platform != 'web' && (value == null || value.isEmpty)) {
      throw StateError('无法获取设备标识，请稍后重试');
    }
    return value;
  }
  /// Google ID Token 仅用于向账号服务换取业务 token，不用于本地登录判定。
  Future<AccountSession> googleLogin({
    String ip = '',
    String utmSource = '',
    CancelToken? cancelToken,
  }) => _withSessionLock(() async {
    final signer = _signer;
    if (cancelToken?.isCancelled == true) throw cancelToken!.cancelError!;
    final deviceId = await _deviceUuid();
    if (cancelToken?.isCancelled == true) throw cancelToken!.cancelError!;
    AuthDebugLog.event('Google 原生授权 START');
    final proof = await googleIdentity.authenticate(signer.platform);
    if (cancelToken?.isCancelled == true) throw cancelToken!.cancelError!;
    if (proof.email.trim().isEmpty || proof.idToken.isEmpty) {
      throw const GoogleLoginException(
        'missing_credential',
        'Google 未返回完整登录凭证',
      );
    }
    AuthDebugLog.event('Google 原生授权 OK，开始账号凭证交换');
    final response = await _post(
      '/account/google_login',
      {
        'email': proof.email,
        'idToken': proof.idToken,
        'ip': ip.trim(),
        'utm_source': utmSource,
        if (deviceId != null && deviceId.isNotEmpty) 'uuid': deviceId,
        'product': signer.product,
        'platform': signer.platform,
      },
      AccountOperation.googleLogin,
      cancelToken,
    );
    final data = response['data'];
    if (data is! Map<String, dynamic>) {
      throw ApiDecodeException('Google 登录成功响应缺少 data');
    }
    final result = AccountSession.fromJson(data);
    if (result.product != signer.product ||
        result.platform != signer.platform ||
        result.email.toLowerCase() != proof.email.toLowerCase()) {
      throw ApiDecodeException('Google 登录响应的账号、产品或平台与请求不一致');
    }
    await store.write(result);
    await _consumeNewUserTest();
    _session = result;
    return result;
  });
  /// 启动时调用。恢复本地状态；服务器鉴权接口尚未提供。
  Future<AccountSession?> restoreSession() => _withSessionLock(() async {
    final restored = await store.read();
    if (restored != null &&
        (restored.product != _signer.product ||
            restored.platform != _signer.platform)) {
      await store.clear();
      _session = null;
    } else {
      _session = restored;
    }
    return _session;
  });
  /// 仅清除本地凭证；文档未提供服务端注销接口。
  Future<void> logout() => _withSessionLock(() async {
    await store.clear();
    _session = null;
  });
  Future<T> _withSessionLock<T>(Future<T> Function() operation) async {
    if (_sessionBusy) throw StateError('登录状态正在更新，请稍后重试');
    _sessionBusy = true;
    try {
      return await operation();
    } finally {
      _sessionBusy = false;
    }
  }
  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> _post(
    String path,
    Map<String, dynamic> body,
    AccountOperation operation,
    CancelToken? cancelToken,
  ) async {
    Map<String, dynamic> response;
    try {
      final raw = await client.post<dynamic>(
        path,
        data: body,
        cancelToken: cancelToken,
        options: Options(extra: {kSkipUnauthorizedHandler: true}),
      );
      AuthDebugLog.responseShape(path, raw);
      Object? decoded = raw;
      if (raw is String) {
        try {
          decoded = jsonDecode(raw);
        } on FormatException {
          throw ApiDecodeException('账号接口返回非 JSON 文本，详见响应结构日志');
        }
        AuthDebugLog.responseShape(path, decoded);
      }
      if (decoded is! Map<String, dynamic>) {
        throw ApiDecodeException('账号接口应返回 JSON 对象，实际为 ${decoded.runtimeType}');
      }
      response = decoded;
    } on DioException catch (error, stack) {
      AuthDebugLog.error('POST $path', error, stack);
      final data = error.response?.data;
      AuthDebugLog.responseShape(path, data);
      if (data is Map<String, dynamic> &&
          data['status'] is int &&
          data['status'] != 0) {
        AuthDebugLog.status(path, data['status'] as int, operation);
        throw AccountException(data['status'] as int, operation);
      }
      rethrow;
    }
    final status = response['status'];
    if (status is! int) throw ApiDecodeException('账号接口响应缺少有效的 status');
    AuthDebugLog.status(path, status, operation);
    if (status != 0) throw AccountException(status, operation);
    return response;
  }
}
import 'dart:developer' as developer;
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import '../../core/network/api_client.dart';
import 'models/account_models.dart';
import 'google_identity.dart';
/// 仅输出白名单诊断字段，不序列化请求、响应或原始异常内容。
abstract final class AuthDebugLog {
  static final entries = ValueNotifier<List<String>>([]);
  /// 仅传入固定事件名称或白名单字段，禁止传入原始请求及响应。
  static void event(String message) {
    if (!kDebugMode) return;
    final line = '[Auth] ${DateTime.now().toIso8601String()} $message';
    final updated = [...entries.value, line];
    entries.value = List.unmodifiable(
      updated.length > 80 ? updated.sublist(updated.length - 80) : updated,
    );
    debugPrint(line);
    developer.log(line, name: 'bots.auth');
  }
  static void responseShape(String path, Object? body) {
    if (!kDebugMode) return;
    if (body is Map) {
      const fields = ['status', 'code', 'data', 'message', 'msg', 'error'];
      final shape = fields
          .map((key) {
            if (!body.containsKey(key)) return '$key=missing';
            final value = body[key];
            // 仅状态字段允许输出短数字值，其他字段只记录类型。
            if ((key == 'status' || key == 'code') &&
                (value is int ||
                    (value is String &&
                        RegExp(r'^-?[0-9]{1,6}$').hasMatch(value)))) {
              return '$key=$value(${value.runtimeType})';
            }
            return '$key:type=${value.runtimeType}';
          })
          .join(', ');
      event('POST $path response object fields=${body.length} {$shape}');
    } else if (body is String) {
      event(
        'POST $path response String length=${body.length} '
        'looksLikeHtml=${body.trimLeft().startsWith("<")}',
      );
    } else {
      event('POST $path response type=${body.runtimeType}');
    }
  }
  static void status(String path, int status, AccountOperation operation) {
    if (!kDebugMode) return;
    final message = status == 0
        ? '成功'
        : AccountException(status, operation).message;
    event('POST $path status=$status $message');
  }
  static void error(String operation, Object error, StackTrace stack) {
    if (!kDebugMode) return;
    String detail;
    if (error is GoogleLoginException) {
      detail = 'Google code=${error.code} ${error.message}';
    } else if (error is AccountException) {
      detail = 'status=${error.status} ${error.message}';
    } else if (error is DioException) {
      detail =
          'HTTP=${error.response?.statusCode ?? "无响应"} '
          'type=${error.type.name} ${error.userMessage} '
          'cause=${error.error?.runtimeType ?? "none"}';
    } else if (error is PlatformException) {
      // message/details 可能含凭证或设备信息，只记录原生错误码。
      final code = error.code.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^a-zA-Z0-9_.-]'), '_');
      detail = 'PlatformException code=$code（设备读取或安全存储失败）';
    } else if (error is ApiDecodeException) {
      detail = '接口响应格式错误：${error.message}';
    } else if (error is ArgumentError) {
      detail = '请求参数校验失败';
    } else if (error is StateError) {
      detail = '配置、设备标识或会话状态异常；具体提示见页面';
    } else {
      detail = 'type=${error.runtimeType}';
    }
    event('$operation FAILED $detail');
    debugPrintStack(label: '[Auth] $operation stack', stackTrace: stack);
  }
}
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../experience/experience_page.dart';
import 'account_service.dart';
import 'phone_login_page.dart';
import 'name_page.dart';
import 'startup_profile_store.dart';
import '../onboarding/onboarding_flow.dart';
/// Restores local credentials before choosing the login or experience UI.
/// Server-side token validation remains the responsibility of protected APIs.
class AuthGate extends StatefulWidget {
  const AuthGate({
    super.key,
    required this.service,
    this.experienceBuilder,
    this.profileStore = const StartupProfileStore(),
  });
  final AccountService service;
  final StartupProfileStore profileStore;
  final Widget Function(bool newUser)? experienceBuilder;
  @override
  State<AuthGate> createState() => _AuthGateState();
}
class _AuthGateState extends State<AuthGate> {
  bool _loading = true, _authenticated = false, _newUser = false;
  String? _error;
  Map<String, dynamic> _profile = {};
  bool _returning = false;
  String? _uid;
  Map<String, dynamic> get _registrationChats =>
      Map<String, dynamic>.from(_profile['registrationChats'] as Map? ?? {});
  Future<void> _authenticatedSession(AccountSession session) async {
    final chats = _registrationChats;
    final pending =
        widget.service.testNewUser ||
        chats[session.uid] == 'pending' ||
        (session.isNewUser && chats[session.uid] != 'done');
    if (pending) {
      final value = {
        ..._profile,
        'registrationChats': {...chats, session.uid: 'pending'},
      };
      await widget.profileStore.write(value);
      _profile = value;
    }
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _uid = session.uid;
      _newUser = pending;
      _authenticated = true;
    });
  }
  Future<void> _completeRegistrationChat() async {
    final value = {
      ..._profile,
      'registrationChats': {..._registrationChats, _uid!: 'done'},
    };
    await widget.profileStore.write(value);
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _profile = value;
      _newUser = false;
    });
  }
  Future<bool> _advance(String? next) async {
    final value = {..._profile, 'page': next, 'introDone': next == null};
    await widget.profileStore.write(value);
    if (mounted) setState(() => _profile = value);
    return true;
  }
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _restore();
  }
  Future<void> _restore() async {
    setState(() {
      _loading = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final session = await widget.service.restoreSession();
      _profile = await widget.profileStore.read();
      if (session == null) {
        _returning = await widget.service.hasCompletedRegistration();
      }
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _authenticated = session != null;
        _uid = session?.uid;
        _newUser =
            session != null && _registrationChats[session.uid] == 'pending';
      });
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _error = '无法读取登录状态，请重试');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _loading = false);
    }
  }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (_loading || _error != null) {
      return Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF100621),
        body: Center(
          child: _error == null
              ? const CircularProgressIndicator()
              : Column(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    Text(_error!, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
                    TextButton(onPressed: _restore, child: const Text('重试')),
                  ],
                ),
        ),
      );
    }
    if (!_authenticated && !_returning && _profile['introDone'] != true) {
      return OnboardingFlow(
        initialPageId: _profile['page'] as String?,
        onAdvance: _advance,
        onComplete: () {},
      );
    }
    if (_authenticated &&
        _newUser &&
        (_profile['name'] as String? ?? '').isEmpty) {
      return NamePage(
        onContinue: (name) async {
          final value = {..._profile, 'name': name};
          await widget.profileStore.write(value);
          if (mounted) setState(() => _profile = value);
        },
      );
    }
    if (!_authenticated) {
      return PhoneLoginPage(
        service: widget.service,
        onAuthenticated: _authenticatedSession,
      );
    }
    return widget.experienceBuilder?.call(_newUser) ??
        ExperiencePage(
          userId: _uid!,
          accountService: widget.service,
          profileStore: widget.profileStore,
          registrationCompleted: true,
          returningUser: !_newUser,
          onRegistrationChatComplete: _completeRegistrationChat,
        );
  }
}
import 'package:android_id/android_id.dart';
import 'package:device_info_plus/device_info_plus.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
/// Android 使用 ANDROID_ID，iOS 使用 identifierForVendor；Web 不采集设备标识。
/// 系统标识可能因系统重置或应用重装等操作改变，不保证永久不变。
Future<String?> readDeviceUuid(String platform) async {
  if (platform == 'web') return null;
  if (kIsWeb) throw StateError('当前运行平台与账号平台配置不一致');
  final String? value;
  if (platform == 'android' &&
      defaultTargetPlatform == TargetPlatform.android) {
    value = await const AndroidId().getId();
  } else if (platform == 'ios' && defaultTargetPlatform == TargetPlatform.iOS) {
    value = (await DeviceInfoPlugin().iosInfo).identifierForVendor;
  } else {
    throw StateError('当前运行平台与账号平台配置不一致');
  }
  final uuid = value?.trim();
  if (uuid == null || uuid.isEmpty) {
    throw StateError('无法获取设备标识，请稍后重试');
  }
  return uuid;
}
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:google_sign_in/google_sign_in.dart';
import '../../core/config/app_env.dart';
/// 仅在一次登录期间持有，不写入本地存储或日志。
class GoogleIdentityProof {
  const GoogleIdentityProof({required this.email, required this.idToken});
  final String email;
  final String idToken;
}
class GoogleLoginException implements Exception {
  const GoogleLoginException(this.code, this.message, {this.cancelled = false});
  final String code;
  final String message;
  final bool cancelled;
  @override
  String toString() => 'GoogleLoginException($code): $message';
}
abstract interface class GoogleIdentityProvider {
  Future<GoogleIdentityProof> authenticate(String platform);
}
/// 原生 Google OAuth 身份凭证，不使用 Firebase Auth，也不申请额外 API scopes。
class NativeGoogleIdentityProvider implements GoogleIdentityProvider {
  static Future<void>? _initialization;
  static final _sdk = GoogleSignIn.instance;
  @override
  Future<GoogleIdentityProof> authenticate(String platform) async {
    validateConfiguration(platform);
    var initialized = false;
    try {
      await (_initialization ??= _sdk.initialize(
        clientId: platform == 'ios' ? AppEnv.googleIosClientId : null,
        serverClientId: AppEnv.googleServerClientId,
      ));
      initialized = true;
      final account = await _sdk.authenticate();
      final idToken = account.authentication.idToken;
      if (account.email.isEmpty || idToken == null || idToken.isEmpty) {
        throw const GoogleLoginException(
          'missing_credential',
          'Google 未返回完整的邮箱和 ID Token，请重新登录',
        );
      }
      return GoogleIdentityProof(email: account.email, idToken: idToken);
    } on GoogleSignInException catch (error) {
      throw googleSignInFailure(error.code);
    } finally {
      // 业务登录态由账号系统管理；清除 SDK 临时登录态，不撤销 Google 授权。
      if (initialized) {
        try {
          await _sdk.signOut();
        } catch (_) {
          // 清理失败不能掩盖原始登录结果；下次授权前仍由系统确认账号。
        }
      }
    }
  }
  static void validateConfiguration(String platform) {
    if (kIsWeb || !{'ios', 'android'}.contains(platform)) {
      throw const GoogleLoginException(
        'unsupported_platform',
        '此 Demo 的 Google 登录支持 iOS 和 Android，请在手机上测试',
      );
    }
    if ((platform == 'ios' && defaultTargetPlatform != TargetPlatform.iOS) ||
        (platform == 'android' &&
            defaultTargetPlatform != TargetPlatform.android)) {
      throw const GoogleLoginException(
        'platform_mismatch',
        '当前设备与 API_PLATFORM 配置不一致',
      );
    }
    final pattern = RegExp(r'^[a-zA-Z0-9-]+\.apps\.googleusercontent\.com$');
    if (!pattern.hasMatch(AppEnv.googleServerClientId)) {
      throw const GoogleLoginException(
        'missing_server_client_id',
        '请配置 GOOGLE_SERVER_CLIENT_ID（Google Web 类型的 OAuth Client ID）',
      );
    }
    if (platform == 'ios' && !pattern.hasMatch(AppEnv.googleIosClientId)) {
      throw const GoogleLoginException(
        'missing_ios_client_id',
        '请配置 GOOGLE_IOS_CLIENT_ID 并生成 iOS 回调配置',
      );
    }
  }
}
/// 仅使用 SDK 错误码，description/details 可能携带敏感凭证。
GoogleLoginException googleSignInFailure(GoogleSignInExceptionCode code) =>
    switch (code) {
      GoogleSignInExceptionCode.canceled => const GoogleLoginException(
        'canceled',
        'Google 登录已取消（若选完账号后自动取消，请检查 OAuth 配置）',
        cancelled: true,
      ),
      GoogleSignInExceptionCode.clientConfigurationError =>
        const GoogleLoginException(
          'clientConfigurationError',
          'Google 客户端配置错误，请检查 Client ID、iOS 回调和 Android 签名指纹',
        ),
      GoogleSignInExceptionCode.providerConfigurationError =>
        const GoogleLoginException(
          'providerConfigurationError',
          'Google 登录服务配置异常，请检查 OAuth 项目及登录服务是否可用',
        ),
      GoogleSignInExceptionCode.interrupted => const GoogleLoginException(
        'interrupted',
        'Google 登录被中断，请重试',
      ),
      GoogleSignInExceptionCode.uiUnavailable => const GoogleLoginException(
        'uiUnavailable',
        '无法打开 Google 登录窗口，请回到前台重试',
      ),
      _ => const GoogleLoginException(
        'google_sign_in_failed',
        'Google 授权失败，请检查网络和 OAuth 配置后重试',
      ),
    };
import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import '../../core/network/api_client.dart';
import 'account_service.dart';
import 'auth_debug_log.dart';
class LoginDemoPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const LoginDemoPage({super.key, required this.service});
  final AccountService service;
  @override
  State<LoginDemoPage> createState() => _LoginDemoPageState();
}
class _LoginDemoPageState extends State<LoginDemoPage> {
  final _country = TextEditingController(text: '86');
  final _phone = TextEditingController();
  final _code = TextEditingController();
  Timer? _timer;
  CancelToken? _request;
  bool _busy = true;
  String _message = '正在读取本地会话…';
  bool _failed = false;
  String get _fullPhone => '${_country.text.trim()}-${_phone.text.trim()}';
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _country.addListener(_refresh);
    _phone.addListener(_refresh);
    _timer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 1), (_) => _refresh());
    _restore();
  }
  void _refresh() {
    if (mounted) setState(() {});
  }
  Future<void> _run(
    Future<String> Function() action, {
    String operation = '退出会话',
  }) async {
    AuthDebugLog.event('$operation START');
    setState(() {
      _busy = true;
      _failed = false;
      _message = '处理中…';
    });
    try {
      final message = await action();
      AuthDebugLog.event('$operation OK');
      if (mounted) setState(() => _message = message);
    } catch (error, stack) {
      AuthDebugLog.error(operation, error, stack);
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _failed = !(error is GoogleLoginException && error.cancelled);
          _message = _errorMessage(error);
        });
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    }
  }
  String _errorMessage(Object error) {
    if (error is GoogleLoginException) return error.message;
    if (error is AccountException) return '${error.message}（${error.status}）';
    if (error is ArgumentError) return '${error.message}';
    if (error is StateError) return error.message;
    if (error is ApiDecodeException) return error.message;
    if (error is PlatformException) return '设备信息或安全存储读取失败，请在设备解锁后重试';
    if (error is DioException) {
      if (error.error is StateError) return (error.error as StateError).message;
      return error.userMessage;
    }
    return '操作失败，请稍后重试';
  }
  Future<void> _restore() => _run(() async {
    final session = await widget.service.restoreSession();
    return session == null ? '尚无本地会话，请输入手机号测试。' : '已恢复本地会话，尚未验证服务端有效性。';
  }, operation: '恢复会话');
  Future<void> _send() => _run(() async {
    _request = CancelToken();
    await widget.service.sendPhoneVerify(
      phone: _fullPhone,
      cancelToken: _request,
    );
    return '验证码已发送，请查看手机短信。';
  }, operation: '发送验证码');
  Future<void> _login() => _run(() async {
    FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
    _request = CancelToken();
    await widget.service.phoneVerifyLogin(
      phone: _fullPhone,
      code: _code.text,
      cancelToken: _request,
    );
    _code.clear();
    return '登录成功，用户信息和 token 已保存。';
  }, operation: '手机验证码登录');
  Future<void> _googleLogin() => _run(() async {
    FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
    _request = CancelToken();
    await widget.service.googleLogin(cancelToken: _request);
    _code.clear();
    return 'Google 登录成功，账号系统会话已保存。';
  }, operation: 'Google 登录');
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _timer?.cancel();
    _request?.cancel('测试页已关闭');
    _country.dispose();
    _phone.dispose();
    _code.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final service = widget.service;
    final session = service.session;
    final seconds = service.resendSeconds(_fullPhone);
    final signer = service.client.signer;
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('账号登录测试')),
      body: SafeArea(
        child: Center(
          child: ConstrainedBox(
            constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 520),
            child: ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              children: [
                const Text(
                  'diagnostics-v2 · ${kDebugMode
                      ? "Debug"
                      : kProfileMode
                      ? "Profile"
                      : "Release"}',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
                ),
                Text(
                  '账号接口 Demo',
                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(
                  '产品：${signer?.product ?? "未配置"} · 平台：${signer?.platform ?? "未配置"}',
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  service.client.dio.options.baseUrl,
                  style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodySmall,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 24),
                OutlinedButton(
                  onPressed: _busy ? null : _googleLogin,
                  child: const Text('使用 Google 账号登录'),
                ),
                const Padding(
                  padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
                  child: Center(child: Text('或使用手机验证码')),
                ),
                Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    SizedBox(
                      width: 90,
                      child: TextField(
                        controller: _country,
                        enabled: !_busy,
                        keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                        inputFormatters: [
                          FilteringTextInputFormatter.digitsOnly,
                        ],
                        decoration: const InputDecoration(
                          labelText: '国家区号',
                          prefixText: '+',
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 12),
                    Expanded(
                      child: TextField(
                        controller: _phone,
                        enabled: !_busy,
                        keyboardType: TextInputType.phone,
                        inputFormatters: [
                          FilteringTextInputFormatter.digitsOnly,
                        ],
                        decoration: const InputDecoration(
                          labelText: '手机号',
                          hintText: '输入测试手机号',
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                OutlinedButton(
                  onPressed: _busy || seconds > 0 ? null : _send,
                  child: Text(seconds > 0 ? '${seconds}s 后重新发送' : '发送验证码'),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                TextField(
                  controller: _code,
                  enabled: !_busy,
                  keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                  autofillHints: const [AutofillHints.oneTimeCode],
                  inputFormatters: [FilteringTextInputFormatter.digitsOnly],
                  decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: '短信验证码'),
                  onSubmitted: (_) {
                    if (!_busy) _login();
                  },
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
                FilledButton(
                  onPressed: _busy ? null : _login,
                  child: const Text('登录并保存会话'),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
                if (_busy) const LinearProgressIndicator(),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Semantics(
                  liveRegion: true,
                  child: Text(
                    _message,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: _failed
                          ? Theme.of(context).colorScheme.error
                          : null,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 24),
                if (session != null)
                  Card(
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            '当前会话',
                            style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium,
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 8),
                          SelectableText(
                            'UID：${session.uid}\n昵称：${session.nick}\n手机号：${session.phone.isEmpty ? '未绑定' : session.phone}\n邮箱：${session.email.isEmpty ? '未返回' : session.email}\n产品 / 平台：${session.product} / ${session.platform}\n首次注册：${session.isNewUser ? "是" : "否"}',
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 8),
                          const Text('Token 已保存（不显示完整凭证）'),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                OutlinedButton(
                  onPressed: _busy ? null : _restore,
                  child: const Text('读取本地会话'),
                ),
                TextButton(
                  onPressed: _busy
                      ? null
                      : () => _run(() async {
                          await service.logout();
                          _code.clear();
                          return '本地会话已清除。';
                        }),
                  child: const Text('退出并清除本地会话'),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                if (kDebugMode)
                  ValueListenableBuilder<List<String>>(
                    valueListenable: AuthDebugLog.entries,
                    builder: (context, entries, _) => Card(
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                        child: Column(
                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                          children: [
                            const Text('诊断日志（最近 80 条）'),
                            Wrap(
                              spacing: 8,
                              children: [
                                TextButton(
                                  onPressed: () =>
                                      AuthDebugLog.event('日志输出测试 OK'),
                                  child: const Text('测试日志输出'),
                                ),
                                TextButton(
                                  onPressed: () async {
                                    await Clipboard.setData(
                                      ClipboardData(text: entries.join('\n')),
                                    );
                                    if (!context.mounted) return;
                                    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                                      const SnackBar(content: Text('诊断日志已复制')),
                                    );
                                  },
                                  child: const Text('复制日志'),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                            SelectableText(
                              entries.isEmpty ? '暂无记录' : entries.join('\n'),
                              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                const Text(
                  '若控制台没有日志，可在本页复制诊断日志。',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
                ),
                const Text(
                  '会发送真实短信。同一手机号间隔至少 60 秒，每天最多 3 次。\nip 默认为空；移动端 uuid 自动从设备读取。',
                  style: TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
import 'package:json_annotation/json_annotation.dart';
import '../../../core/network/api_client.dart';
part 'account_models.g.dart';
enum AccountOperation { sendCode, login, googleLogin }
class AccountException implements Exception {
  const AccountException(this.status, this.operation);
  final int status;
  final AccountOperation operation;
  String get message =>
      signatureErrorMessage(status) ??
      switch (status) {
        1 => '请求参数错误，请检查输入',
        2 =>
          operation == AccountOperation.sendCode
              ? '验证码发送过于频繁，请稍后重试'
              : '登录尝试过于频繁，请一分钟后重试',
        3 when operation == AccountOperation.login => '验证码错误，请重新输入',
        4 when operation == AccountOperation.googleLogin =>
          'Google 登录凭证无效或已过期，请重新授权',
        16 when operation == AccountOperation.sendCode => '今日验证码发送次数已达上限',
        17 when operation == AccountOperation.sendCode => '该手机号暂时无法接收验证码',
        -1 => '服务暂时不可用，请稍后重试',
        _ => '请求失败（错误码：$status）',
      };
  @override
  String toString() => 'AccountException: $message';
}
/// token 不进入 toString；registered=1 才表示首次注册。
@JsonSerializable(checked: true)
class AccountSession {
  const AccountSession({
    required this.registered,
    required this.uid,
    required this.nick,
    required this.avatar,
    required this.phone,
    required this.product,
    required this.platform,
    required this.token,
    this.email = '',
  });
  factory AccountSession.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    final registered = json['registered'];
    if (registered is! int || (registered != 0 && registered != 1)) {
      throw ApiDecodeException('登录响应字段 registered 格式错误');
    }
    try {
      final session = _$AccountSessionFromJson(json);
      session._validate();
      return session;
    } on ApiDecodeException {
      rethrow;
    } catch (_) {
      throw ApiDecodeException('登录响应字段格式错误');
    }
  }
  final int registered;
  bool get isNewUser => registered == 1;
  final String uid;
  final String nick;
  @JsonKey(defaultValue: '')
  final String avatar;
  @JsonKey(defaultValue: '')
  final String phone;
  final String product;
  final String platform;
  final String token;
  @JsonKey(defaultValue: '')
  final String email;
  void _validate() {
    for (final field in {
      'uid': uid,
      'product': product,
      'platform': platform,
      'token': token,
    }.entries) {
      if (field.value.isEmpty) {
        throw ApiDecodeException('登录响应字段 ${field.key} 格式错误');
      }
    }
  }
  /// 含敏感凭证，仅用于持久化，禁止打印。
  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => _$AccountSessionToJson(this);
}
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'phone_login_artwork.dart';
class NamePage extends StatefulWidget {
  const NamePage({super.key, required this.onContinue, this.initialName = ''});
  final Future<void> Function(String name) onContinue;
  final String initialName;
  @override
  State<NamePage> createState() => _NamePageState();
}
class _NamePageState extends State<NamePage> {
  late final _name = TextEditingController(text: widget.initialName);
  bool _busy = false;
  String? _error;
  Future<void> _continue() async {
    if (_busy || _name.text.trim().isEmpty) return;
    setState(() {
      _busy = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      await widget.onContinue(_name.text.trim());
      if (mounted) FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _error = '保存失败，请重试');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    }
  }
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _name.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => AnnotatedRegion<SystemUiOverlayStyle>(
    value: SystemUiOverlayStyle.light,
    child: Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF100621),
      resizeToAvoidBottomInset: false,
      body: LayoutBuilder(
        builder: (context, bounds) {
          final media = MediaQuery.of(context);
          final width = math.min(
            bounds.maxWidth - media.padding.horizontal,
            420.0,
          );
          final available = bounds.maxHeight - media.viewInsets.bottom;
          final top = math.min(
            308.0,
            math.max(media.padding.top + 54, available - 260),
          );
          return Stack(
            children: [
              const Positioned.fill(child: PhoneLoginArtwork(codeStep: false)),
              Positioned(
                top: 0,
                bottom: media.viewInsets.bottom,
                left: (bounds.maxWidth - width) / 2,
                width: width,
                child: SingleChildScrollView(
                  padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                    16,
                    top,
                    16,
                    math.max(media.padding.bottom, 16),
                  ),
                  child: Column(
                    children: [
                      Semantics(
                        header: true,
                        label: '怎么称呼你比较好？',
                        child: Image.asset(
                          'assets/design/name_title.png',
                          width: 242.304,
                          height: 25.9,
                          excludeFromSemantics: true,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 14),
                      const Text(
                        '留下你的名字，以后我就知道怎么叫你啦。',
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 14,
                          height: 20 / 14,
                          letterSpacing: 1,
                          color: Color(0xB3FFFFFF),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 23),
                      SizedBox(
                        height: 60,
                        child: Stack(
                          children: [
                            Positioned.fill(
                              child: FittedBox(
                                fit: BoxFit.fill,
                                child: loginSvg(
                                  'name_field',
                                  width: 343,
                                  height: 60,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                            Center(
                              child: TextField(
                                key: const ValueKey('login-name'),
                                controller: _name,
                                enabled: !_busy,
                                maxLength: 40,
                                textInputAction: TextInputAction.next,
                                autofillHints: const [AutofillHints.nickname],
                                onChanged: (_) => setState(() => _error = null),
                                onSubmitted: (_) => _continue(),
                                style: const TextStyle(
                                  fontSize: 16,
                                  color: Colors.white,
                                  letterSpacing: 1,
                                ),
                                decoration: const InputDecoration(
                                  hintText: '请输入你的名字',
                                  hintStyle: TextStyle(
                                    color: Color(0xFF514762),
                                  ),
                                  counterText: '',
                                  border: InputBorder.none,
                                  enabledBorder: InputBorder.none,
                                  focusedBorder: InputBorder.none,
                                  contentPadding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                    horizontal: 26,
                                  ),
                                  isDense: true,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 28),
                      Opacity(
                        opacity: _name.text.trim().isEmpty ? .24 : 1,
                        child: DecoratedBox(
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(104),
                            gradient: const LinearGradient(
                              colors: [Color(0xFF2EA5EF), Color(0xFFA43ADD)],
                            ),
                          ),
                          child: TextButton(
                            key: const ValueKey('name-next'),
                            onPressed: _busy || _name.text.trim().isEmpty
                                ? null
                                : _continue,
                            style: TextButton.styleFrom(
                              overlayColor: Colors.transparent,
                              minimumSize: const Size(double.infinity, 58),
                            ),
                            child: _busy
                                ? const SizedBox(
                                    width: 20,
                                    height: 20,
                                    child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                                      strokeWidth: 2,
                                      color: Colors.white,
                                    ),
                                  )
                                : const Text(
                                    '继续',
                                    style: TextStyle(
                                      color: Colors.white,
                                      fontSize: 16,
                                      letterSpacing: 1,
                                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      if (_error != null)
                        Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 12),
                          child: Text(
                            _error!,
                            style: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xFFFFB2DA)),
                          ),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          );
        },
      ),
    ),
  );
}
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_svg/flutter_svg.dart';
Widget loginSvg(String name, {required double width, required double height}) =>
    SvgPicture.asset(
      'assets/login/$name.svg',
      width: width,
      height: height,
      excludeFromSemantics: true,
    );
/// Original Figma artwork placed in its 375px design viewport.
class PhoneLoginArtwork extends StatelessWidget {
  const PhoneLoginArtwork({super.key, required this.codeStep});
  final bool codeStep;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => IgnorePointer(
    child: ExcludeSemantics(
      child: LayoutBuilder(
        builder: (context, bounds) {
          final width = math.min(bounds.maxWidth, 600.0);
          final height = codeStep ? 227.0 : 309.0;
          return Align(
            alignment: Alignment.topCenter,
            child: SizedBox(
              width: width,
              height: height * width / 375,
              child: FittedBox(
                fit: BoxFit.fill,
                child: SizedBox(
                  width: 375,
                  height: height,
                  child: ClipRect(child: codeStep ? _characters() : _cards()),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    ),
  );
  Widget _characters() => Opacity(
    opacity: .72 * .46,
    child: ShaderMask(
      blendMode: BlendMode.dstIn,
      shaderCallback: (rect) => const LinearGradient(
        begin: Alignment.topCenter,
        end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
        colors: [
          Color(0x00FFFFFF),
          Color(0xC5FFFFFF),
          Color(0x4DFFFFFF),
          Color(0x00FFFFFF),
        ],
        stops: [0, .519868, .833481, 1],
      ).createShader(rect),
      child: Stack(
        children: [
          for (final item in [
            (x: -80.09, y: 31.94, flipped: true),
            (x: 174.56, y: 29.0, flipped: false),
          ])
            Positioned(
              left: item.x,
              top: item.y,
              width: 293.709,
              height: 361.336,
              child: Center(
                child: Transform.rotate(
                  angle: (item.flipped ? 22.04 : -22.04) * math.pi / 180,
                  child: Transform.flip(
                    flipX: item.flipped,
                    child: Image.asset(
                      'assets/login/otp_character.png',
                      width: 190.236,
                      height: 312.811,
                      fit: BoxFit.cover,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    ),
  );
  Widget _cards() => ShaderMask(
    blendMode: BlendMode.dstIn,
    shaderCallback: (rect) => const LinearGradient(
      begin: Alignment.topCenter,
      end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
      colors: [
        Color(0x00FFFFFF),
        Color(0x38FFFFFF),
        Color(0x66FFFFFF),
        Color(0x00FFFFFF),
      ],
      stops: [0, .188459, .512898, 1],
    ).createShader(Rect.fromLTWH(0, -1, rect.width, 309)),
    child: Stack(
      children: [
        for (final card in _loginCards)
          Positioned(
            left: card.x,
            top: card.y,
            width: card.w,
            height: card.h,
            child: Center(
              child: Transform.rotate(
                angle: -6.63 * math.pi / 180,
                child: Container(
                  width: card.textWidth + 76,
                  height: 71,
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                    horizontal: 13,
                    vertical: 16,
                  ),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: const Color(0xFF2A2A34),
                    border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFF46474E)),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                  ),
                  child: Row(
                    children: [
                      SizedBox(
                        width: 39,
                        height: 39,
                        child: OverflowBox(
                          minWidth: 41,
                          maxWidth: 41,
                          minHeight: 41,
                          maxHeight: 41,
                          child: card.avatar == 'other'
                              ? loginSvg('avatar_other', width: 41, height: 41)
                              : Image.asset(
                                  'assets/login/avatar_${card.avatar}.png',
                                  width: 41,
                                  height: 41,
                                ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 9),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Column(
                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                          children: [
                            Text(
                              card.name,
                              textScaler: TextScaler.noScaling,
                              maxLines: 1,
                              overflow: TextOverflow.clip,
                              style: const TextStyle(
                                inherit: false,
                                fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
                                fontSize: 14,
                                height: 16 / 14,
                                letterSpacing: 1,
                                color: Color(0xFF9B9CA1),
                              ),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 4),
                            Text(
                              card.topic,
                              textScaler: TextScaler.noScaling,
                              maxLines: 1,
                              overflow: TextOverflow.clip,
                              style: TextStyle(
                                inherit: false,
                                fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                                fontSize: 14,
                                height: 17 / 14,
                                letterSpacing: 1,
                                color: card.color,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
      ],
    ),
  );
}
