import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_svg/flutter_svg.dart';
import '../../core/network/api_client.dart';
import 'account_service.dart';
import 'phone_login_artwork.dart';
class PhoneLoginPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const PhoneLoginPage({
    super.key,
    required this.service,
    required this.onAuthenticated,
    this.preview = false,
    this.initialCodeStep = false,
    this.initialPhone = '',
    this.onOpenServiceAgreement,
    this.onOpenPrivacyPolicy,
  });
  final AccountService service;
  final FutureOr<void> Function(AccountSession) onAuthenticated;
  final bool preview, initialCodeStep;
  final String initialPhone;
  final VoidCallback? onOpenServiceAgreement, onOpenPrivacyPolicy;
  @override
  State<PhoneLoginPage> createState() => _PhoneLoginPageState();
}
class _PhoneLoginPageState extends State<PhoneLoginPage> {
  late final _phone = TextEditingController(text: widget.initialPhone);
  final _code = TextEditingController();
  final _codeFocus = FocusNode();
  late bool _codeStep = widget.initialCodeStep;
  bool _agreed = false, _busy = false;
  String? _error;
  CancelToken? _request;
  Timer? _timer;
  String get _fullPhone => '86-${_phone.text}';
  bool get _validPhone => RegExp(r'^1[0-9]{10}$').hasMatch(_phone.text);
  bool get _enabled =>
      !_busy && (_codeStep ? _code.text.length == 6 : _validPhone && _agreed);
  int get _seconds =>
      widget.preview ? 0 : widget.service.resendSeconds(_fullPhone);
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _timer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 1), (_) {
      if (mounted && _codeStep) setState(() {});
    });
    if (_codeStep && !widget.preview) {
      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
        if (mounted) _codeFocus.requestFocus();
      });
    }
  }
  String _message(Object error) {
    if (error is AccountException) return error.message;
    if (error is DioException) return error.userMessage;
    if (error is StateError) return error.message;
    if (error is ArgumentError) return '${error.message}';
    if (error is PlatformException) return '设备或安全存储暂不可用，请重试';
    return '暂时无法完成，请稍后重试';
  }
  Future<void> _sendCode({bool resend = false}) async {
    if (_busy || !_validPhone || (!resend && !_agreed)) return;
    if (resend && _seconds > 0) return;
    setState(() {
      _busy = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      if (!widget.preview && _seconds == 0) {
        _request = CancelToken();
        await widget.service.sendPhoneVerify(
          phone: _fullPhone,
          cancelToken: _request,
        );
      }
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {
        _codeStep = true;
        _code.clear();
      });
      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
        if (mounted) _codeFocus.requestFocus();
      });
    } catch (error) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _error = _message(error));
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    }
  }
  Future<void> _verify() async {
    if (!_enabled) return;
    if (widget.preview) {
      setState(() => _error = '当前为界面预览，不会提交登录请求');
      return;
    }
    setState(() {
      _busy = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      _request = CancelToken();
      final session = await widget.service.phoneVerifyLogin(
        phone: _fullPhone,
        code: _code.text,
        cancelToken: _request,
      );
      if (!mounted) return;
      TextInput.finishAutofillContext();
      FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
      await widget.onAuthenticated(session);
    } catch (error) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _error = _message(error));
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    }
  }
  void _backToPhone() {
    if (_busy) return;
    FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
    setState(() {
      _codeStep = false;
      _code.clear();
      _error = null;
    });
  }
  void _openPolicy(bool privacy) {
    final open = privacy
        ? widget.onOpenPrivacyPolicy
        : widget.onOpenServiceAgreement;
    if (open != null) {
      open();
      return;
    }
    showDialog<void>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text(privacy ? '隐私协议' : '服务协议'),
        content: const Text('协议内容将在正式上线前补充。'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: const Text('知道了'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _timer?.cancel();
    _request?.cancel('登录页面已关闭');
    _phone.dispose();
    _code.dispose();
    _codeFocus.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }
  static const _text = TextStyle(
    inherit: false,
    textBaseline: TextBaseline.alphabetic,
    fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
    fontSize: 16,
    color: Colors.white,
    height: 1.2,
  );
  Widget _phoneInput() => SizedBox(
    height: 60,
    child: Stack(
      children: [
        Positioned.fill(
          child: FittedBox(
            fit: BoxFit.fill,
            child: loginSvg('phone_field', width: 343, height: 60),
          ),
        ),
        Row(
          children: [
            const SizedBox(
              width: 62,
              child: Center(child: Text('+86', style: _text)),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 31),
            Expanded(
              child: TextField(
                key: const ValueKey('login-phone'),
                controller: _phone,
                enabled: !_busy,
                keyboardType: TextInputType.phone,
                textInputAction: TextInputAction.next,
                autofillHints: const [AutofillHints.telephoneNumberNational],
                inputFormatters: [
                  FilteringTextInputFormatter.digitsOnly,
                  LengthLimitingTextInputFormatter(11),
                ],
                onChanged: (_) => setState(() => _error = null),
                onSubmitted: (_) {
                  if (_enabled) _sendCode();
                },
                style: _text,
                cursorColor: const Color(0xFFF44BE6),
                decoration: InputDecoration(
                  hintText: '请输入手机号',
                  hintStyle: _text.copyWith(color: const Color(0xFF52535A)),
                  border: InputBorder.none,
                  enabledBorder: InputBorder.none,
                  focusedBorder: InputBorder.none,
                  disabledBorder: InputBorder.none,
                  contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 16),
                  isDense: true,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ],
    ),
  );
  Widget _codeInput() => SizedBox(
    height: 56,
    child: Stack(
      children: [
        Row(
          children: [
            for (var i = 0; i < 6; i++) ...[
              if (i > 0) const SizedBox(width: 7),
              Expanded(
                child: Container(
                  height: 56,
                  alignment: Alignment.center,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: const Color(0x1AFFFFFF),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  ),
                  child: ExcludeSemantics(
                    child: Text(
                      i < _code.text.length ? _code.text[i] : '',
                      style: _text.copyWith(fontSize: 24),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ],
        ),
        Positioned.fill(
          child: TextField(
            key: const ValueKey('login-code'),
            controller: _code,
            focusNode: _codeFocus,
            enabled: !_busy,
            keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
            textInputAction: TextInputAction.done,
            autofillHints: const [AutofillHints.oneTimeCode],
            inputFormatters: [
              FilteringTextInputFormatter.digitsOnly,
              LengthLimitingTextInputFormatter(6),
            ],
            onChanged: (_) => setState(() => _error = null),
            onSubmitted: (_) => _verify(),
            showCursor: false,
            enableInteractiveSelection: true,
            style: _text.copyWith(color: Colors.transparent),
            decoration: const InputDecoration(
              labelText: '六位短信验证码',
              floatingLabelBehavior: FloatingLabelBehavior.never,
              labelStyle: TextStyle(color: Colors.transparent),
              border: InputBorder.none,
              enabledBorder: InputBorder.none,
              focusedBorder: InputBorder.none,
              disabledBorder: InputBorder.none,
              contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    ),
  );
  Widget _nextButton() => Semantics(
    button: true,
    enabled: _enabled,
    label: '下一步',
    child: ExcludeSemantics(
      child: Opacity(
        opacity: _codeStep || _enabled || _busy ? 1 : .24,
        child: DecoratedBox(
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(104),
            gradient: const LinearGradient(
              colors: [Color(0xFF2EA5EF), Color(0xFFA43ADD)],
            ),
          ),
          child: TextButton(
            key: const ValueKey('login-next'),
            onPressed: _enabled ? (_codeStep ? _verify : _sendCode) : null,
            style: TextButton.styleFrom(
              overlayColor: Colors.transparent,
              minimumSize: const Size(double.infinity, 58),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 32, vertical: 14),
              foregroundColor: Colors.white,
            ),
            child: _busy
                ? const SizedBox(
                    width: 20,
                    height: 20,
                    child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                      strokeWidth: 2,
                      color: Colors.white,
                    ),
                  )
                : Text(
                    '下一步',
                    style: _text.copyWith(
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                      letterSpacing: 1,
                    ),
                  ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
  Widget _agreements() => Row(
    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
    children: [
      Semantics(
        checked: _agreed,
        label: '同意服务协议和隐私协议',
        child: InkWell(
          key: const ValueKey('login-agree'),
          splashFactory: NoSplash.splashFactory,
          overlayColor: const WidgetStatePropertyAll(Colors.transparent),
          onTap: _busy ? null : () => setState(() => _agreed = !_agreed),
          child: SizedBox(
            width: 28,
            height: 44,
            child: Center(
              child: _agreed
                  ? const Icon(
                      Icons.check_circle,
                      size: 16,
                      color: Color(0xFFF44BE6),
                    )
                  : loginSvg('agreement_unchecked', width: 16, height: 16),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
      Flexible(
        child: Wrap(
          alignment: WrapAlignment.center,
          crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
          children: [
            Text(
              '我已阅读并同意',
              style: _text.copyWith(
                fontSize: 12,
                color: const Color(0x8FFFFFFF),
              ),
            ),
            _policyLink('《服务协议》', false),
            Text(
              '和',
              style: _text.copyWith(
                fontSize: 12,
                color: const Color(0x8FFFFFFF),
              ),
            ),
            _policyLink('《隐私协议》', true),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    ],
  );
  Widget _policyLink(String text, bool privacy) => InkWell(
    onTap: () => _openPolicy(privacy),
    child: Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 10),
      child: Text(
        text,
        style: _text.copyWith(fontSize: 12, color: const Color(0x8FF44BE6)),
      ),
    ),
  );
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => AnnotatedRegion<SystemUiOverlayStyle>(
    value: SystemUiOverlayStyle.light,
    child: PopScope(
      canPop: !_codeStep && !_busy,
      onPopInvokedWithResult: (didPop, result) {
        if (!didPop && _codeStep) _backToPhone();
      },
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF100621),
        resizeToAvoidBottomInset: false,
        body: LayoutBuilder(
          builder: (context, bounds) {
            final media = MediaQuery.of(context);
            final available = bounds.maxHeight - media.viewInsets.bottom;
            final width = math.min(
              bounds.maxWidth - media.padding.horizontal,
              420.0,
            );
            final top = math.min(
              _codeStep ? 213.0 : 308.0,
              math.max(
                media.padding.top + 54,
                available - (_codeStep ? 270 : 290) - 20,
              ),
            );
            return Stack(
              children: [
                Positioned.fill(child: PhoneLoginArtwork(codeStep: _codeStep)),
                Positioned(
                  top: 0,
                  bottom: media.viewInsets.bottom,
                  left: (bounds.maxWidth - width) / 2,
                  width: width,
                  child: SingleChildScrollView(
                    key: const ValueKey('login-content'),
                    padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
                      16,
                      top,
                      16,
                      math.max(media.padding.bottom, 16),
                    ),
                    child: AutofillGroup(
                      child: Column(
                        children: [
                          Semantics(
                            header: true,
                            label: _codeStep ? '输入验证码' : '手机号登录',
                            child: loginSvg(
                              _codeStep ? 'otp_title' : 'phone_title',
                              width: 233,
                              height: 26,
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: 12),
                          Text(
                            _codeStep
                                ? '验证码发送至+86 ${_phone.text}'
                                : '登录开始你的路程,Pals在等你',
                            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                            style: _text.copyWith(
                              color: const Color(0xB3FFFFFF),
                              letterSpacing: 1,
                            ),
                          ),
                          SizedBox(height: _codeStep ? 22 : 26),
                          _codeStep ? _codeInput() : _phoneInput(),
                          SizedBox(height: _codeStep ? 34 : 28),
                          _nextButton(),
                          if (_codeStep)
                            Row(
                              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                              children: [
                                TextButton(
                                  onPressed: _busy ? null : _backToPhone,
                                  child: Text(
                                    '修改手机号',
                                    style: _text.copyWith(
                                      fontSize: 12,
                                      color: const Color(0x99FFFFFF),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ),
                                TextButton(
                                  onPressed: _busy || _seconds > 0
                                      ? null
                                      : () => _sendCode(resend: true),
                                  child: Text(
                                    _seconds > 0 ? '${_seconds}s 后重发' : '重新发送',
                                    style: _text.copyWith(
                                      fontSize: 12,
                                      color: const Color(0xFFF44BE6),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            )
                          else ...[
                            const SizedBox(height: 3),
                            _agreements(),
                          ],
                          if (_error != null)
                            Padding(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8),
                              child: Semantics(
                                liveRegion: true,
                                child: Text(
                                  _error!,
                                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                                  style: _text.copyWith(
                                    fontSize: 13,
                                    color: const Color(0xFFFFB2DA),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                if (_codeStep)
                  Positioned(
                    left: media.padding.left + 4,
                    top: media.padding.top + 8,
                    child: SizedBox(
                      width: 48,
                      height: 48,
                      child: IconButton(
                        key: const ValueKey('login-back'),
                        tooltip: '返回手机号输入',
                        onPressed: _busy ? null : _backToPhone,
                        padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                        icon: SvgPicture.asset(
                          'assets/chat/back.svg',
                          width: 24,
                          height: 24,
                          excludeFromSemantics: true,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
              ],
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
}
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';
import '../../core/network/api_client.dart';
import 'models/account_models.dart';
abstract interface class SessionStore {
  Future<AccountSession?> read();
  Future<void> write(AccountSession session);
  Future<void> clear();
}
/// 已完成注册的历史记录，与登录凭证的生命周期独立。
abstract interface class RegistrationHistoryStore {
  Future<bool> hasCompletedRegistration();
}
/// 移动端安全存储；Web 登录凭证使用 sessionStorage，注册记录持久保存。
class SecureSessionStore implements SessionStore, RegistrationHistoryStore {
  SecureSessionStore({required String namespace, FlutterSecureStorage? storage})
    : _key = 'bots.account.session.$namespace',
      _storage =
          storage ??
          const FlutterSecureStorage(
            webOptions: WebOptions(useSessionStorage: true),
            mOptions: MacOsOptions(usesDataProtectionKeychain: false),
          );
  final String _key;
  final FlutterSecureStorage _storage;
  static const _historyWebOptions = WebOptions(useSessionStorage: false);
  @override
  Future<AccountSession?> read() async {
    final raw = await _storage.read(key: _key);
    if (raw == null) return null;
    try {
      final data = jsonDecode(raw);
      if (data is! Map<String, dynamic>) throw const FormatException();
      return AccountSession.fromJson(data);
    } on FormatException {
      await clear();
      return null;
    } on ApiDecodeException {
      await clear();
      return null;
    }
  }
  String get _registrationKey => '$_key.registration-completed';
  @override
  Future<bool> hasCompletedRegistration() async {
    if (await _storage.read(
          key: _registrationKey,
          webOptions: _historyWebOptions,
        ) ==
        'true') {
      return true;
    }
    // Migrate users who signed in before the separate completion flag existed.
    // This is registration history, not proof that a token is still valid.
    if (await read() == null) return false;
    await _storage.write(
      key: _registrationKey,
      value: 'true',
      webOptions: _historyWebOptions,
    );
    return true;
  }
  @override
  Future<void> write(AccountSession session) async {
    await _storage.write(key: _key, value: jsonEncode(session.toJson()));
    await _storage.write(
      key: _registrationKey,
      value: 'true',
      webOptions: _historyWebOptions,
    );
  }
  String get _testNewUserKey => '$_key.test-new-user';
  Future<void> resetForNewUserTest() async {
    await _storage.write(key: _testNewUserKey, value: 'true');
    await clear();
    await _storage.delete(
      key: _registrationKey,
      webOptions: _historyWebOptions,
    );
  }
  Future<bool> consumeNewUserTest() async {
    final enabled = await _storage.read(key: _testNewUserKey) == 'true';
    if (enabled) await _storage.delete(key: _testNewUserKey);
    return enabled;
  }
  @override
  Future<void> clear() => _storage.delete(key: _key);
}
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';
/// Local introduction progress and preferred name; never authentication proof.
class StartupProfileStore {
  static const _key = 'bots.startup-profile.v2';
  final FlutterSecureStorage storage;
  const StartupProfileStore({this.storage = const FlutterSecureStorage()});
  Future<Map<String, dynamic>> read() async {
    final raw = await storage.read(key: _key);
    return raw == null ? {} : Map<String, dynamic>.from(jsonDecode(raw) as Map);
  }
  Future<void> write(Map<String, dynamic> value) =>
      storage.write(key: _key, value: jsonEncode(value));
  Future<bool> hasSelectedInterests(String userId) async {
    final profile = await read();
    final selected = (profile['interestsByUser'] as Map?)?[userId];
    return selected is List && selected.isNotEmpty;
  }
  Future<void> saveSelectedInterests(String userId, List<String> values) async {
    if (values.isEmpty) throw ArgumentError('请选择至少一个兴趣');
    final profile = await read();
    await write({
      ...profile,
      'interestsByUser': {
        ...?profile['interestsByUser'] as Map?,
        userId: values.toSet().toList(),
      },
    });
  }
  Future<void> clear() => storage.delete(key: _key);
}
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
/// A replaceable static asset animated without an additional runtime.
class IdleCharacter extends StatefulWidget {
  const IdleCharacter({super.key, this.playing = true, this.amplitude = 1});
  final bool playing;
  final double amplitude;
  @override
  State<IdleCharacter> createState() => _IdleCharacterState();
}
class _IdleCharacterState extends State<IdleCharacter>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late final AnimationController _controller = AnimationController(
    vsync: this,
    duration: const Duration(seconds: 4),
  );
  @override
  void didChangeDependencies() {
    super.didChangeDependencies();
    _syncPlayback();
  }
  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant IdleCharacter oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    _syncPlayback();
  }
  void _syncPlayback() {
    if (widget.playing && !MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)) {
      if (!_controller.isAnimating) _controller.repeat();
    } else {
      _controller.stop();
    }
  }
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _controller.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => RepaintBoundary(
    child: AnimatedBuilder(
      animation: _controller,
      child: Image.asset(
        'assets/characters/viitor_idle.png',
        fit: BoxFit.contain,
        semanticLabel: '紫色长发的 ViiTor，抬手打招呼，手持小人偶',
      ),
      builder: (context, child) {
        final phase = _controller.value * math.pi * 2;
        final amount = MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)
            ? 0.0
            : widget.amplitude;
        final breath = (1 - math.cos(phase)) / 2;
        return Transform.translate(
          offset: Offset(0, -3 * breath * amount),
          child: Transform.rotate(
            angle: math.sin(phase) * 0.008 * amount,
            alignment: Alignment.bottomCenter,
            child: Transform.scale(
              scaleX: 1 + 0.004 * breath * amount,
              scaleY: 1 + 0.012 * breath * amount,
              alignment: Alignment.bottomCenter,
              child: child,
            ),
          ),
        );
      },
    ),
  );
}
class CharacterPreviewPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const CharacterPreviewPage({super.key});
  @override
  State<CharacterPreviewPage> createState() => _CharacterPreviewPageState();
}
class _CharacterPreviewPageState extends State<CharacterPreviewPage> {
  bool playing = true;
  double amplitude = 1;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Scaffold(
    backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF171136),
    appBar: AppBar(
      title: const Text('角色待机预览'),
      foregroundColor: Colors.white,
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF171136),
    ),
    body: DecoratedBox(
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        gradient: RadialGradient(
          center: Alignment(0, -0.15),
          radius: 0.9,
          colors: [Color(0xFF6551A8), Color(0xFF171136)],
        ),
      ),
      child: SafeArea(
        child: Center(
          child: ConstrainedBox(
            constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 520),
            child: Column(
              children: [
                const Padding(
                  padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 12, 20, 0),
                  child: Text(
                    'ViiTor · 陪在你身边',
                    style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70, fontSize: 16),
                  ),
                ),
                Expanded(
                  child: Stack(
                    alignment: Alignment.center,
                    children: [
                      Positioned(
                        bottom: 12,
                        child: Container(
                          width: 180,
                          height: 24,
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(100),
                            boxShadow: const [
                              BoxShadow(
                                color: Color(0x665F3EB5),
                                blurRadius: 25,
                                spreadRadius: 8,
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      Positioned.fill(
                        child: Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 20, 24, 16),
                          child: IdleCharacter(
                            playing: playing,
                            amplitude: amplitude,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                Container(
                  margin: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 8, 20, 16),
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: const Color(0xFF30244D),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
                    border: Border.all(color: Colors.white12),
                  ),
                  child: Column(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    children: [
                      Row(
                        children: [
                          Expanded(
                            child: Text(
                              playing ? '轻轻呼吸，慢慢摇摆' : '已暂停',
                              style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white),
                            ),
                          ),
                          IconButton.filledTonal(
                            tooltip: playing ? '暂停动画' : '播放动画',
                            onPressed: () => setState(() => playing = !playing),
                            icon: Icon(
                              playing ? Icons.pause : Icons.play_arrow,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      Row(
                        children: [
                          const Text(
                            '动作幅度',
                            style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white70),
                          ),
                          Expanded(
                            child: Slider(
                              value: amplitude,
                              min: 0,
                              max: 2,
                              divisions: 20,
                              label: '${amplitude.toStringAsFixed(1)}×',
                              onChanged: (value) =>
                                  setState(() => amplitude = value),
                            ),
                          ),
                          Text(
                            '${amplitude.toStringAsFixed(1)}×',
                            style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white70),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      const Text(
                        '静态角色图演示 · 后续可替换正式动画素材',
                        style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white54, fontSize: 12),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
}
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import '../auth/auth_gate.dart';
import '../auth/account_service.dart';
/// The Figma launch composition, followed by the existing application flow.
class SplashPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const SplashPage({super.key});
  @override
  State<SplashPage> createState() => _SplashPageState();
}
class _SplashPageState extends State<SplashPage> {
  Timer? _timer;
  bool _loading = false;
  bool _ready = false;
  String? _error;
  @override
  void didChangeDependencies() {
    super.didChangeDependencies();
    if (!_loading) {
      _loading = true;
      _prepare();
    }
  }
  Future<void> _prepare() async {
    try {
      await Future.wait([
        for (final layer in SplashArtwork.layers)
          precacheImage(AssetImage('assets/design/${layer.name}.png'), context),
      ]);
    } catch (_) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() => _error = '启动资源加载失败，请重试');
      return;
    }
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() => _ready = true);
    _timer = Timer(const Duration(seconds: 2), () {
      if (!mounted) return;
      Navigator.of(context).pushReplacement(
        PageRouteBuilder<void>(
          pageBuilder: (_, animation, secondaryAnimation) =>
              AuthGate(service: account),
          transitionDuration: MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)
              ? Duration.zero
              : const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
          transitionsBuilder: (_, animation, secondaryAnimation, child) =>
              FadeTransition(opacity: animation, child: child),
        ),
      );
    });
  }
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _timer?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => _error != null
      ? Scaffold(
          backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF431A90),
          body: Center(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                Text(_error!, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.white)),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                FilledButton(
                  onPressed: () {
                    setState(() => _error = null);
                    _prepare();
                  },
                  child: const Text('重试'),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        )
      : _ready
      ? const SplashArtwork()
      : const AnnotatedRegion<SystemUiOverlayStyle>(
          value: SystemUiOverlayStyle.light,
          child: Scaffold(backgroundColor: Color(0xFF431A90)),
        );
}
/// Individually exported decorative layers preserve the original lettering,
/// masks and glows. Coordinates include Figma's parent clipping/effect bounds.
/// System status and gesture bars are supplied by the device, not painted here.
class SplashArtwork extends StatefulWidget {
  const SplashArtwork({super.key, this.animate = true});
  final bool animate;
  static const layers = <({String name, Rect rect})>[
    (name: 'splash_background', rect: Rect.fromLTWH(0, 1, 375, 811)),
    (name: 'splash_character', rect: Rect.fromLTWH(54, 253, 262, 447)),
    (name: 'splash_ribbon', rect: Rect.fromLTWH(0, 544, 375, 268)),
    (name: 'splash_shadow', rect: Rect.fromLTWH(81.1, 634.1, 235.8, 104.8)),
    (name: 'splash_brand', rect: Rect.fromLTWH(58.06, 134, 254.505, 80.089)),
  ];
  @override
  State<SplashArtwork> createState() => _SplashArtworkState();
}
class _SplashArtworkState extends State<SplashArtwork>
    with TickerProviderStateMixin {
  late final _entrance = AnimationController(
    vsync: this,
    duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 1200),
  );
  late final _idle = AnimationController(
    vsync: this,
    duration: const Duration(seconds: 4),
  );
  late final _ribbon = AnimationController(
    vsync: this,
    duration: const Duration(seconds: 12),
  );
  late final _backRibbon = AnimationController(
    vsync: this,
    duration: const Duration(seconds: 24),
  );
  void _syncAnimations() {
    if (!widget.animate || MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)) {
      _entrance.value = 1;
      _backRibbon.stop();
      _backRibbon.value = 0;
      _ribbon.stop();
      _ribbon.value = 0;
      _idle.stop();
      _idle.value = 0;
    } else {
      _entrance.forward();
      if (!_idle.isAnimating) _idle.repeat();
      if (!_ribbon.isAnimating) _ribbon.repeat();
      if (!_backRibbon.isAnimating) _backRibbon.repeat();
    }
  }
  @override
  void didChangeDependencies() {
    super.didChangeDependencies();
    _syncAnimations();
  }
  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant SplashArtwork oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    if (oldWidget.animate != widget.animate) {
      if (widget.animate) _entrance.value = 0;
      _syncAnimations();
    }
  }
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _entrance.dispose();
    _idle.dispose();
    _ribbon.dispose();
    _backRibbon.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }
  Widget _animateLayer(String name, Widget child) {
    if (!widget.animate || MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)) {
      return child;
    }
    return AnimatedBuilder(
      animation: Listenable.merge([_entrance, _idle]),
      child: child,
      builder: (context, child) {
        final enter = Curves.easeOutCubic.transform(_entrance.value);
        final remaining = 1 - enter;
        final float = 1 - math.cos(_idle.value * math.pi * 2);
        final offset = switch (name) {
          'splash_character' => Offset(0, remaining * 45 - float * 2),
          'splash_brand' => Offset(0, remaining * 14),
          'ribbon_back' => Offset(-375 * remaining, 0),
          'splash_ribbon' => Offset(375 * remaining, 0),
          _ => Offset.zero,
        };
        return Opacity(
          opacity: name == 'splash_brand' || name == 'splash_character'
              ? enter
              : 1,
          child: Transform.translate(offset: offset, child: child),
        );
      },
    );
  }
  Widget _ribbonArtwork(Widget still) {
    if (!widget.animate || MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)) {
      return still;
    }
    // One complete phrase and emblem. Adjacent copies overlap in the empty
    // margin so the cropped export can travel along its original 30° slope.
    const stride = 314.0;
    const rise = stride * 0.5773502692;
    return AnimatedBuilder(
      animation: _ribbon,
      builder: (context, _) => Stack(
        clipBehavior: Clip.none,
        children: [
          for (var index = -2; index <= 2; index++)
            Positioned(
              left: (index + _ribbon.value) * stride,
              top: -(index + _ribbon.value) * rise,
              width: 330,
              height: 268,
              child: ClipRect(
                child: ShaderMask(
                  blendMode: BlendMode.dstIn,
                  shaderCallback: (bounds) => const LinearGradient(
                    colors: [Colors.transparent, Colors.white, Colors.white],
                    stops: [0, 16 / 330, 1],
                  ).createShader(bounds),
                  child: OverflowBox(
                    alignment: Alignment.topLeft,
                    minWidth: 375,
                    maxWidth: 375,
                    minHeight: 268,
                    maxHeight: 268,
                    child: still,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => AnnotatedRegion<SystemUiOverlayStyle>(
    value: SystemUiOverlayStyle.light.copyWith(
      statusBarColor: Colors.transparent,
      systemNavigationBarColor: const Color(0xFF431A90),
    ),
    child: Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF431A90),
      body: Semantics(
        label: 'Pals。你的专属 AI 伙伴，也是另一个自己。Welcome to Pals!',
        image: true,
        child: ExcludeSemantics(
          child: SizedBox.expand(
            child: FittedBox(
              // Fill every screen ratio without stretching the illustration.
              fit: BoxFit.cover,
              clipBehavior: Clip.hardEdge,
              child: SizedBox(
                width: 375,
                height: 812,
                child: Stack(
                  clipBehavior: Clip.hardEdge,
                  children: [
                    for (final layer in SplashArtwork.layers)
                      Positioned.fromRect(
                        rect: layer.rect,
                        child: _animateLayer(
                          layer.name,
                          _layerImage(layer.name),
                        ),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
  Widget _layerImage(String name) {
    final image = RepaintBoundary(
      child: Image.asset(
        'assets/design/$name.png',
        fit: name == 'splash_character' ? BoxFit.cover : BoxFit.fill,
        filterQuality: FilterQuality.medium,
      ),
    );
    if (name == 'splash_background' &&
        widget.animate &&
        !MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)) {
      return Stack(
        fit: StackFit.expand,
        children: [
          image,
          RepaintBoundary(
            child: CustomPaint(painter: _BackRibbonPainter(_backRibbon)),
          ),
        ],
      );
    }
    return name == 'splash_ribbon' ? _ribbonArtwork(image) : image;
  }
}
/// Covers the baked-in lettering at its original angle; the character is
/// painted above this background layer, so the moving text passes behind her.
class _BackRibbonPainter extends CustomPainter {
  _BackRibbonPainter(this.progress) : super(repaint: progress);
  final Animation<double> progress;
  final TextPainter _label = TextPainter(
    text: const TextSpan(
      text: 'Pals-Your AI friend and digital twin.',
      style: TextStyle(
        color: Colors.white,
        fontSize: 20,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
        fontFamily: 'Arial',
      ),
    ),
    textDirection: TextDirection.ltr,
  )..layout();
  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    canvas.save();
    canvas.scale(size.width / 375, size.height / 811);
    // Coordinates are relative to the background export (one pixel below
    // the artboard origin), including its original clipped ribbon edges.
    final band = Path()
      ..moveTo(0, 300)
      ..lineTo(375, 361)
      ..lineTo(375, 409)
      ..lineTo(0, 348)
      ..close();
    canvas.clipPath(band);
    canvas.drawPaint(
      Paint()
        ..shader = const LinearGradient(
          colors: [Color(0xFFBA44FC), Color(0xFF8C67FF)],
        ).createShader(const Rect.fromLTWH(0, 300, 375, 109)),
    );
    canvas.translate(0, 300);
    canvas.rotate(math.atan2(61, 375));
    final stride = _label.width + 36;
    final shift = progress.value * stride;
    for (var index = -1; index <= 2; index++) {
      _label.paint(
        canvas,
        Offset(16 + index * stride - shift, (47.4 - _label.height) / 2),
      );
    }
    canvas.restore();
  }
  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant _BackRibbonPainter oldDelegate) =>
      oldDelegate.progress != progress;
}

    vsync: this,

class _Ribbon extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Ribbon({
    required this.text,
    required this.fontSize,
    required this.progress,
  });
  final String text;
  final double fontSize;
  final double progress;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Semantics(
    label: text,
    child: DecoratedBox(
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        gradient: LinearGradient(
          colors: [Color(0xFFB743F4), Color(0xFF8B68FF)],
        ),
      ),
      child: CustomPaint(painter: _RibbonTextPainter(text, fontSize, progress)),
    ),
  );
}
class _RibbonTextPainter extends CustomPainter {
  _RibbonTextPainter(this.text, this.fontSize, this.progress);
  final String text;
  final double fontSize;
  final double progress;
  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final label = TextPainter(
      text: TextSpan(
        text: '$text     ',
        style: TextStyle(
          color: Colors.white,
          fontSize: fontSize,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w900,
        ),
      ),
      textDirection: TextDirection.ltr,
    )..layout();
    final width = label.width;
    canvas.save();
    canvas.clipRect(Offset.zero & size);
    // Repeat exact measured text widths so wrapping at the cycle is seamless.
    final offset = (progress * width) % width;
    for (double x = -offset; x < size.width; x += width) {
      label.paint(canvas, Offset(x, (size.height - label.height) / 2));
    }
    canvas.restore();
    label.dispose();
  }
  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant _RibbonTextPainter oldDelegate) =>
      progress != oldDelegate.progress ||
      text != oldDelegate.text ||
      fontSize != oldDelegate.fontSize;
}
class _DotsPainter extends CustomPainter {
  const _DotsPainter();
  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final paint = Paint();
    for (double y = 0; y < size.height; y += 8) {
      for (double x = 0; x < size.width; x += 8) {
        final top = math.max(0.0, 1 - Offset(x - 360, y - 25).distance / 170);
        final bottom = math.max(
          0.0,
          1 - Offset(x - 30, y - 810).distance / 210,
        );
        final mid = math.max(0.0, 1 - Offset(x - 10, y - 510).distance / 95);
        final intensity = math.max(top, math.max(bottom, mid));
        if (intensity > 0) {
          paint.color = Colors.white.withValues(alpha: intensity * 0.13);
          canvas.drawCircle(Offset(x, y), 0.7 + intensity * 2.4, paint);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant _DotsPainter oldDelegate) => false;
}

import 'dart:ui';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_svg/flutter_svg.dart';

  String name, {
  double? width,
  double? height,
  BoxFit fit = BoxFit.contain,
}) => SvgPicture.asset(
  'assets/chat/$name.svg',
  width: width,
  height: height,
  fit: fit,
  excludeFromSemantics: true,
);
class ChatBackground extends StatelessWidget {
  const ChatBackground({super.key});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => IgnorePointer(
    child: Stack(
      fit: StackFit.expand,
      children: [
        const ColoredBox(color: Colors.black),
        Opacity(
          opacity: 0.77,
          child: Image.asset(
            'assets/chat/background.png',
            fit: BoxFit.cover,
            excludeFromSemantics: true,
          ),
        ),
        const Positioned(
          top: 0,
          left: 0,
          right: 0,
          height: 139,
          child: DecoratedBox(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              gradient: LinearGradient(
                begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                colors: [Color(0xA819171C), Color(0x0019171C)],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const Positioned(
          bottom: 0,
          left: 0,
          right: 0,
          height: 294,
          child: DecoratedBox(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              gradient: LinearGradient(
                begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                colors: [
                  Colors.transparent,
                  Color(0x5E000000),
                  Color(0xE0000000),
                ],
                stops: [0, 0.48171, 1],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    ),
  );
}

class ChatBubble extends StatelessWidget {
  const ChatBubble({super.key, required this.text, required this.isUser});
  final String text;
  final bool isUser;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => LayoutBuilder(
    builder: (context, constraints) {
      final maxWidth = math.min(269.0, constraints.maxWidth);
      final style = TextStyle(
        inherit: false,
        textBaseline: TextBaseline.alphabetic,
        fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
        fontSize: 14,
        height: 18 / 14,
        color: isUser ? const Color(0xFF333333) : Colors.white,
      );
      final measurement = TextPainter(
        text: TextSpan(text: text, style: style),
        textDirection: Directionality.of(context),
        textScaler: MediaQuery.textScalerOf(context),
      )..layout(maxWidth: math.max(1, maxWidth - 24));
      final width = isUser
          ? (measurement.width.ceilToDouble() + 24).clamp(
              math.min(94.0, maxWidth),
              maxWidth,
            )
          : maxWidth;
      measurement.dispose();
      return Align(
        alignment: isUser ? Alignment.centerRight : Alignment.centerLeft,
        child: SizedBox(
          width: width.toDouble(),
          child: Stack(
            children: [
              Positioned.fill(
                child: LayoutBuilder(
                  builder: (context, bounds) {
                    final asset = isUser
                        ? (bounds.maxHeight > 40 ? 'outgoing_long' : 'outgoing')
                        : (bounds.maxHeight > 80
                              ? 'incoming_long'
                              : 'incoming');
                    return chatSvg(asset, fit: BoxFit.fill);
                  },
                ),
              ),
              ConstrainedBox(
                constraints: BoxConstraints(minHeight: isUser ? 38 : 78),
                child: Padding(
                  padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                    horizontal: 12,
                    vertical: isUser ? 10 : 12,
                  ),
                  child: Text(text, style: style),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      );
    },
  );
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:math';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import '../../core/network/api_client.dart';
import 'chat_message.dart';
import 'chat_service.dart';
class ChatController extends ChangeNotifier {
  ChatController(
    this.service, {
    required this.displayName,
    this.onUnauthorized,
  });
  final ChatService service;
  final String displayName;
  final Future<void> Function()? onUnauthorized;
  final _turns = <int, Map<String, dynamic>>{};
  String? _sessionId, error;
  String? _pendingId, _pendingText;
  bool loading = true, sending = false, _disposed = false, _active = false;
  bool _authFailed = false;
  int _generation = 0, _attempt = 0;
  CancelToken? _connection, _sendCancel;
  Timer? _reconnect;
  List<ChatMessage> get messages {
    final turns = _turns.values.toList()
      ..sort((a, b) => (a['id'] as int).compareTo(b['id'] as int));
    return [
      for (final turn in turns)
        if (turn['messageType'] != 'explore_task') ...[
          if ((turn['userText'] as String? ?? '').isNotEmpty &&
              !{'welcome', 'discovery'}.contains(turn['messageType']))
            ChatMessage(turn['userText'] as String, isUser: true),
          if ((turn['assistantText'] as String? ?? '').isNotEmpty)
            ChatMessage(turn['assistantText'] as String),
          if (turn['status'] == 'queued' || turn['status'] == 'processing')
            ChatMessage(
              turn['status'] == 'queued' ? 'Tony 正在等待回复…' : 'Tony 正在回复…',
            ),
          if (turn['status'] == 'failed')
            const ChatMessage('这次回复未完成，请重新发送消息再试。'),
        ],
    ];
  }
  void _notify() {
    if (!_disposed) notifyListeners();
  }
  void merge(Map<String, dynamic> snapshot) {
    final turns = snapshot['turns'];
    if (turns is! List) throw const ChatException('聊天快照格式错误');
    for (final raw in turns) {
      final turn = Map<String, dynamic>.from(raw as Map);
      final id = turn['id'] as int;
      final old = _turns[id];
      if (old == null ||
          (turn['updateTime'] as int? ?? 0) >= (old['updateTime'] as int? ?? 0)) {
        _turns[id] = turn;
      }
    }
    _notify();
  }
  Future<void> start() async {
    if (_disposed || _active || _authFailed) return;
    _active = true;
    final generation = ++_generation;
    final cancel = _connection = CancelToken();
    try {
      if (_sessionId == null) {
        var state = await service.request('/me', cancelToken: cancel);
        if (state['initialized'] != true || state['sessionId'] == null) {
          state = await service.request(
            '/me/bootstrap',
            method: 'POST',
            data: {
              if (displayName.trim().isNotEmpty)
                'displayName': displayName.trim(),
              'languageTag': 'zh-CN',
              'timeZone': 'Asia/Shanghai',
              'soundEnabled': true,
            },
            cancelToken: cancel,
          );
        }
        _sessionId = state['sessionId'] as String?;
        if (_sessionId == null || _sessionId!.isEmpty) {
          throw const ChatException('聊天会话尚未初始化');
        }
      }
      await service.request('/home', cancelToken: cancel);
      final snapshot = await service.request(
        '/sessions/${Uri.encodeComponent(_sessionId!)}/messages',
        cancelToken: cancel,
      );
      if (generation != _generation) return;
      merge(snapshot);
      loading = false;
      error = null;
      _notify();
      await for (final event in service.subscribe(_sessionId!, cancel)) {
        if (generation != _generation) return;
        if (event.type == 'stream_error') {
          throw const ChatException('聊天连接中断，正在重连');
        }
        if (event.type == 'snapshot') {
          merge(Map<String, dynamic>.from(jsonDecode(event.data) as Map));
          _attempt = 0;
          error = null;
          _notify();
        }
      }
    } catch (e, stack) {
      if (kDebugMode) debugPrint('[Chat] stream failure ${e.runtimeType}\n$stack');
      if (generation != _generation) return;
      if (e is DioException && e.type == DioExceptionType.cancel) return;
      await _handleError(e);
    } finally {
      if (generation == _generation && !_disposed) {
        _active = false;
        loading = false;
        _notify();
        if (!_authFailed) {
          final seconds = [1, 2, 4, 8, 10][min(_attempt++, 4)];
          _reconnect = Timer(Duration(seconds: seconds), start);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  Future<void> _handleError(Object e) async {
    if (e is DioException && e.response?.statusCode == 401) {
      _authFailed = true;
      error = '登录已过期，请重新登录';
      _notify();
      await onUnauthorized?.call();
    } else {
      error = e is DioException
          ? e.userMessage
          : e is ChatException
          ? e.message
          : '聊天暂时不可用，请稍后重试';
      _notify();
    }
  }
  Future<bool> send(String text) async {
    text = text.trim();
    if (sending || _sessionId == null || _authFailed || text.isEmpty) {
      return false;
    }
    if (text.runes.length > 4000) {
      error = '单条消息最多 4000 字';
      _notify();
      return false;
    }
    // Keep the operation ID after an ambiguous network failure.
    if (_pendingText != text) {
      _pendingText = text;
      final random = Random.secure();
      _pendingId = List.generate(
        16,
        (_) => random.nextInt(256).toRadixString(16).padLeft(2, '0'),
      ).join();
    }
    sending = true;
    error = null;
    _notify();
    final cancel = _sendCancel = CancelToken();
    try {
      final turn = await service.request(
        '/sessions/${Uri.encodeComponent(_sessionId!)}/messages',
        method: 'POST',
        data: {
          'clientMessageId': _pendingId,
          'message': text,
          'attachmentIds': <String>[],
        },
        cancelToken: cancel,
      );
      if (_disposed) return false;
      merge({
        'turns': [turn],
      });
      _pendingText = null;
      _pendingId = null;
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      if (!_disposed) await _handleError(e);
      return false;
    } finally {
      sending = false;
      _notify();
    }
  }
  void pause() {
    ++_generation;
    _active = false;
    _reconnect?.cancel();
    _connection?.cancel();
  }
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _disposed = true;
    pause();
    _sendCancel?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }
}
class VoiceClip {
  const VoiceClip({
    required this.path,
    required this.duration,
    this.ephemeral = false,
  });
  final String path;
  final Duration duration;
  final bool ephemeral;
  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    'path': path,
    'durationMs': duration.inMilliseconds,
    'ephemeral': ephemeral,
  };
  factory VoiceClip.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> value) => VoiceClip(
    path: value['path'] as String,
    duration: Duration(milliseconds: value['durationMs'] as int),
    ephemeral: value['ephemeral'] == true,
  );
}
class ChatMessage {
  const ChatMessage(this.text, {this.isUser = false, this.voice});
  final String text;
  final bool isUser;
  final VoiceClip? voice;
  factory ChatMessage.fromStored(String value) {
    final separator = value.indexOf('：');
    return ChatMessage(
      separator < 0 ? value : value.substring(separator + 1),
      isUser: value.startsWith('你：'),
    );
  }
  factory ChatMessage.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> value) => ChatMessage(
    value['text'] as String? ?? '',
    isUser: value['isUser'] == true,
    voice: value['voice'] is Map
        ? VoiceClip.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic>.from(value['voice'] as Map))
        : null,
  );
  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
    'text': text,
    'isUser': isUser,
    if (voice != null) 'voice': voice!.toJson(),
  };
}
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'chat_artwork.dart';
import 'chat_message.dart';
import 'voice_capture.dart';
import 'voice_playback.dart';
import 'voice_storage.dart';
/// Shared welcome/conversation UI. Microphone access starts only on a tap.
/// Audio is recorded locally; a server upload is not implied by saving it.
class ChatPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const ChatPage({
    super.key,
    required this.onExit,
    required this.onExplore,
    this.messages = const [],
    this.onSend,
    this.onVoiceRecorded,
    this.voiceCapture,
    this.voicePlayback,
    this.name = 'Tony',
    this.exitLabel = '跳过',
    this.initialDraft = '',
    this.initialRecordingPreview = false,
    this.startRecordingOnOpen = false,
    this.animate = true,
    this.busy = false,
    this.error,
    this.timeLabel,
    this.returningUser = false,
    this.showWelcome = true,
  });
  final bool returningUser, showWelcome;
  final VoidCallback onExit, onExplore;
  final List<ChatMessage> messages;
  final Future<bool> Function(String text)? onSend;
  final Future<bool> Function(VoiceClip clip)? onVoiceRecorded;
  final VoiceCapture? voiceCapture;
  final VoicePlayback? voicePlayback;
  final String name, exitLabel, initialDraft;
  final bool initialRecordingPreview, startRecordingOnOpen, animate, busy;
  final String? error, timeLabel;
  @override
  State<ChatPage> createState() => _ChatPageState();
}
class _ChatPageState extends State<ChatPage> with WidgetsBindingObserver {
  late final _input = TextEditingController(text: widget.initialDraft);
  final _focus = FocusNode();
  final _scroll = ScrollController();
  late final _localMessages = [...widget.messages];
  late bool _recordingPreview = widget.initialRecordingPreview;
  late final _voice = widget.voiceCapture ?? VoiceCapture();
  VoicePlayback? _playback;
  StreamSubscription<void>? _playbackComplete;
  String? _playingPath;
  bool _sending = false;
  Future<bool>? _voiceSave;
  String? _error;
  final _ephemeralMessages = <ChatMessage>[];
  late final _openedAt = DateTime.now();
  bool get _recording =>
      _recordingPreview || _voice.recording || _voice.pending != null;
  int get _seconds => _voice.elapsed.inSeconds;
  List<ChatMessage> get _messages => [
    ...widget.onSend == null ? _localMessages : widget.messages,
    ..._ephemeralMessages,
  ];
  bool get _busy => widget.busy || _sending || _voice.busy;
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addObserver(this);
    _voice.addListener(_refreshVoice);
    if (widget.startRecordingOnOpen) {
      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
        if (mounted) unawaited(_startRecording());
      });
    }
  }
  void _refreshVoice() {
    if (mounted) setState(() {});
  }
  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant ChatPage oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    if ((!_scroll.hasClients || _scroll.offset < 80) &&
        (oldWidget.messages.length != widget.messages.length ||
            oldWidget.messages.lastOrNull?.text !=
                widget.messages.lastOrNull?.text ||
            oldWidget.messages.map((m) => m.text).join() !=
                widget.messages.map((m) => m.text).join())) {
      _scrollToLatest();
    }
  }
  Future<void> _startRecording() async {
    if (_busy) return;
    _focus.unfocus();
    await _stopPlayback();
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() {
      _error = null;
      _recordingPreview = false;
    });
    await _voice.start();
  }
  Future<void> _cancelRecording() async {
    if (_recordingPreview) {
      setState(() => _recordingPreview = false);
      return;
    }
    await _voice.cancel();
  }
  Future<void> _saveRecording() async {
    if (_busy) return;
    if (_recordingPreview) {
      setState(() => _recordingPreview = false);
      return;
    }
    final clip = await _voice.finish();
    if (!mounted || clip == null) return;
    setState(() {
      _sending = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      // Browser blob URLs are scoped to this page lifetime and are never
      // persisted as if they were durable native files.
      _voiceSave = clip.ephemeral || widget.onVoiceRecorded == null
          ? Future.value(true)
          : widget.onVoiceRecorded!(clip);
      final saved = await _voiceSave!;
      if (!mounted) return;
      if (!saved) {
        setState(() => _error = '保存失败，录音已保留，请重试或取消。');
        return;
      }
      if (clip.ephemeral || widget.onVoiceRecorded == null) {
        _ephemeralMessages.add(ChatMessage('', isUser: true, voice: clip));
      }
      _voice.accept();
      _scrollToLatest();
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _error = '保存失败，录音已保留，请重试或取消。');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _sending = false;
          _voiceSave = null;
        });
      }
    }
  }
  Future<void> _stopPlayback() async {
    try {
      await _playback?.stop();
    } catch (_) {
      /* Keep capture available. */
    }
    if (mounted) setState(() => _playingPath = null);
  }
  Future<void> _play(VoiceClip clip) async {
    if (_busy || _recording) return;
    if (_playingPath == clip.path) {
      await _stopPlayback();
      return;
    }
    try {
      if (_playback == null) {
        _playback = widget.voicePlayback ?? DeviceVoicePlayback();
        _playbackComplete = _playback!.completed.listen((_) {
          if (mounted) setState(() => _playingPath = null);
        });
      }
      await _playback!.stop();
      await _playback!.play(clip);
      if (mounted) setState(() => _playingPath = clip.path);
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _playingPath = null;
          _error = '无法播放这条本地录音，文件可能已被清理。';
        });
      }
    }
  }
  @override
  void didChangeAppLifecycleState(AppLifecycleState state) {
    if (state == AppLifecycleState.paused ||
        state == AppLifecycleState.hidden ||
        state == AppLifecycleState.detached) {
      if (_voice.recording || (_voice.busy && _voice.pending == null)) {
        unawaited(_voice.cancel());
      }
      unawaited(_stopPlayback());
    }
  }
  Future<void> _send() async {
    final text = _input.text.trim();
    if (_busy || text.isEmpty) return;
    setState(() {
      _sending = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final sent = await widget.onSend?.call(text) ?? true;
      if (!mounted) return;
      if (!sent) {
        setState(() => _error = widget.error == null ? '发送失败，请重试' : null);
        return;
      }
      if (widget.onSend == null) {
        _localMessages.addAll([
          ChatMessage(text, isUser: true),
          const ChatMessage('谢谢你告诉我！你想先聊聊兴趣，还是一起探索这个世界？（模拟回复）'),
        ]);
      }
      _input.clear();
      _scrollToLatest();
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() => _error = widget.error == null ? '发送失败，请重试' : null);
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _sending = false);
    }
  }
  void _scrollToLatest() => WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
    if (mounted && _scroll.hasClients) _scroll.jumpTo(0);
  });
  @override
  void dispose() {
    WidgetsBinding.instance.removeObserver(this);
    _voice.removeListener(_refreshVoice);
    unawaited(_closeVoice());
    unawaited(_playbackComplete?.cancel());
    if (_playback != null) {
      if (widget.voicePlayback == null) {
        unawaited(_playback!.dispose());
      } else {
        unawaited(_playback!.stop());
      }
    }
    for (final message in _ephemeralMessages) {
      final clip = message.voice;
      if (clip != null) {
        unawaited(deleteVoiceFile(clip.path).catchError((_) {}));
      }
    }
    _input.dispose();
    _focus.dispose();
    _scroll.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }
  Future<void> _closeVoice() async {
    try {
      if (await _voiceSave == true) _voice.accept();
    } catch (_) {
      /* Uncommitted drafts are removed below. */
    }
    if (widget.voiceCapture == null) {
      _voice.dispose();
    } else {
      await _voice.cancel();
    }
  }
  Widget _header() => Row(
    children: [
      if (widget.returningUser)
        IconButton(
          tooltip: '返回',
          onPressed: _busy ? null : widget.onExit,
          icon: chatSvg('back', width: 24, height: 24),
        ),
      Container(
        height: 36,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(6, 4, 16, 4),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: const Color(0x62000000),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(40),
        ),
        child: Row(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            ClipOval(
              child: Image.asset(
                'assets/chat/avatar.png',
                width: 28,
                height: 28,
                excludeFromSemantics: true,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 9),
            Text(
              widget.name,
              style: const TextStyle(
                inherit: false,
                textBaseline: TextBaseline.alphabetic,
                fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
                fontSize: 14,
                height: 18 / 14,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                color: Colors.white,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      const Spacer(),
      if (!widget.returningUser)
        TextButton(
          onPressed: _busy ? null : widget.onExit,
          style: TextButton.styleFrom(
            overlayColor: Colors.transparent,
            foregroundColor: const Color(0xD9FFFFFF),
            minimumSize: const Size(44, 44),
            padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
          ),
          child: Text(
            widget.exitLabel,
            style: const TextStyle(
              inherit: false,
              textBaseline: TextBaseline.alphabetic,
              fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
              fontSize: 14,
            ),
          ),
        ),
    ],
  );
  Widget _transcript({double bottomPadding = 0}) {
    final time =
        widget.timeLabel ??
        '${_openedAt.hour.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${_openedAt.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}';
    final transcript = SingleChildScrollView(
      key: const ValueKey('chat-transcript'),
      physics: const ClampingScrollPhysics(),
      controller: _scroll,
      reverse: true,
      padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 0, 16, bottomPadding),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 1.5),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: const Color(0x66000000),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(57),
            ),
            child: Text(
              time,
              style: const TextStyle(
                inherit: false,
                textBaseline: TextBaseline.alphabetic,
                fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
                fontSize: 12,
                height: 14 / 12,
                color: Colors.white,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 9, vertical: 5),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: const Color(0x66000000),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(57),
            ),
            child: const Text(
              '所有内容均由 AI 生成，均为虚构内容。',
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: TextStyle(
                inherit: false,
                textBaseline: TextBaseline.alphabetic,
                fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
                fontSize: 12,
                height: 1.5,
                color: Color(0xCCFFFFFF),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 21),
          if (widget.showWelcome)
            SizedBox(
              width: double.infinity,
              child: ChatGreeting(name: widget.name),
            ),
          for (var i = 0; i < _messages.length; i++) ...[
            SizedBox(height: i == 0 ? 24 : 16),
            if (_messages[i].voice case final clip?)
              _voiceBubble(clip)
            else
              ChatBubble(text: _messages[i].text, isUser: _messages[i].isUser),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
    return _messages.length < 3
        ? transcript
        : ShaderMask(
            blendMode: BlendMode.dstIn,
            shaderCallback: (bounds) => LinearGradient(
              begin: Alignment.topCenter,
              end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
              colors: const [Colors.transparent, Colors.white, Colors.white],
              stops: [0, (161 / bounds.height).clamp(0, 1), 1],
            ).createShader(bounds),
            child: transcript,
          );
  }
  Widget _voiceBubble(VoiceClip clip) => Align(
    alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
    child: ConstrainedBox(
      constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 269),
      child: Material(

          topLeft: Radius.circular(16),
          topRight: Radius.circular(16),
          bottomLeft: Radius.circular(16),
        ),
        child: InkWell(
          onTap: () => _play(clip),
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
            child: Row(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                Icon(
                  _playingPath == clip.path
                      ? Icons.stop_rounded
                      : Icons.play_arrow_rounded,
                  semanticLabel: _playingPath == clip.path ? '停止播放' : '播放录音',
                  color: const Color(0xFF51397B),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Flexible(
                  child: Text(
                    '语音 ${math.max(1, clip.duration.inSeconds)}秒 · ${clip.ephemeral ? '本次会话' : '仅本机'}',
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      inherit: false,
                      textBaseline: TextBaseline.alphabetic,
                      fontSize: 14,
                      color: Color(0xFF333333),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
  Widget _composer() => TextFieldTapRegion(
    child: Row(
      children: [
        Expanded(
          child: ChatGlass(
            child: SizedBox(
              height: 49,
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  const SizedBox(width: 18),
                  Expanded(
                    child: TextField(
                      controller: _input,
                      focusNode: _focus,
                      inputFormatters: [
                        TextInputFormatter.withFunction(
                          (oldValue, newValue) => _busy ? oldValue : newValue,
                        ),
                      ],
                      textInputAction: TextInputAction.send,
                      onEditingComplete: () {},
                      onTapOutside: (_) => _focus.unfocus(),
                      onSubmitted: (_) => _send(),
                      onChanged: (_) => setState(() {}),
                      cursorColor: const Color(0xFFA64BFB),
                      style: const TextStyle(
                        inherit: false,
                        textBaseline: TextBaseline.alphabetic,
                        fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
                        fontSize: 15,
                        color: Colors.white,
                      ),
                      decoration: const InputDecoration(
                        hintText: 'Talk to the Oracle...',
                        hintStyle: TextStyle(
                          inherit: false,
                          textBaseline: TextBaseline.alphabetic,
                          color: Color(0xFFB1B0B2),
                          fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
                          fontSize: 15,
                        ),
                        border: InputBorder.none,
                        enabledBorder: InputBorder.none,
                        focusedBorder: InputBorder.none,
                        disabledBorder: InputBorder.none,
                        isDense: true,
                        contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  SizedBox(
                    width: 48,
                    height: 48,
                    child: _busy
                        ? const Center(
                            child: SizedBox(
                              width: 22,
                              height: 22,
                              child: CircularProgressIndicator(

                                color: Colors.white,
                              ),
                            ),
                          )
                        : _input.text.isEmpty
                        ? null
                        : IconButton(

                              _focus.requestFocus();
                            },
                            padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                            icon: chatSvg('clear', width: 31, height: 31),

              icon: chatSvg('mic', width: 28, height: 28),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    ),
  );
  Widget _recordingBar() => Center(
    child: SizedBox(

                    child: chatSvg('waveform', width: 41.56, height: 23),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              SizedBox(
                width: 38,
                child: FittedBox(
                  fit: BoxFit.scaleDown,
                  alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                  child: Text(
                    '${(_seconds ~/ 60).toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:${(_seconds % 60).toString().padLeft(2, '0')}',
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      inherit: false,
                      textBaseline: TextBaseline.alphabetic,
                      fontFamily: 'Newsreader',
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                      fontSize: 14,
                      color: Colors.white,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          SizedBox(
            width: 50,
            height: 50,
            child: IconButton(
              tooltip: '完成录音',
              padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
              onPressed: _busy ? null : _saveRecording,
              icon: const ChatSendIcon(),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    ),
  );
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => AnnotatedRegion<SystemUiOverlayStyle>(
    value: SystemUiOverlayStyle.light.copyWith(
      statusBarColor: Colors.transparent,
      systemNavigationBarColor: Colors.black,
    ),
    child: PopScope(
      canPop: !_busy,
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: Colors.black,
        resizeToAvoidBottomInset: false,
        body: Stack(
          fit: StackFit.expand,
          children: [
            const RepaintBoundary(child: ChatBackground()),
            SafeArea(
              top: false,
              bottom: false,
              child: LayoutBuilder(
                builder: (context, constraints) {
                  final media = MediaQuery.of(context);
                  final headerTop =
                      math.max(
                        media.padding.top,
                        constraints.maxHeight >= 600 ? 44.0 : 0.0,
                      ) +
                      9;
                  final footerBottom = media.viewInsets.bottom > 0
                      ? media.viewInsets.bottom + 8
                      : math.max(media.padding.bottom, 34.0) + 8;
                  final footerTop = constraints.maxHeight - footerBottom - 49;
                  final bannerTop = footerTop - 68;
                  final showBanner =
                      !widget.returningUser && bannerTop > headerTop + 124;
                  final messageTop = headerTop + (showBanner ? 86 : 52);
                  // The floating explore button is an overlay, not the list
                  // boundary. Messages remain visible beside and below it,
                  // all the way down to the composer.
                  final messageBottom = math.max(messageTop + 1, footerTop - 9);
                  final welcomeGap = math.min(
                    68.0,
                    math.max(8.0, (bannerTop + 4 - messageTop) * 0.25),
                  );
                  final welcomePadding = _messages.isEmpty && showBanner
                      ? math.max(
                          0.0,
                          messageBottom - (bannerTop + 4 - welcomeGap),
                        )
                      : 0.0;
                  final error = widget.error ?? _error ?? _voice.error;
                  return Stack(
                    children: [
                      Positioned(
                        top: headerTop,
                        left: widget.returningUser ? 4 : 16,
                        right: 8,
                        height: 44,
                        child: _header(),
                      ),
                      Positioned(
                        top: messageTop,
                        left: 0,
                        right: 0,
                        height: math.max(1, messageBottom - messageTop),
                        child: _transcript(bottomPadding: welcomePadding),
                      ),
                      if (showBanner)
                        Positioned(
                          left: 0,
                          top: bannerTop,
                          child: ChatExploreBanner(
                            onPressed: _busy ? null : widget.onExplore,
                          ),
                        ),
                      if (error != null)
                        Positioned(
                          left: 16,
                          right: 16,
                          bottom: footerBottom + 57,
                          child: Text(
                            error,
                            style: const TextStyle(
                              inherit: false,
                              textBaseline: TextBaseline.alphabetic,
                              color: Colors.white,
                              fontSize: 12,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      Positioned(
                        left: 16,
                        right: 16,
                        bottom: footerBottom,
                        height: 50,
                        child: _recording ? _recordingBar() : _composer(),
                      ),
                    ],
                  );
                },
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
}
