/// Own the stream for exactly the lifetime of the visible conversation.
class LiveChatPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const LiveChatPage({
    super.key,
    required this.onExit,
    required this.onExplore,
    required this.accountService,
    this.returningUser = true,
    this.initialDraft = '',
    this.error,
    this.busy = false,
    this.startRecordingOnOpen = false,
  });
  final VoidCallback onExit, onExplore;
  final AccountService accountService;
  final bool returningUser, startRecordingOnOpen;
  final String initialDraft;
  final String? error;
  final bool busy;
  @override
  State<LiveChatPage> createState() => _LiveChatPageState();
}
class _LiveChatPageState extends State<LiveChatPage>
    with WidgetsBindingObserver {
  ChatController? _chat;
  bool _foreground = true;
  String? _error;
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addObserver(this);
    unawaited(_initialize());
  }
  Future<void> _initialize() async {
    try {
      final profile = await const StartupProfileStore().read();
      if (!mounted) return;
      _chat = ChatController(
        ChatService.authenticated(widget.accountService),
        displayName:
            profile['name'] as String? ??
            widget.accountService.session?.nick ??
            '',
        onUnauthorized: () async {
          await widget.accountService.logout();
          if (!mounted) return;
          Navigator.of(context).pushAndRemoveUntil(
            MaterialPageRoute<void>(
              builder: (_) => AuthGate(service: widget.accountService),
            ),
            (_) => false,
          );
        },
      )..addListener(_refresh);
      setState(() {});
      if (_foreground) unawaited(_chat!.start());
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _error = '无法初始化聊天，请退出后重试');
    }
  }
  void _refresh() {
    if (mounted) setState(() {});
  }
  @override
  void didChangeAppLifecycleState(AppLifecycleState state) {
    if (state == AppLifecycleState.resumed) {
      _foreground = true;
      unawaited(_chat?.start());
    } else if (state == AppLifecycleState.paused ||
        state == AppLifecycleState.hidden ||
        state == AppLifecycleState.detached) {
      _foreground = false;
      _chat?.pause();
    }
  }
  @override
  void dispose() {
    WidgetsBinding.instance.removeObserver(this);
    _chat?.removeListener(_refresh);
    _chat?.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => ChatPage(
    name: 'Tony',
    returningUser: widget.returningUser,
    messages: _chat?.messages ?? const [],
    busy: widget.busy || (_chat?.sending ?? false),
    error: widget.error ?? _error ?? _chat?.error,
    showWelcome: false,
    initialDraft: widget.initialDraft,
    startRecordingOnOpen: widget.startRecordingOnOpen,
    onSend: (text) async {
      if (_chat == null || _chat!.loading) {
        setState(() => _error = '正在连接聊天，请稍后发送');
        return false;
      }
      setState(() => _error = null);
      return _chat!.send(text);
    },
    onExit: widget.onExit,
    onExplore: widget.onExplore,
  );
}
import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:record/record.dart';
import 'chat_message.dart';
import 'voice_storage.dart';
abstract interface class VoiceRecorder {
  Future<bool> hasPermission();
  Future<void> start();
  Future<String?> stop();
  Future<void> cancel();
  Future<void> delete(String path);
  Future<void> dispose();
  Stream<double> get levels;
  Stream<void> get interruptions;
}
class DeviceVoiceRecorder implements VoiceRecorder {
  final _recorder = AudioRecorder();
  @override
  Future<bool> hasPermission() => _recorder.hasPermission();
  @override
  Future<void> start() async => _recorder.start(
    RecordConfig(
      encoder: kIsWeb ? AudioEncoder.wav : AudioEncoder.aacLc,
      sampleRate: 24000,
      bitRate: 64000,
      numChannels: 1,
    ),
    path: await newVoicePath(),
  );
  @override
  Future<String?> stop() async {
    final path = await _recorder.stop();
    if (path == null || !await voiceFileExists(path)) {
      if (path != null) await deleteVoiceFile(path);
      return null;
    }
    return path;
  }
  @override
  Future<void> cancel() => _recorder.cancel();
  @override
  Future<void> delete(String path) => deleteVoiceFile(path);
  @override
  Future<void> dispose() => _recorder.dispose();
  @override
  Stream<double> get levels => _recorder
      .onAmplitudeChanged(const Duration(milliseconds: 100))
      .map((level) => ((level.current + 60) / 60).clamp(0.0, 1.0));
  @override
  Stream<void> get interruptions => _recorder
      .onStateChanged()
      .where((state) => state != RecordState.record)
      .map((_) {});
}
/// Lazily opens the microphone only after an explicit tap. A pending take is
/// retained if persistence fails; cancellation and page disposal delete drafts.
class VoiceCapture extends ChangeNotifier {
  VoiceCapture({VoiceRecorder? backend, Duration Function()? elapsed})
    : _recorder = backend,
      _elapsedOverride = elapsed;
  VoiceRecorder? _recorder;
  final Duration Function()? _elapsedOverride;
  final _watch = Stopwatch();
  Timer? _timer;
  StreamSubscription<double>? _levels;
  StreamSubscription<void>? _interruptions;
  bool _disposed = false;
  bool _deviceActive = false;
  int _generation = 0;
  Future<void>? _starting;
  Future<VoiceClip?>? _finishing;
  bool busy = false, recording = false;
  double level = 0;
  Duration elapsed = Duration.zero;
  VoiceClip? pending;
  String? error;
  Duration get _duration => _elapsedOverride?.call() ?? _watch.elapsed;
  void _notify() {
    if (!_disposed) notifyListeners();
  }
  Future<void> start() {
    if (busy || recording || pending != null || _disposed) {
      return Future.value();
    }
    final future = _start();
    _starting = future;
    return future;
  }
  Future<void> _start() async {
    final generation = ++_generation;
    busy = true;
    error = null;
    _notify();
    try {
      final recorder = _recorder ??= DeviceVoiceRecorder();
      if (!await recorder.hasPermission()) {
        error = '未获得麦克风权限，请在系统设置中允许，或继续文字聊天。';
        return;
      }
      if (_disposed || generation != _generation) return;
      await recorder.start();
      _deviceActive = true;
      if (_disposed || generation != _generation) {
        await recorder.cancel();
        _deviceActive = false;
        return;
      }
      recording = true;
      elapsed = Duration.zero;
      _watch
        ..reset()
        ..start();
      _levels = recorder.levels.listen(
        (value) {
          level = value;
          _notify();
        },
        onError: (_) {
          level = 0;
          _notify();
        },
      );
      _interruptions = recorder.interruptions.listen((_) {
        if (recording && !busy) {
          unawaited(
            cancel().then((_) {
              error = '录音已中断，请重新录制。';
              _notify();
            }),
          );
        }
      });
      _timer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(milliseconds: 200), (_) {
        elapsed = _duration;
        _notify();
        if (elapsed >= const Duration(minutes: 2)) unawaited(finish());
      });
    } catch (_) {
      error = '无法开始录音，请检查麦克风是否可用后重试。';
      try {
        await _recorder?.cancel();
      } catch (_) {
        /* Best-effort device release. */
      }
    } finally {
      busy = false;
      _notify();
    }
  }
  Future<void> _stopMonitoring() async {
    _timer?.cancel();
    _watch.stop();
    await _levels?.cancel();
    await _interruptions?.cancel();
    _levels = null;
    _interruptions = null;
    level = 0;
  }
  Future<VoiceClip?> finish() {
    if (busy || _disposed) return Future.value(null);
    final future = _finish();
    _finishing = future;
    return future;
  }
  Future<VoiceClip?> _finish() async {
    if (busy || _disposed) return null;
    if (pending != null) return pending;
    if (!recording) return null;
    busy = true;
    elapsed = _duration;
    recording = false;
    _notify();
    try {
      await _stopMonitoring();
      final path = await _recorder!.stop();
      _deviceActive = false;
      if (_disposed) {
        if (path != null) await _recorder!.delete(path);
        return null;
      }
      if (path == null || elapsed < const Duration(milliseconds: 500)) {
        if (path != null) await _recorder!.delete(path);
        error = '录音太短或没有有效音频，请重新录制。';
        return null;
      }
      pending = VoiceClip(path: path, duration: elapsed, ephemeral: kIsWeb);
      return pending;
    } catch (_) {
      error = '录音保存失败，请重试。';
      try {
        await _recorder?.cancel();
      } catch (_) {
        /* Best-effort device release. */
      }
      return null;
    } finally {
      busy = false;
      _notify();
    }
  }
  /// Transfer ownership to the saved conversation after persistence succeeds.
  void accept() {
    pending = null;
    error = null;
    _notify();
  }
  Future<void> cancel() async {
    ++_generation;
    // An in-flight permission/start operation checks the generation before
    // opening the mic, or immediately cancels after the platform start returns.
    if (busy) {
      await _starting;
      await _finishing;
      if (busy) return;
    }
    busy = true;
    recording = false;
    _notify();
    try {
      await _stopMonitoring();
      if (_deviceActive) {
        await _recorder?.cancel();
        _deviceActive = false;
      }
      final clip = pending;
      if (clip != null) await _recorder?.delete(clip.path);
      pending = null;
      elapsed = Duration.zero;
    } catch (_) {
      error = '无法取消录音，请重试。';
    } finally {
      busy = false;
      _notify();
    }
  }
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _disposed = true;
    ++_generation;
    unawaited(_close());
    super.dispose();
  }
  Future<void> _close() async {
    await _starting;
    await _finishing;
    await _stopMonitoring();
    try {
      if (_deviceActive) await _recorder?.cancel();
      if (pending != null) await _recorder?.delete(pending!.path);
    } catch (_) {
      /* Dispose must still release the platform recorder. */
    }
    try {
      await _recorder?.dispose();
    } catch (_) {
      /* Device already closed. */
    }
  }
}
import 'package:audioplayers/audioplayers.dart';
import 'chat_message.dart';
abstract interface class VoicePlayback {
  Future<void> play(VoiceClip clip);
  Future<void> stop();
  Future<void> dispose();
  Stream<void> get completed;
}
class DeviceVoicePlayback implements VoicePlayback {
  final _player = AudioPlayer();
  @override
  Future<void> play(VoiceClip clip) => _player.play(
    clip.ephemeral ? UrlSource(clip.path) : DeviceFileSource(clip.path),
  );
  @override
  Future<void> stop() => _player.stop();
  @override
  Future<void> dispose() => _player.dispose();
  @override
  Stream<void> get completed => _player.onPlayerComplete;
}
export 'voice_storage_io.dart'
    if (dart.library.js_interop) 'voice_storage_web.dart';
import 'dart:io';
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';
Future<String> newVoicePath() async {
  final root = await getApplicationSupportDirectory();
  final directory = await Directory('${root.path}/voice-notes')
      .create(recursive: true);
  return '${directory.path}/voice-${DateTime.now().microsecondsSinceEpoch}.m4a';
}
Future<bool> voiceFileExists(String path) async {
  final file = File(path);
  return await file.exists() && await file.length() > 44;
}
Future<void> deleteVoiceFile(String path) async {
  final file = File(path);
  if (await file.exists()) await file.delete();
}
import 'package:web/web.dart' as web;
Future<String> newVoicePath() async => '';
Future<bool> voiceFileExists(String path) async => path.startsWith('blob:');
Future<void> deleteVoiceFile(String path) async =>
    web.URL.revokeObjectURL(path);
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'experience_store.dart';
import '../chat/chat_message.dart';
enum ExperienceStep {
  welcome,
  world,
  name,
  character,
  chat,
  interests,
  notification,
  home,
}
class DemoCharacter {
  const DemoCharacter(this.id, this.name, this.description);
  final String id, name, description;
}
const demoCharacters = [
  DemoCharacter('nova', 'Nova', '好奇、活泼，喜欢音乐和旅行'),
  DemoCharacter('milo', 'Milo', '温和、细腻，喜欢阅读和电影'),
  DemoCharacter('lumi', 'Lumi', '富有想象力，喜欢游戏和艺术'),
];
const interestOptions = ['音乐', '旅行', '阅读', '电影', '游戏', '艺术'];
class ExperienceController extends ChangeNotifier {
  ExperienceController(this.store, {DateTime Function()? clock})
    : clock = clock ?? DateTime.now;
  final ExperienceStore store;
  final DateTime Function() clock;
  Map<String, dynamic> _data = {'step': 'welcome'};
  bool ready = false, busy = false;
  String? error;
  ExperienceStep get step =>
      ExperienceStep.values.byName(_data['step'] as String);
  String? get onboardingPageId =>
      _data['onboardingPageId'] as String? ??
      (step == ExperienceStep.world ? 'world' : null);
  Future<void> advanceOnboarding(String? nextPageId) => _save({
    'onboardingPageId': nextPageId,
    // Keep compatibility with existing mock-flow steps and old saved progress.
    'step': nextPageId == null
        ? 'name'
        : nextPageId == 'world'
        ? 'world'
        : 'welcome',
  });
  String get name => _data['name'] as String? ?? '';
  DemoCharacter get character => demoCharacters.firstWhere(
    (c) => c.id == _data['character'],
    orElse: () => demoCharacters.first,
  );
  List<String> get interests =>
      List<String>.from(_data['interests'] as List? ?? []);
  List<String> get messages =>
      List<String>.from(_data['messages'] as List? ?? []);
  List<ChatMessage> get chatMessages {
    final saved = _data['chatMessages'];
    return saved is List
        ? saved
              .map(
                (entry) => ChatMessage.fromJson(
                  Map<String, dynamic>.from(entry as Map),
                ),
              )
              .toList()
        : messages.map(ChatMessage.fromStored).toList();
  }
  Future<bool> saveVoice(VoiceClip clip) async {
    if (busy || clip.ephemeral) return false;
    await _save({
      'chatMessages': [
        ...chatMessages.map((message) => message.toJson()),
        ChatMessage('', isUser: true, voice: clip).toJson(),
      ],
    });
    return error == null;
  }
  bool get muted => _data['muted'] == true;
  bool get hasTask => _data['startedAt'] != null;
  int get elapsed => hasTask
      ? clock()
            .difference(DateTime.parse(_data['startedAt'] as String))
            .inSeconds
      : 0;
  int get discoveryCount => !hasTask || elapsed < 5
      ? 0
      : elapsed < 10
      ? 1
      : 2;
  bool isRead(int index) => (_data['read'] as List? ?? []).contains(index);
  String discoveryTitle(int index) => index == 0
      ? '${interests.isEmpty ? '音乐' : interests.first}：一个值得尝试的小灵感'
      : '${character.name} 认识了新朋友';
  String discoveryBody(int index) => index == 0
      ? '模拟发现：为自己留出十分钟，挑选一个喜欢的作品，记录最打动你的地方。这个内容用于验证详情与追问流程，并非实时搜索结果。'
      : '模拟社交经历：${character.name} 与另一位 AI 交流了各自的兴趣，约好下次交换推荐。完整 AI 对话查看暂未开放。';
  Future<void> load() async {
    try {
      final saved = await store.read();
      if (saved != null) {
        ExperienceStep.values.byName(saved['step'] as String);
        if (saved['startedAt'] != null) {
          DateTime.parse(saved['startedAt'] as String);
        }
        for (final key in ['interests', 'messages']) {
          List<String>.from(saved[key] as List? ?? []);
        }
        _data = saved;
      }
      ready = true;
      error = null;
    } catch (_) {
      error = '无法恢复模拟进度，请重试。';
    }
    notifyListeners();
  }
  Future<void> _save(Map<String, dynamic> changes) async {
    if (busy) return;
    busy = true;
    error = null;
    notifyListeners();
    final next = {..._data, ...changes};
    try {
      await store.write(next);
      _data = next;
    } catch (_) {
      error = '保存失败，请重试；当前进度未推进。';
    } finally {
      busy = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }
  Future<void> go(ExperienceStep next) => _save({'step': next.name});
  Future<void> setName(String value) async {
    if (value.trim().isEmpty) return;
    await _save({'name': value.trim(), 'step': 'character'});
  }
  Future<void> bind(String id) async {
    if (_data['character'] != null || !demoCharacters.any((c) => c.id == id)) {
      return;
    }
    await _save({'character': id, 'step': 'chat'});
  }
  Future<void> saveInterests(List<String> values) =>
      _save({'interests': values.toSet().toList()});
  Future<void> toggleMute() => _save({'muted': !muted});
  Future<void> toggleInterest(String value) {
    final selected = interests;
    selected.contains(value) ? selected.remove(value) : selected.add(value);
    return _save({'interests': selected});
  }
  Future<void> send(String text) async {
    if (text.trim().isEmpty) return;
    final detected = {...interests, ...interestOptions.where(text.contains)};
    await _save({
      'interests': detected.toList(),
      'chatMessages': [
        ...chatMessages.map((message) => message.toJson()),
        ChatMessage(text.trim(), isUser: true).toJson(),
        const ChatMessage('谢谢你分享！我记住了，我们可以围绕这些兴趣一起探索。（模拟回复）').toJson(),
      ],
      'messages': [
        ...messages,
        '你：${text.trim()}',
        '${character.name}：谢谢你分享！我记住了，我们可以围绕这些兴趣一起探索。（模拟回复）',
      ],
    });
  }
  Future<void> start() async {
    if (hasTask) {
      await go(ExperienceStep.home);
      return;
    }
    await _save({
      'startedAt': clock().toUtc().toIso8601String(),
      'step': 'notification',
    });
  }
  Future<void> notification(bool accepted) => _save({
    'notification': accepted ? 'systemAuthorized' : 'skipped',
    'step': 'home',
    'onboardingCompleted': true,
  });
  Future<void> markRead(int index) => _save({
    'read': {...List<int>.from(_data['read'] as List? ?? []), index}.toList(),
  });
  Future<void> reset() async {
    if (busy) return;
    busy = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      await store.write({'step': 'welcome'});
      _data = {'step': 'welcome'};
      ready = true;
      error = null;
    } catch (_) {
      error = '重置失败，请重试。';
    } finally {
      busy = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }
}
import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import '../notifications/push_service.dart';
import '../notifications/notification_prompt.dart';
import '../home/home_page.dart';
import '../plaza/plaza_page.dart';
import '../onboarding/onboarding_flow.dart';
import '../onboarding/interests_page.dart';
import '../auth/startup_profile_store.dart';
import '../chat/chat_page.dart';
import '../chat/live_chat_page.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import '../auth/account_service.dart';
import '../auth/auth_gate.dart';
import '../../core/config/app_env.dart';
import '../auth/login_demo_page.dart';
import 'experience_controller.dart';
import 'experience_store.dart';
class ExperiencePage extends StatefulWidget {
  const ExperiencePage({
    super.key,
    this.controller,
    this.accountService,
    this.previewWelcomeOnStart = false,
    this.registrationCompleted = false,
    this.returningUser = true,
    this.onRegistrationChatComplete,
    this.userId = 'local-preview',
    this.profileStore = const StartupProfileStore(),
  });
  final bool previewWelcomeOnStart;
  final bool registrationCompleted;
  final bool returningUser;
  final String userId;
  final StartupProfileStore profileStore;
  final Future<void> Function()? onRegistrationChatComplete;
  final ExperienceController? controller;
  final AccountService? accountService;
  @override
  State<ExperiencePage> createState() => _ExperiencePageState();
}
class _ExperiencePageState extends State<ExperiencePage>
    with WidgetsBindingObserver {
  late final ExperienceController flow;
  final input = TextEditingController();
  Timer? timer;
  final push = PushService();
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    flow = widget.controller ?? ExperienceController(LocalExperienceStore());
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addObserver(this);
    flow.addListener(refresh);
    flow.load();
    push.addListener(refresh);
    push.initialize();
    timer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 1), (_) => refresh());
  }
  bool demoOpening = false;
  bool _welcomePreviewDismissed = false;
  bool _showFlowTools = false;
  bool _finishingRegistration = false;
  String? _registrationError;
  Future<void> _finishRegistrationChat({bool explore = false}) async {
    if (_finishingRegistration || flow.busy) return;
    setState(() {
      _finishingRegistration = true;
      _registrationError = null;
    });
    try {
      await widget.onRegistrationChatComplete?.call();
      if (mounted && explore) await _explore();
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _registrationError = '保存失败，请重试');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _finishingRegistration = false);
    }
  }
  void refresh() {
    if (!mounted) return;
    setState(() {});
    final notification = push.pendingNotification;
    if (kDebugMode &&
        !demoOpening &&
        notification?['source'] == 'local-demo' &&
        notification?['discoveryId'] == 'local-demo-discovery') {
      push.pendingNotification = null;
      demoOpening = true;
      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) async {
        if (!mounted) return;
        await Navigator.of(context).push(
          MaterialPageRoute<void>(
            builder: (_) => Scaffold(
              appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('模拟发现详情')),
              body: const SafeArea(
                child: Padding(
                  padding: EdgeInsets.all(24),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text('给自己留十分钟听首歌', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 24)),
                      SizedBox(height: 16),
                      Text('这是一条固定的本地模拟发现。你已完成：安排通知 → 系统展示 → 点击 → 进入详情。'),
                      SizedBox(height: 16),
                      Text('无需登录或后端，仅验证前端交互；没有执行真实探索，也没有经过 APNs。'),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        );
        demoOpening = false;
        refresh();
      });
    }
  }
  @override
  void didChangeAppLifecycleState(AppLifecycleState state) {
    refresh();
    if (state == AppLifecycleState.resumed) push.refresh();
  }
  @override
  void dispose() {
    timer?.cancel();
    push.removeListener(refresh);
    push.dispose();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.removeObserver(this);
    flow.removeListener(refresh);
    if (widget.controller == null) flow.dispose();
    input.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }
  Widget action(String label, Future<void> Function() onTap) => Padding(
    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 6),
    child: FilledButton(
      onPressed: flow.busy ? null : onTap,
      child: Text(label),
    ),
  );
  Widget heading(String title, String body) => Column(
    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
    children: [
      Text(title, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineMedium),
      const SizedBox(height: 16),
      Text(body),
      const SizedBox(height: 24),
    ],
  );
  Widget chat() => Column(
    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
    children: [
      Text('${flow.character.name}：你好，${flow.name}！最近有什么让你特别感兴趣的事？'),
      ...flow.messages.map(
        (m) => Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
          child: Text(m),
        ),
      ),
      TextField(
        controller: input,
        decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: '输入消息（本版仅文字模拟）'),
      ),
      action('发送', () async {
        final text = input.text;
        await flow.send(text);
        if (flow.error == null) input.clear();
      }),
    ],
  );
  Future<void> detail(int index) async {
    await flow.markRead(index);
    if (!mounted) return;
    await showModalBottomSheet<void>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      builder: (context) => SafeArea(
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              Text(
                flow.discoveryTitle(index),
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              Text(flow.discoveryBody(index)),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              FilledButton(
                onPressed: () {
                  input.text = '聊聊「${flow.discoveryTitle(index)}」';
                  Navigator.pop(context);
                },
                child: const Text('带回聊天继续聊'),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
  List<Widget> content() {
    switch (flow.step) {
      case ExperienceStep.welcome:
        return [
          heading('欢迎来到 ViiTor', '这里生活着许多独特的 AI，它们有自己的性格、兴趣和故事，正在寻找属于自己的朋友。'),
          const Icon(Icons.graphic_eq, size: 64),
          const Text('旁白占位：尚未接入音频'),
          action(flow.muted ? '取消静音（模拟）' : '静音（模拟）', flow.toggleMute),
          action('Continue', () => flow.go(ExperienceStep.world)),
        ];
      case ExperienceStep.world:
        return [
          heading('探索世界，再回来分享', 'AI 不只是等待聊天。它们会探索世界、发现有趣的事、认识其他 AI，再回来与你分享故事。'),
          action('Meet your AI friend', () => flow.go(ExperienceStep.name)),
        ];
      case ExperienceStep.name:
        return [
          heading('你想让我如何称呼你？', '用文字输入名字，帮助 AI 准确称呼你。'),
          TextField(
            controller: input,
            maxLength: 40,
            decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: '你的名字'),
          ),
          action('继续', () async {
            await flow.setName(input.text);
            if (flow.step != ExperienceStep.name) input.clear();
          }),
        ];
      case ExperienceStep.character:
        return [
          heading('认识你的 AI', '模拟候选列表。暂用手动选择验证流程，每位用户只绑定一个 AI；最终获取方式待确认。'),
          ...demoCharacters.map(
            (c) => Card(
              child: ListTile(
                title: Text(c.name),
                subtitle: Text(c.description),
                trailing: const Icon(Icons.chevron_right),
                onTap: flow.busy ? null : () => flow.bind(c.id),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ];
      case ExperienceStep.chat:
        return [
          heading(
            '与 ${flow.character.name} 初次见面',
            '模拟回复；输入含音乐、旅行等兴趣词时会自动带入爱好选择。',
          ),
          chat(),
          action('去选择爱好 / 先去探索', () => flow.go(ExperienceStep.interests)),
        ];
      case ExperienceStep.interests:
        return [
          heading('你喜欢什么？', '聊天里提到的爱好已自动带入。可以调整，也可以直接开始。'),
          Wrap(
            spacing: 8,
            children: interestOptions
                .map(
                  (i) => FilterChip(
                    label: Text(i),
                    selected: flow.interests.contains(i),
                    onSelected: flow.busy
                        ? null
                        : (_) => flow.toggleInterest(i),
                  ),
                )
                .toList(),
          ),
          action('开始探索（爱好可选）', flow.start),
        ];
      case ExperienceStep.notification:
        return [
          heading('有新发现时，要告诉你吗？', '允许通知后，有真实发现时可以通知你。当前探索仍为模拟，后端推送尚未接通。'),
          if (push.error != null) Text(push.error!),
          if (push.authorization == 'denied')
            action('通知已关闭，前往系统设置', push.openSettings),
          action(push.busy ? '正在申请…' : '允许通知并继续', () async {
            if (push.busy) return;
            await push.request();
            if (push.error == null || !push.supported) {
              await flow.notification(push.allowed);
            }
          }),
          action('先不用', () => flow.notification(false)),
        ];
      case ExperienceStep.home:
        return [
          if (push.pendingNotification != null)
            const Text('已收到通知点击，真实发现详情等待后端接口接入。'),
          if (push.supported)
            Text(
              '通知：${push.authorization} · ${push.token == null ? '尚未获取 APNs token' : '已获取 APNs token'} · 尚未登记到后端',
            ),
          if (push.error != null) Text(push.error!),
          if (push.supported && push.allowed && push.token == null)
            action('重试注册推送', push.register),
          heading('${flow.character.name} 和你的日常', '继续聊天，或看看探索带回了什么。'),
          if (flow.discoveryCount < 2) const LinearProgressIndicator(),
          Text(
            flow.discoveryCount < 2
                ? '模拟探索中，可继续聊天或离开。约 5 秒首条发现，10 秒完整结果。'
                : '模拟探索已完成',
          ),
          ...List.generate(
            flow.discoveryCount,
            (i) => Card(
              child: ListTile(
                title: Text(flow.discoveryTitle(i)),
                subtitle: Text(flow.isRead(i) ? '已读' : '新发现 · 未读'),
                onTap: () => detail(i),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          if (flow.discoveryCount == 2) ...[
            const Text('下次计划：找一个不同视角的朋友交换推荐。（模拟计划，未自动执行）'),
            const Text('AI 社交：认识了一位新朋友；完整聊天查看入口待后续版本。'),
          ],
          const Divider(),
          chat(),
        ];
    }
  }
  bool get _showOnboarding {
    if (!flow.ready || widget.registrationCompleted) return false;
    if (widget.previewWelcomeOnStart) return !_welcomePreviewDismissed;
    return flow.step == ExperienceStep.welcome ||
        flow.step == ExperienceStep.world;
  }
  Future<bool> _advanceOnboarding(String? nextPageId) async {
    if (flow.busy) return false;
    if (flow.step == ExperienceStep.welcome ||
        flow.step == ExperienceStep.world) {
      await flow.advanceOnboarding(nextPageId);
      return flow.error == null;
    }
    // Design review must not overwrite an existing user's mock experience.
    return true;
  }
  bool get _showHome =>
      flow.ready &&
      !_showFlowTools &&
      (widget.registrationCompleted ||
          flow.step == ExperienceStep.home ||
          (widget.previewWelcomeOnStart && _welcomePreviewDismissed));
  void _notice(String message) {
    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
        .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(message)));
  }
  Future<void> _resetAsNewUser() async {
    final confirmed = await showDialog<bool>(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('重置为新用户（测试）？'),
        content: const Text('清除本地登录、引导进度和模拟聊天。下次登录后重新走完整新用户流程；服务器账号及数据不会删除。'),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
            child: const Text('取消'),
          ),
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
            child: const Text('重置'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
    if (confirmed != true || !mounted) return;
    try {
      await flow.reset();
      if (flow.error != null) throw StateError(flow.error!);
      await account.resetForNewUserTest();
      await const StartupProfileStore().clear();
      await const NotificationPromptStore().clear();
      if (!mounted) return;
      Navigator.of(context).pushAndRemoveUntil(
        MaterialPageRoute<void>(builder: (_) => AuthGate(service: account)),
        (_) => false,
      );
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) _notice('重置失败，请重试');
    }
  }
  Future<void> _homeMenu() async {
    await showModalBottomSheet<void>(
      context: context,
      useSafeArea: true,
      builder: (sheetContext) => SafeArea(
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            if (AppEnv.enableTestTools)
              ListTile(
                leading: const Icon(Icons.restart_alt),
                title: const Text('重置为新用户（测试）'),
                onTap: () {
                  Navigator.pop(sheetContext);
                  _resetAsNewUser();
                },
              ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.explore_outlined),
              title: const Text('创作广场'),
              onTap: () {
                Navigator.pop(sheetContext);
                Navigator.of(context).push(
                  MaterialPageRoute<void>(
                    builder: (_) => PlazaPage(
                      onChat: _homeChat,
                      onVoice: () => _homeChat(record: true),
                    ),
                  ),
                );
              },
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.tune),
              title: const Text('流程验证'),
              onTap: () {
                Navigator.pop(sheetContext);
                setState(() => _showFlowTools = true);
              },
            ),
            ListTile(
              leading: const Icon(Icons.person_outline),
              title: const Text('账号联调'),
              onTap: () {
                Navigator.pop(sheetContext);
                Navigator.of(context).push(
                  MaterialPageRoute<void>(
                    builder: (_) => LoginDemoPage(service: account),
                  ),
                );
              },
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
  Future<void> _homeChat({bool record = false}) async {
    final explore = await Navigator.of(context).push<bool>(
      MaterialPageRoute<bool>(
        builder: (chatContext) => widget.registrationCompleted
            ? LiveChatPage(
                accountService: widget.accountService ?? account,
                initialDraft: input.text,
                startRecordingOnOpen: record,
                onExit: () => Navigator.pop(chatContext, false),
                onExplore: () => Navigator.pop(chatContext, true),
              )
            : ListenableBuilder(
                listenable: flow,
                builder: (_, _) => ChatPage(
                  name: flow.character.name,
                  returningUser: true,
                  messages: flow.chatMessages,
                  busy: flow.busy,
                  error: flow.error,
                  initialDraft: input.text,
                  startRecordingOnOpen: record,
                  onSend: (text) async {
                    await flow.send(text);
                    if (flow.error == null) input.clear();
                    return flow.error == null;
                  },
                  onVoiceRecorded: flow.saveVoice,
                  onExit: () => Navigator.pop(chatContext, false),
                  onExplore: () => Navigator.pop(chatContext, true),
                ),
              ),
      ),
    );
    if (explore == true && mounted) await _explore();
  }
  bool _exploreOpening = false;
  Future<void> _explore() async {
    if (_exploreOpening) return;
    _exploreOpening = true;
    try {
      var selected = await widget.profileStore.hasSelectedInterests(
        widget.userId,
      );
      if (!mounted) return;
      if (!selected) {
        selected =
            await Navigator.of(context).push<bool>(
              MaterialPageRoute(
                builder: (routeContext) => InterestsPage(
                  onStart: (values) async {
                    await flow.saveInterests(values);
                    if (flow.error != null) return false;
                    await widget.profileStore.saveSelectedInterests(
                      widget.userId,
                      values,
                    );
                    if (routeContext.mounted) Navigator.pop(routeContext, true);
                    return true;
                  },
                ),
              ),
            ) ==
            true;
      }
      if (selected && mounted) {
        await Navigator.of(context).push(
          MaterialPageRoute<void>(
            builder: (_) => PlazaPage(
              onChat: _homeChat,
              onVoice: () => _homeChat(record: true),
            ),
          ),
        );
      }
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) _notice('无法读取兴趣选择，请重试');
    } finally {
      _exploreOpening = false;
    }
  }
  Future<void> _homeDiscovery(int index) async {
    if (flow.discoveryCount > index) {
      await detail(index);
      if (mounted && input.text.isNotEmpty) await _homeChat();
      return;
    }
    await Navigator.of(context).push(
      MaterialPageRoute<void>(
        builder: (_) => PlazaPage(
          initialDiscovery: index,
          onChat: _homeChat,
          onVoice: () => _homeChat(record: true),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) =>
      widget.registrationCompleted && !widget.returningUser
      ? !flow.ready
            ? const Scaffold(body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()))
            : LiveChatPage(
                accountService: widget.accountService ?? account,
                returningUser: false,
                error: _registrationError,
                busy: _finishingRegistration,
                onExit: _finishRegistrationChat,
                onExplore: () => _finishRegistrationChat(explore: true),
              )
      : _showOnboarding
      ? OnboardingFlow(
          initialPageId: widget.previewWelcomeOnStart
              ? null
              : flow.onboardingPageId,
          busy: flow.busy,
          error: flow.error,
          onAdvance: _advanceOnboarding,
          onComplete: () => setState(() => _welcomePreviewDismissed = true),
        )
      : _showHome
      ? HomeNotificationPrompt(
          push: push,
          child: HomePage(
            onMenu: _homeMenu,
            onFriends: () => _notice('好友功能尚未开放'),
            onExplore: _explore,
            onChat: _homeChat,
            onVoice: () => _homeChat(record: true),
            onDiscovery: _homeDiscovery,
            discoveries: flow.discoveryCount == 0
                ? HomePage.designDiscoveries
                : [
                    for (var i = 0; i < flow.discoveryCount; i++)
                      HomeDiscovery(
                        description: flow.isRead(i) ? '已读发现' : '探索带回了新发现：',
                        title: flow.discoveryTitle(i),
                        style: i == 0
                            ? DiscoveryStyle.blue
                            : DiscoveryStyle.purple,
                      ),
                  ],
          ),
        )
      : Scaffold(
          appBar: AppBar(
            title: const Text('ViiTor · 流程验证'),
            leading: _showFlowTools
                ? IconButton(
                    tooltip: '返回首页',
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.arrow_back),
                    onPressed: () => setState(() => _showFlowTools = false),
                  )
                : null,
            actions: [
              IconButton(
                tooltip: '启动页预览',
                icon: const Icon(Icons.palette_outlined),
                onPressed: () => Navigator.pushNamed(context, '/splash'),
              ),
              IconButton(
                tooltip: '角色待机预览',
                icon: const Icon(Icons.auto_awesome),
                onPressed: () =>
                    Navigator.pushNamed(context, '/character-preview'),
              ),
              PopupMenuButton<String>(
                onSelected: (value) async {
                  if (value == 'demoPush') {
                    final scheduled = await push.scheduleDemo();
                    if (!context.mounted) return;
                    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                      SnackBar(
                        content: Text(
                          scheduled
                              ? '已安排本地通知。现在回到桌面，约 5 秒后点击通知。'
                              : push.error ?? '仅支持 iOS 调试版。',
                        ),
                      ),
                    );
                  } else if (value == 'copyPushToken') {
                    if (!kDebugMode) return;
                    await push.refresh();
                    String message;
                    final token = push.token;
                    if (!push.supported) {
                      message = 'APNs 测试仅支持 iOS。';
                    } else if (token != null && token.isNotEmpty) {
                      try {
                        await Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: token));
                        message = '已复制 APNs token，可粘贴到苹果推送控制台。';
                      } catch (_) {
                        message = '复制失败，请重试。';
                      }
                    } else {
                      if (push.allowed) {
                        await push.register();
                      } else {
                        await push.request();
                      }
                      message =
                          push.error ??
                          (push.allowed
                              ? '已请求 APNs 注册，请稍等后再次点击复制。'
                              : '请在系统设置中允许通知后重试。');
                    }
                    if (!context.mounted) return;
                    ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
                        .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(message)));
                  } else if (value == 'auth') {
                    await Navigator.of(context).push(
                      MaterialPageRoute<void>(
                        builder: (_) => LoginDemoPage(service: account),
                      ),
                    );
                  } else {
                    final confirmed = await showDialog<bool>(
                      context: context,
                      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
                        title: const Text('重置模拟体验？'),
                        content: const Text('仅清除本地模拟进度和聊天，不影响真实登录凭证。'),
                        actions: [
                          TextButton(
                            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
                            child: const Text('取消'),
                          ),
                          TextButton(
                            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
                            child: const Text('重置'),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    );
                    if (confirmed == true) {
                      input.clear();
                      await flow.reset();
                    }
                  }
                },
                itemBuilder: (_) => const [
                  if (kDebugMode)
                    PopupMenuItem(
                      value: 'demoPush',
                      child: Text('5 秒后发送本地测试通知'),
                    ),
                  if (kDebugMode)
                    PopupMenuItem(
                      value: 'copyPushToken',
                      child: Text('复制 APNs token'),
                    ),
                  PopupMenuItem(value: 'auth', child: Text('账号联调页')),
                  PopupMenuItem(value: 'reset', child: Text('重置模拟体验')),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
          body: SafeArea(
            child: ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              children: [
                FilledButton.icon(
                  onPressed: () =>
                      Navigator.pushNamed(context, '/welcome-preview'),
                  icon: const Icon(Icons.waving_hand_outlined),
                  label: const Text('引导页预览'),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                const Text(
                  '模拟环境 · 占位 UI · 与真实账号隔离',
                  style: TextStyle(color: Colors.deepOrange),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 20),
                if (flow.error != null)
                  Text(flow.error!, style: const TextStyle(color: Colors.red)),
                if (!flow.ready) action('加载 / 重试', flow.load) else ...content(),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
}
import 'dart:convert';
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';
abstract interface class ExperienceStore {
  Future<Map<String, dynamic>?> read();
  Future<void> write(Map<String, dynamic> value);
}
/// 独立的本地模拟体验，不与真实账号绑定。
class LocalExperienceStore implements ExperienceStore {
  final _storage = const FlutterSecureStorage();
  static const _key = 'viitor.mock-experience.v1';
  @override
  Future<Map<String, dynamic>?> read() async {
    final raw = await _storage.read(key: _key);
    if (raw == null) return null;
    return jsonDecode(raw) as Map<String, dynamic>;
  }
  @override
  Future<void> write(Map<String, dynamic> value) =>
      _storage.write(key: _key, value: jsonEncode(value));
}
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
/// Code-drawn glow and hint with a gentle continuous animation.
/// This layer never moves or intercepts the actual chat/voice controls.
class HomeChatHint extends StatefulWidget {
  const HomeChatHint({super.key});
  @override
  State<HomeChatHint> createState() => _HomeChatHintState();
}
class _HomeChatHintState extends State<HomeChatHint>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late final _motion = AnimationController(
    vsync: this,
    duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 2600),
  );
  @override
  void didChangeDependencies() {
    super.didChangeDependencies();
    if (MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)) {
      _motion.stop();
      _motion.value = 0;
    } else if (!_motion.isAnimating) {
      _motion.repeat();
    }
  }
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _motion.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => IgnorePointer(
    child: ExcludeSemantics(
      child: RepaintBoundary(
        child: AnimatedBuilder(
          animation: _motion,
          builder: (context, _) {
            final phase = _motion.value * math.pi * 2;
            final breath = (1 - math.cos(phase)) / 2;
            return SizedBox(
              width: 183,
              height: 180,
              child: Stack(
                clipBehavior: Clip.none,
                children: [
                  Positioned(
                    left: 40,
                    top: 57.5,
                    child: Transform.scale(
                      key: const ValueKey('home-chat-ring'),
                      scale: 1 + .12 * breath,
                      child: Opacity(
                        opacity: .9 + .1 * breath,
                        child: CustomPaint(
                          size: const Size(122, 122),
                          painter: _RingPainter(progress: _motion.value),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  Positioned(
                    left: 51,
                    top: 68.5,
                    child: Transform.scale(
                      scale: 1 + .06 * breath,
                      child: CustomPaint(
                        size: const Size(100, 100),
                        painter: _RingPainter(
                          progress: _motion.value,
                          inner: true,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  Positioned(
                    left: 0,
                    top: 0,
                    child: Transform.translate(
                      key: const ValueKey('home-chat-hint-motion'),
                      offset: Offset(0, -5 * math.sin(phase)),
                      child: SizedBox(
                        width: 150,
                        height: 80,
                        child: Stack(
                          children: [
                            Positioned(
                              left: 13,
                              top: 3,
                              child: const CustomPaint(
                                size: Size(127, 44),
                                painter: _BubblePainter(),
                              ),
                            ),
                            Positioned(
                              left: 0,
                              top: 0,
                              child: Opacity(
                                opacity: .55 + .45 * breath,
                                child: const CustomPaint(
                                  size: Size(12, 12),
                                  painter: _StarPainter(),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                            Positioned(
                              left: 96.5,
                              top: 44,
                              child: CustomPaint(
                                size: const Size(9, 31),
                                painter: _ThreadPainter(glow: breath),
                              ),
                            ),
                            const Positioned(
                              left: 13,
                              top: 12,
                              width: 127,
                              child: Text(
                                '快跟我聊聊吧',
                                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                                textScaler: TextScaler.noScaling,
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
                                  fontSize: 14,
                                  height: 20 / 14,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
                                  letterSpacing: .14,
                                  color: Colors.white,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
}
class _RingPainter extends CustomPainter {
  const _RingPainter({required this.progress, this.inner = false});
  final double progress;
  final bool inner;
  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final center = size.center(Offset.zero);
    final radius = inner ? 29.0 : 39.0;
    final bounds = Rect.fromCircle(center: center, radius: radius);
    final breath = (1 - math.cos(progress * math.pi * 2)) / 2;
    final glow = Paint()
      ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
      ..strokeWidth = inner ? 5 : 9
      ..color = const Color(0xFFD59AFF).withValues(alpha: .55 + .25 * breath)
      ..maskFilter = MaskFilter.blur(BlurStyle.normal, inner ? 4 : 7);
    canvas.drawCircle(center, radius, glow);
    canvas.drawCircle(
      center,
      radius,
      Paint()
        ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
        ..strokeWidth = inner ? 1.8 : 2.4
        ..shader = SweepGradient(
          transform: GradientRotation(progress * math.pi * 2),
          colors: const [
            Color(0x99CA9AFF),
            Color(0xFFFFF5FF),
            Color(0xFFE0B8FF),
            Color(0x99CA9AFF),
          ],
          stops: const [0, .35, .6, 1],
        ).createShader(bounds),
    );
  }
  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(_RingPainter oldDelegate) =>
      oldDelegate.progress != progress || oldDelegate.inner != inner;
}
class _BubblePainter extends CustomPainter {
  const _BubblePainter();
  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final path = Path()
      ..moveTo(19.5, .5)
      ..lineTo(107.5, .5)
      ..arcToPoint(const Offset(107.5, 38.5), radius: const Radius.circular(19))
      ..lineTo(96.5, 38.5)
      ..quadraticBezierTo(95.5, 38.5, 95, 39.5)
      ..lineTo(93, 42)
      ..quadraticBezierTo(91.5, 44, 90, 42)
      ..lineTo(88, 39.5)
      ..quadraticBezierTo(87, 38.5, 85, 38.5)
      ..lineTo(19.5, 38.5)
      ..arcToPoint(const Offset(19.5, .5), radius: const Radius.circular(19))
      ..close();
    canvas.drawPath(
      path,
      Paint()
        ..shader = const LinearGradient(
          begin: Alignment.topLeft,
          end: Alignment.bottomRight,
          colors: [Color(0x666E358B), Color(0x337E3BB0)],
        ).createShader(Offset.zero & size),
    );
    canvas.drawPath(
      path,
      Paint()
        ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
        ..strokeWidth = 1
        ..color = const Color(0xFFD1AAFE),
    );
  }
  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(_BubblePainter oldDelegate) => false;
}
class _ThreadPainter extends CustomPainter {
  const _ThreadPainter({required this.glow});
  final double glow;
  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    canvas.drawLine(
      const Offset(4.5, 0),
      const Offset(4.5, 26.5),
      Paint()
        ..strokeWidth = 1
        ..shader = const LinearGradient(
          begin: Alignment.topCenter,
          end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
          colors: [Color(0x00BA7AFF), Color(0xFFEFDAFF)],
        ).createShader(Offset.zero & size),
    );
    canvas.drawCircle(
      const Offset(4.5, 26.5),
      3.5 + glow,
      Paint()
        ..color = const Color(0x99D694F9)
        ..maskFilter = const MaskFilter.blur(BlurStyle.normal, 2),
    );
    canvas.drawCircle(
      const Offset(4.5, 26.5),
      2,
      Paint()..color = const Color(0xFFF6E4FF),
    );
  }
  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(_ThreadPainter oldDelegate) => oldDelegate.glow != glow;
}
class _StarPainter extends CustomPainter {
  const _StarPainter();
  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    for (final (center, radius) in [
      (const Offset(5, 5), 5.0),
      (const Offset(10, 10), 2.0),
    ]) {
      final path = Path()..moveTo(center.dx, center.dy - radius);
      for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        final angle = -math.pi / 2 + i * math.pi / 2;
        final next = angle + math.pi / 2;
        path.quadraticBezierTo(
          center.dx + math.cos(angle + math.pi / 4) * radius * .3,
          center.dy + math.sin(angle + math.pi / 4) * radius * .3,
          center.dx + math.cos(next) * radius,
          center.dy + math.sin(next) * radius,
        );
      }
      canvas.drawPath(path..close(), Paint()..color = const Color(0xFFD1AAFE));
    }
  }
  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(_StarPainter oldDelegate) => false;
}
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'dart:ui';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_svg/flutter_svg.dart';
import '../shared/design_scene.dart';
import 'home_chat_hint.dart';
enum DiscoveryStyle { blue, purple, glass }
class HomeDiscovery {
  const HomeDiscovery({
    required this.description,
    required this.title,
    this.style = DiscoveryStyle.blue,
  });
  final String description, title;
  final DiscoveryStyle style;
}
/// 11.12 Figma homepage, 271:1403; the glass variant is 288:2019.
class HomePage extends StatefulWidget {
  const HomePage({
    super.key,
    required this.onMenu,
    required this.onExplore,
    required this.onChat,
    required this.onVoice,
    required this.onDiscovery,
    this.onFriends,
    this.discoveries = designDiscoveries,
    this.initialPage = 0,
  });
  final VoidCallback onMenu, onExplore, onChat, onVoice;
  final VoidCallback? onFriends;
  final ValueChanged<int> onDiscovery;
  final List<HomeDiscovery> discoveries;
  final int initialPage;
  static const designDiscoveries = [
    HomeDiscovery(description: '广场上出现了新的创作挑战:', title: '“用AI创作一段属于秋天的故”'),
    HomeDiscovery(
      description: '广场上出现了新的创作挑战:',
      title: '“用AI创作一段属于秋天的故”',
      style: DiscoveryStyle.purple,
    ),
    HomeDiscovery(
      description: '广场上出现了新的创作挑战:',
      title: '“用AI创作一段属于秋天的故”',
      style: DiscoveryStyle.glass,
    ),
  ];
  @override
  State<HomePage> createState() => _HomePageState();
}
class _HomePageState extends State<HomePage> {
  late final PageController _pages = PageController(
    initialPage: widget.initialPage,
    viewportFraction: 327 / 375,
  );
  late int _selected = widget.initialPage;
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _pages.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }
  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant HomePage oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    if (_selected >= widget.discoveries.length &&
        widget.discoveries.isNotEmpty) {
      _selected = widget.discoveries.length - 1;
      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
        if (mounted && _pages.hasClients) _pages.jumpToPage(_selected);
      });
    }
  }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => AnnotatedRegion<SystemUiOverlayStyle>(
    value: SystemUiOverlayStyle.light.copyWith(
      statusBarColor: Colors.transparent,
      systemNavigationBarColor: const Color(0xFF18191E),
    ),
    child: Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF18191E),
      body: LayoutBuilder(
        builder: (context, constraints) {
          final safe = MediaQuery.paddingOf(context);
          final contentWidth = math.min(
            constraints.maxWidth - safe.horizontal,
            600.0,
          );
          final sceneScale = math.max(
            contentWidth / 375,
            constraints.maxHeight / 812,
          );
          final cardWidth = contentWidth * 327 / 375 - 12;
          final cardHeight = widget.discoveries.fold<double>(
            185,
            (height, item) => math.max(
              height,
              _DiscoveryCard.heightFor(context, item, cardWidth),
            ),
          );
          final glass =
              widget.discoveries.isNotEmpty &&
              widget
                      .discoveries[_selected.clamp(
                        0,
                        widget.discoveries.length - 1,
                      )]
                      .style ==
                  DiscoveryStyle.glass;
          final cardTop = safe.top + (glass ? 62 : 70);
          final indicatorTop = safe.top + 70 + cardHeight + 14;
          final sceneOffset = math.max(
            0.0,
            indicatorTop + 30 - 360 * sceneScale,
          );
          final footerBottom = math.max(24.0, safe.bottom) + 6;
          // Decorative artwork covers the viewport; its scaled height must
          // not create scroll extent on shorter phones. Only actual content
          // (for example accessibility-sized cards) may require scrolling.
          final canvasHeight = math.max(
            constraints.maxHeight,
            indicatorTop + 26 + 16 + 48 + footerBottom,
          );
          return Stack(
            children: [
              Positioned.fill(
                child: SingleChildScrollView(
                  key: const ValueKey('home-content'),
                  child: Center(
                    child: SizedBox(
                      width: contentWidth,
                      height: canvasHeight,
                      child: ClipRect(
                        child: Stack(
                          children: [
                            Positioned(
                              left: (contentWidth - 375 * sceneScale) / 2,
                              top: sceneOffset,
                              width: 375 * sceneScale,
                              height: 812 * sceneScale,
                              child: const DesignScene(
                                asset: 'assets/design/home_raw.png',
                                sourceRect: Rect.fromLTWH(
                                  -25.04,
                                  -88,
                                  413.08,
                                  926.55,
                                ),
                                semanticLabel: 'ViiTor 在星空露台向你挥手',
                              ),
                            ),
                            Positioned(
                              left: 0,
                              right: 0,
                              bottom: 0,
                              height: 237 * sceneScale,
                              child: const IgnorePointer(
                                child: DecoratedBox(
                                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                    gradient: LinearGradient(
                                      begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                                      end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                                      colors: [
                                        Colors.transparent,
                                        Color(0x66000000),
                                        Color(0x85000000),
                                      ],
                                      stops: [0, 0.88984, 1],
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                            Positioned(
                              left: 0,
                              right: 0,
                              top: cardTop,
                              height: cardHeight,
                              child: PageView.builder(
                                key: const ValueKey('home-discoveries'),
                                controller: _pages,
                                itemCount: widget.discoveries.length,
                                onPageChanged: (value) =>
                                    setState(() => _selected = value),
                                itemBuilder: (context, index) => Padding(
                                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(
                                    left: 5,
                                    right: 7,
                                  ),
                                  child: AnimatedBuilder(
                                    animation: _pages,
                                    builder: (context, child) {
                                      final page =
                                          _pages.hasClients &&
                                              _pages
                                                  .position
                                                  .hasContentDimensions
                                          ? (_pages.page ??
                                                _selected.toDouble())
                                          : _selected.toDouble();
                                      final distance = (page - index)
                                          .abs()
                                          .clamp(0.0, 1.0);
                                      return Transform.translate(
                                        offset: Offset(0, 18 * distance),
                                        child: Transform.scale(
                                          scale: 1 - 0.152 * distance,
                                          alignment: Alignment.topLeft,
                                          child: _DiscoveryCard(
                                            discovery:
                                                widget.discoveries[index],
                                            outlined: distance > 0.5,
                                            onPressed: () =>
                                                widget.onDiscovery(index),
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      );
                                    },
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                            Positioned(
                              left: 0,
                              right: 0,
                              top: indicatorTop - 16,
                              height: 42,
                              child: Row(
                                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                                children: [
                                  for (
                                    var i = 0;
                                    i < widget.discoveries.length;
                                    i++
                                  )
                                    Semantics(
                                      label: '发现第 ${i + 1} 页',
                                      selected: i == _selected,
                                      button: true,
                                      child: GestureDetector(
                                        behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                                        onTap: () => _pages.animateToPage(
                                          i,
                                          duration:
                                              MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(
                                                context,
                                              )
                                              ? Duration.zero
                                              : const Duration(
                                                  milliseconds: 250,
                                                ),
                                          curve: Curves.easeOut,
                                        ),
                                        child: SizedBox(
                                          width: 20,
                                          height: 42,
                                          child: Center(
                                            child: Container(
                                              width: 10,
                                              height: 10,
                                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                                color: i == _selected
                                                    ? const Color(0xFFE5DFFF)
                                                    : null,
                                                border: i == _selected
                                                    ? null
                                                    : Border.all(
                                                        color: const Color(
                                                          0xFF9990C3,
                                                        ),
                                                        width: 0.7,
                                                      ),
                                              ),
                                            ),
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              Positioned(
                left: safe.left + 7,
                top: safe.top + 3,
                child: SizedBox(
                  width: 48,
                  height: 48,
                  child: IconButton(
                    tooltip: '菜单',
                    onPressed: widget.onMenu,
                    padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                    icon: _svg('menu', 22.9583, 20.5417),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              Positioned(
                left: safe.left + 16,
                right: safe.right + 16,
                bottom: footerBottom,
                child: Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                  children: [
                    Transform.translate(
                      offset: const Offset(-16, 0),
                      child: Tooltip(
                        message: '逛一逛',
                        child: Semantics(
                          button: true,
                          label: '逛一逛',
                          child: GestureDetector(
                            behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                            onTap: widget.onExplore,
                            child: SizedBox(
                              width: 130,
                              height: 45,
                              child: Center(
                                child: ClipRect(
                                  child: SizedBox(
                                    width: 130,
                                    height: 40,
                                    child: Stack(
                                      children: [
                                        Positioned(
                                          left: -107,
                                          top: 0,
                                          child: SvgPicture.asset(
                                            'assets/home/explore.svg',
                                            width: 237,
                                            height: 40,
                                            excludeFromSemantics: true,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      ],
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    _GlassPill(
                      width: 116,
                      height: 45,
                      opacity: 0.15,
                      child: Row(
                        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                        children: [
                          _ToolbarAction(
                            label: '聊天',
                            asset: 'chat',
                            onPressed: widget.onChat,
                          ),
                          _ToolbarAction(
                            label: '语音',
                            asset: 'microphone',
                            onPressed: widget.onVoice,
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              Positioned(
                right: safe.right + 16,
                bottom: footerBottom - 39,
                width: 183,
                height: 180,
                child: const HomeChatHint(),
              ),
            ],
          );
        },
      ),
    ),
  );
}
Widget _svg(String name, double width, double height) => SvgPicture.asset(
  'assets/home/$name.svg',
  width: width,
  height: height,
  excludeFromSemantics: true,
);
class _DiscoveryCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _DiscoveryCard({
    required this.discovery,
    required this.onPressed,
    this.outlined = false,
  });
  final bool outlined;
  final HomeDiscovery discovery;
  final VoidCallback onPressed;
  static const descriptionStyle = TextStyle(
    fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
    fontSize: 16,
    height: 31 / 16,
    fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
    letterSpacing: 0,
    color: Colors.white,
  );
  static const titleStyle = TextStyle(
    fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
    fontSize: 18,
    height: 31 / 18,
    fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
    letterSpacing: 0,
    color: Colors.white,
  );
  static double heightFor(
    BuildContext context,
    HomeDiscovery item,
    double width,
  ) {
    double measure(String text, TextStyle style) {
      final p = TextPainter(
        text: TextSpan(text: text, style: style),
        textDirection: Directionality.of(context),
        textScaler: MediaQuery.textScalerOf(context),
      )..layout(maxWidth: math.max(1, width - 78));
      final h = p.height;
      p.dispose();
      return h;
    }
    final scaler = MediaQuery.textScalerOf(context);
    return math.max(
      185,
      81 +
          math.max(0, scaler.scale(16) - 16) * 17 / 16 +
          math.max(42, scaler.scale(14) * 1.25 + 16) +
          measure(item.description, descriptionStyle) +
          measure(item.title, titleStyle),
    );
  }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final blue = discovery.style == DiscoveryStyle.blue;
    final purple = discovery.style == DiscoveryStyle.purple;
    final glass = discovery.style == DiscoveryStyle.glass;
    return Stack(
      fit: StackFit.expand,
      children: [
        if (outlined)
          SvgPicture.asset(
            'assets/home/secondary_background.svg',
            fit: BoxFit.fill,
            excludeFromSemantics: true,
          )
        else if (blue)
          SvgPicture.asset(
            'assets/design/card_blue.svg',
            fit: BoxFit.fill,
            excludeFromSemantics: true,
          )
        else if (glass)
          Image.asset(
            'assets/design/card_glass.png',
            fit: BoxFit.fill,
            excludeFromSemantics: true,
          )
        else
          DecoratedBox(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
              color: purple ? const Color(0xFF1B1336) : null,
              gradient: purple
                  ? null
                  : const LinearGradient(
                      begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                      end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                      colors: [Color(0xFF304960), Color(0xFF2A222D)],
                    ),
              border: Border.all(color: const Color(0x38FFFFFF)),
            ),
          ),
        if (purple && !outlined)
          Positioned(
            top: 0,
            right: 0,
            width: 315,
            height: 185,
            child: ClipRRect(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24),
              child: ImageFiltered(
                imageFilter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 2.75, sigmaY: 2.75),
                child: SvgPicture.asset(
                  'assets/design/card_mask.svg',
                  excludeFromSemantics: true,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        if (purple && !outlined) const Positioned.fill(child: _CardStars()),
        if (blue && !outlined)
          const Positioned(
            left: 12,
            right: 12,
            bottom: 26,
            height: 20,
            child: _CardDiamonds(),
          ),
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(11, 7, 11, 18),
          child: Column(
            children: [
              Align(
                alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                child: glass
                    ? Semantics(
                        label: '今日发现',
                        child: Image.asset(
                          'assets/design/card_glass_heading.png',
                          width: 146,
                          height: 28,
                          excludeFromSemantics: true,
                        ),
                      )
                    : const _DiscoveryHeading(),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 10.716),
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 28),
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      discovery.description,
                      textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                      style: glass
                          ? descriptionStyle.copyWith(
                              color: const Color(0xFF090909),
                            )
                          : descriptionStyle,
                    ),
                    Text(
                      discovery.title,
                      textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                      style: glass
                          ? titleStyle.copyWith(color: const Color(0xFF090909))
                          : titleStyle,
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const Spacer(),
              SizedBox(
                width: 165,
                height: math.max(
                  42,
                  MediaQuery.textScalerOf(context).scale(14) * 1.25 + 16,
                ),
                child: TextButton(
                  onPressed: onPressed,
                  style: TextButton.styleFrom(
                    overlayColor: Colors.transparent,
                    backgroundColor: Colors.white,
                    foregroundColor: const Color(0xFF111111),
                    shape: const StadiumBorder(),
                    padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                  ),
                  child: const Text(
                    '去看看',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
                      fontSize: 14,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _GlassPill extends StatelessWidget {
  const _GlassPill({
    required this.width,
    required this.height,
    required this.opacity,
    required this.child,
  });
  final double width, height, opacity;
  final Widget child;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => SizedBox(
    width: width,
    height: math.max(48, height),
    child: Center(
      child: ClipRRect(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(188),
        child: BackdropFilter(
          filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 2.35, sigmaY: 2.35),
          child: Container(
            width: width,
            height: height,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: opacity),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(188),
              border: Border.all(color: Colors.white.withValues(alpha: 0.37)),
            ),
            child: Center(child: child),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
}
class _ToolbarAction extends StatelessWidget {

    required this.label,
    required this.asset,
    required this.onPressed,
  });

  final VoidCallback onPressed;
