  Widget build(BuildContext context) => SizedBox(
    width: 45,
    height: 45,
    child: IconButton(
      tooltip: label,
      onPressed: onPressed,
      padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
      constraints: const BoxConstraints(minWidth: 45, minHeight: 45),
      icon: _svg(asset, 24, 24),
    ),
  );
}
import 'dart:ui';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';
import 'package:flutter_svg/flutter_svg.dart';
import 'push_service.dart';
class NotificationPromptStore {
  const NotificationPromptStore();
  static const _key = 'bots.notification-prompt.v1';
  static const _storage = FlutterSecureStorage();
  Future<bool> wasShown() async => await _storage.read(key: _key) == 'true';
  Future<void> markShown() => _storage.write(key: _key, value: 'true');
  Future<void> clear() => _storage.delete(key: _key);
}
/// Only mounted on the home screen; never requests OS permission automatically.
class HomeNotificationPrompt extends StatefulWidget {
  const HomeNotificationPrompt({
    super.key,
    required this.push,
    required this.child,
    this.store = const NotificationPromptStore(),
  });
  final PushService push;
  final NotificationPromptStore store;
  final Widget child;
  @override
  State<HomeNotificationPrompt> createState() => _HomeNotificationPromptState();
}
class _HomeNotificationPromptState extends State<HomeNotificationPrompt> {
  bool _checking = false, _done = false;
  bool get _visible =>
      mounted &&
      (ModalRoute.of(context)?.isCurrent ?? true) &&
      (WidgetsBinding.instance.lifecycleState == null ||
          WidgetsBinding.instance.lifecycleState == AppLifecycleState.resumed);
  Future<void> _check() async {
    if (_checking || _done || !_visible || !widget.push.supported) return;
    _checking = true;
    try {
      if (await widget.store.wasShown()) {
        _done = true;
        return;
      }
      if (!_visible) return;
      await widget.push.refresh();
      if (!_visible || widget.push.error != null) return;
      if (widget.push.allowed) {
        _done = true;
        return;
      }
      await widget.store.markShown();
      if (!_visible) {
        await widget.store.clear();
        return;
      }
      _done = true;
      if (!mounted) return;
      final allow = await showDialog<bool>(
        context: context,
        barrierDismissible: false,
        barrierColor: const Color(0xCC000000),
        builder: (_) => const NotificationPermissionDialog(),
      );
      if (!mounted || allow != true) return;
      // Refresh after the choice, in case permission changed in Settings.
      await widget.push.refresh();
      if (!mounted) return;
      if (widget.push.allowed) return;
      if (widget.push.error == null) {
        if (widget.push.authorization == 'denied') {
          await widget.push.openSettings();
        } else {
          await widget.push.request();
        }
      }
      if (mounted && widget.push.error != null) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
            .showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text(widget.push.error!)));
      }
    } catch (_) {
      // A failed status/storage read must not block home or consume the prompt.
    } finally {
      _checking = false;
    }
  }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      if (mounted) _check();
    });
    return widget.child;
  }
}
class NotificationPermissionDialog extends StatelessWidget {
  const NotificationPermissionDialog({super.key});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Dialog(
    backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
    elevation: 0,
    insetPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 26, vertical: 24),
    child: ConstrainedBox(

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
/// APNs 设备状态；尚未提供后端登记协议，不会自动上传 token。
class PushService extends ChangeNotifier {
  PushService({MethodChannel? channel})
    : channel = channel ?? const MethodChannel('viitor/apns');
  final MethodChannel channel;
  String authorization = 'notDetermined';
  String? token;
  Map<String, String>? pendingNotification;
  bool _disposed = false;
  @override
  void notifyListeners() {
    if (!_disposed) super.notifyListeners();
  }
  Future<void> readNotification() async {
    if (!supported) return;
    try {
      final value = await channel.invokeMapMethod<String, String>(
        'takeNotification',
      );
      if (value != null) pendingNotification = value;
      notifyListeners();
    } catch (_) {
      /* Routing remains pending until backend integration. */
    }
  }
  Future<bool> scheduleDemo() async {
    if (!kDebugMode || !supported || busy) return false;
    busy = true;
    error = null;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      await channel.invokeMethod<void>('scheduleDemo');
      return true;
    } on PlatformException catch (e) {
      error = e.message ?? '本地通知安排失败，请重试。';
      return false;
    } on MissingPluginException {
      error = '本地通知原生方法未启用，请检查 Runner 的 Debug 编译配置。';
      return false;
    } catch (_) {
      error = '本地通知调用失败，请重试。';
      return false;
    } finally {
      busy = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }
  String? error;
  bool busy = false;
  bool get supported => !kIsWeb && defaultTargetPlatform == TargetPlatform.iOS;
  bool get allowed =>
      ['authorized', 'provisional', 'ephemeral'].contains(authorization);
  Future<void> initialize() async {
    channel.setMethodCallHandler((call) async {
      if (call.method == 'registrationChanged') await refresh();
      if (call.method == 'notificationOpened') await readNotification();
    });
    await refresh();
    await readNotification();
    if (supported && allowed) await register();
  }
  Future<void> refresh() async {
    if (!supported) return;
    await _read('status');
  }
  Future<void> _read(String method) async {
    try {
      final value = await channel.invokeMapMethod<String, dynamic>(method);
      authorization = value?['authorization'] as String? ?? 'notDetermined';
      final raw = value?['token'] as String?;
      token = raw == null || raw.isEmpty ? null : raw;
      final failure = value?['registrationError'] as String?;
      error = failure == null || failure.isEmpty ? null : failure;
    } catch (_) {
      error = '无法读取通知状态，请重试。';
    }
    notifyListeners();
  }
  Future<void> request() async {
    if (busy || !supported) return;
    busy = true;
    notifyListeners();
    await _read('requestPermission');
    busy = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }
  Future<void> register() async {
    if (!supported || !allowed) return;
    try {
      await channel.invokeMethod<void>('register');
    } catch (_) {
      error = '无法注册推送，请重试。';
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }
  Future<void> openSettings() async {
    try {
      await channel.invokeMethod<void>('openSettings');
    } catch (_) {
      error = '无法打开系统设置。';
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _disposed = true;
    channel.setMethodCallHandler(null);
    super.dispose();
  }
}
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_svg/flutter_svg.dart';
const designInterests = ['音乐', '游戏', '影视', '动漫', '旅游', '宠物', '美食', '阅读', '吃瓜'];
class InterestsPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const InterestsPage({
    super.key,
    this.initialSelection = const [],
    required this.onStart,
  });
  final List<String> initialSelection;
  final Future<bool> Function(List<String>) onStart;
  @override
  State<InterestsPage> createState() => _InterestsPageState();
}
class _InterestsPageState extends State<InterestsPage>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late final _float = AnimationController(
    vsync: this,
    duration: const Duration(seconds: 4),
  );
  bool _reduceMotion = false;
  @override
  void didChangeDependencies() {
    super.didChangeDependencies();
    _reduceMotion = MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context);
    if (_reduceMotion) {
      _float.stop();
    } else if (!_float.isAnimating) {
      _float.repeat();
    }
  }
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _float.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }
  late final Set<String> _selected = widget.initialSelection
      .where(designInterests.contains)
      .toSet();
  bool _busy = false;
  final Map<String, int> _ripples = {};
  String? _error;
  void _toggle(String value) {
    if (_busy) return;
    setState(() {
      _ripples[value] = (_ripples[value] ?? 0) + 1;
      _selected.contains(value)
          ? _selected.remove(value)
          : _selected.add(value);
      _error = null;
    });
  }
  Future<void> _start() async {
    if (_busy || _selected.isEmpty) return;
    setState(() {
      _busy = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final ok = await widget.onStart(_selected.toList());
      if (mounted && !ok) setState(() => _error = '保存失败，请重试');
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _error = '保存失败，请重试');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    }
  }
  Widget _svg(String name, double w, double h) => SvgPicture.asset(
    'assets/design/interest_$name.svg',
    width: w,
    height: h,
    excludeFromSemantics: true,
  );
  Widget _bubbles(double width, bool large) {
    const slots = [
      (55.0, 27.0, 91.0),
      (163.0, 0.0, 100.0),
      (277.0, 40.0, 100.0),
      (-7.0, 118.0, 100.0),
      (103.0, 129.0, 109.0),
      (228.0, 145.0, 100.0),
      (24.0, 234.0, 91.0),
      (131.0, 254.0, 100.0),
      (250.0, 254.0, 100.0),
    ];
    Widget bubble(int i, double diameter) {
      final label = designInterests[i];
      final selected = _selected.contains(label);
      return Semantics(
        button: true,
        selected: selected,
        label: label,
        child: GestureDetector(
          key: ValueKey('interest-$label'),
          onTap: _busy ? null : () => _toggle(label),
          child: AnimatedBuilder(
            animation: _float,
            builder: (context, child) {
              final phase = _float.value * 2 * math.pi + i * 1.7;
              return Transform.translate(
                key: ValueKey('interest-motion-$label'),
                offset: _reduceMotion
                    ? Offset.zero
                    : Offset(math.sin(phase) * 5, math.cos(phase) * 9),
                child: child,
              );
            },
            child: AnimatedScale(
              scale: _reduceMotion || selected ? 1 : .96,
              duration: _reduceMotion
                  ? Duration.zero
                  : const Duration(milliseconds: 320),
              curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
              child: Stack(
                clipBehavior: Clip.none,
                alignment: Alignment.center,
                children: [
                  if (!_reduceMotion && (_ripples[label] ?? 0) > 0)
                    Positioned.fill(
                      child: IgnorePointer(
                        child: TweenAnimationBuilder<double>(
                          key: ValueKey('ripple-$label-${_ripples[label]}'),
                          tween: Tween(begin: 0, end: 1),
                          duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 950),
                          builder: (_, progress, _) => CustomPaint(
                            painter: _InterestRipplePainter(progress),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  SizedBox(
                    width: diameter,
                    height: diameter,
                    child: AnimatedSwitcher(
                      duration: _reduceMotion
                          ? Duration.zero
                          : const Duration(milliseconds: 280),
                      child: FittedBox(
                        key: ValueKey(selected),
                        child: _svg(
                          selected
                              ? (i == 4 ? 'selected_large' : 'selected')
                              : (i == 0 || i == 6 ? 'small' : 'circle'),
                          slots[i].$3,
                          slots[i].$3,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  Text(
                    label,
                    style: const TextStyle(
                      color: Colors.white,
                      fontSize: 16,
                      letterSpacing: 1,
                    ),
                  ),
                  Positioned(
                    right: 5,
                    top: 7,
                    child: AnimatedOpacity(
                      opacity: selected ? 1 : 0,
                      duration: _reduceMotion
                          ? Duration.zero
                          : const Duration(milliseconds: 250),
                      child: _svg('star', 16, 18),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      );
    }
    if (large) {
      return Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 16),
        child: Wrap(
          alignment: WrapAlignment.spaceAround,
          spacing: 10,
          runSpacing: 12,
          children: [for (var i = 0; i < slots.length; i++) bubble(i, 100)],
        ),
      );
    }
    final scale = width / 375;
    return SizedBox(
      height: 378 * scale,
      child: Stack(
        clipBehavior: Clip.hardEdge,
        children: [
          for (var i = 0; i < slots.length; i++)
            Positioned(
              left: slots[i].$1 * scale,
              top: (slots[i].$2 + 12) * scale,
              width: slots[i].$3 * scale,
              height: slots[i].$3 * scale,
              child: bubble(i, slots[i].$3 * scale),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

                                        _selected.length ==
                                        designInterests.length;
                                    _selected.clear();

                                  }),

                                    'start',
                                    _busy ? '保存中…' : '开始探索',

                                    _selected.isEmpty ? null : _start,

                  ),

            ],

  Widget _button(
    String asset,
    String label,
    Color color,
    VoidCallback? onTap,
  ) => Stack(
    children: [

          overlayColor: Colors.transparent,
          minimumSize: const Size(double.infinity, 50),
        ),
        child: Text(
          label,
          style: TextStyle(
            color: color,
            fontSize: 16,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    ],
  );
}
class _InterestRipplePainter extends CustomPainter {
  const _InterestRipplePainter(this.progress);
  final double progress;
  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final center = size.center(Offset.zero);
    final radius = math.min(size.width, size.height) / 2;
    for (var i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
      final t = (progress - i * .18) / (1 - i * .18);
      if (t <= 0 || t >= 1) continue;
      final eased = Curves.easeOutCubic.transform(t);
      final paint = Paint()
        ..style = PaintingStyle.stroke
        ..strokeWidth = 2 * (1 - t) + .5
        ..color = (i == 0 ? const Color(0xFF81DFFF) : const Color(0xFFEBA0FF))
            .withValues(alpha: .65 * (1 - t));
      canvas.drawCircle(center, radius * (.96 + .48 * eased), paint);
    }
  }
  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant _InterestRipplePainter oldDelegate) =>
      progress != oldDelegate.progress;
}
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'onboarding_page.dart';
import 'onboarding_pages.dart';
/// Shared by startup and design preview. Completion never marks registration.
class OnboardingFlow extends StatefulWidget {
  OnboardingFlow({
    super.key,
    List<OnboardingPageDefinition>? pages,
    this.initialPageId,
    this.onAdvance,
    required this.onComplete,
    this.busy = false,
    this.error,
  }) : pages = List.unmodifiable(pages ?? onboardingPages) {
    if (this.pages.isEmpty ||
        this.pages.any((page) => page.id.isEmpty) ||
        this.pages.map((page) => page.id).toSet().length != this.pages.length) {
      throw ArgumentError('引导页面列表不能为空，且每页必须有唯一的非空 ID');
    }
  }
  final List<OnboardingPageDefinition> pages;
  final String? initialPageId;
  /// Persist the next ID, or null on completion. False keeps the current page.
  final Future<bool> Function(String? nextPageId)? onAdvance;
  final VoidCallback onComplete;
  final bool busy;
  final String? error;
  @override
  State<OnboardingFlow> createState() => _OnboardingFlowState();
}
class _OnboardingFlowState extends State<OnboardingFlow> {
  late String _pageId;
  bool _advancing = false;
  String? _error;
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _pageId = widget.pages.any((p) => p.id == widget.initialPageId)
        ? widget.initialPageId!
        : widget.pages.first.id;
  }
  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant OnboardingFlow oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    if (!widget.pages.any((p) => p.id == _pageId)) {
      _pageId = widget.pages.first.id;
    }
  }
  Future<void> _next() async {
    if (_advancing || widget.busy) return;
    final index = widget.pages.indexWhere((p) => p.id == _pageId);
    final next = index + 1 < widget.pages.length
        ? widget.pages[index + 1].id
        : null;
    setState(() {
      _advancing = true;
      _error = null;
    });
    try {
      final saved = await widget.onAdvance?.call(next) ?? true;
      if (!mounted || !saved) return;
      if (next == null) {
        widget.onComplete();
      } else {
        setState(() => _pageId = next);
      }
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _error = '保存失败，请重试');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _advancing = false);
    }
  }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final index = widget.pages.indexWhere((p) => p.id == _pageId);
    final page = widget.pages[index];
    return OnboardingPageScope(
      key: ValueKey(page.id),
      index: index,
      count: widget.pages.length,
      nextLabel: page.nextLabel,
      child: page.builder(
        _next,
        widget.busy || _advancing,
        widget.error ?? _error,
      ),
    );
  }
}
import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';
/// Stable IDs survive reordering; builders keep each screen's own artwork.
class OnboardingPageDefinition {
  const OnboardingPageDefinition({
    required this.id,
    required this.nextLabel,
    required this.builder,
  });
  final String id;
  final String nextLabel;
  final Widget Function(VoidCallback onNext, bool busy, String? error) builder;
}
class OnboardingPageScope extends InheritedWidget {
  const OnboardingPageScope({
    super.key,
    required this.index,
    required this.count,
    required this.nextLabel,
    required super.child,
  });
  final int index, count;
  final String nextLabel;
  static OnboardingPageScope? maybeOf(BuildContext context) =>
      context.dependOnInheritedWidgetOfExactType<OnboardingPageScope>();
  @override
  bool updateShouldNotify(OnboardingPageScope oldWidget) =>
      index != oldWidget.index ||
      count != oldWidget.count ||
      nextLabel != oldWidget.nextLabel;
}

final onboardingPages = List<OnboardingPageDefinition>.unmodifiable([

    id: 'welcome',
    nextLabel: '继续了解',
    builder: (onNext, busy, error) =>
        WelcomePage(onNext: onNext, busy: busy, error: error),
  ),
  OnboardingPageDefinition(
    id: 'world',
    nextLabel: '进入这个世界',
    builder: (onNext, busy, error) =>
        WorldPage(onNext: onNext, busy: busy, error: error),
  ),
]);

/// Native text and width-driven artwork; only empty space flexes with height.
class OnboardingScaffold extends StatelessWidget {
  const OnboardingScaffold({
    super.key,
    this.pageIndex = 0,
    required this.onNext,
    required this.header,
    required this.visualBuilder,
    this.textureAsset,
    this.textureRect = Rect.zero,
    required this.spaceWeights,
    this.background = const [],
    this.footerOverlap = 0,
    this.nextLabel = '继续了解',
    this.busy = false,
    this.error,
  });
  final String nextLabel;
  final int pageIndex;
  final VoidCallback onNext;
  final Widget header;
  final Widget Function(double width) visualBuilder;
  final List<Widget> background;
  final String? textureAsset;
  final Rect textureRect;
  final ({int top, int middle, int bottom}) spaceWeights;
  final double footerOverlap;
  final bool busy;
  final String? error;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final page = OnboardingPageScope.maybeOf(context);
    final pageIndex = page?.index ?? this.pageIndex;
    final pageCount = page?.count ?? 2;
    final nextLabel = page?.nextLabel ?? this.nextLabel;
    return AnnotatedRegion<SystemUiOverlayStyle>(
      value: SystemUiOverlayStyle.light.copyWith(
        statusBarColor: Colors.transparent,
        systemNavigationBarColor: const Color(0xFF431E89),
      ),
      child: Scaffold(
        backgroundColor: const Color(0xFF431E89),
        body: LayoutBuilder(
          builder: (context, constraints) {
            final safe = MediaQuery.paddingOf(context);
            final availableWidth = math.max(
              1.0,
              constraints.maxWidth - safe.horizontal,
            );
            final contentWidth = math.min(availableWidth, 600.0);
            final bottomGap = ((constraints.maxHeight - 600) * 28 / 212).clamp(
              12.0,
              28.0,
            );
            final bottom = math.max(34.0, safe.bottom) + bottomGap + 2;
            final buttonHeight = math.max(
              56.0,
              MediaQuery.textScalerOf(context).scale(16) * 1.25 + 24,
            );
            const indicatorHeight = 13.043;
            const indicatorGap = 16.48;
            final footerHeight = buttonHeight + indicatorGap + indicatorHeight;
            final textureScale = constraints.maxWidth / 375;
            return Stack(
              clipBehavior: Clip.hardEdge,
              children: [
                if (background.isNotEmpty)
                  Positioned.fill(
                    child: FittedBox(
                      fit: BoxFit.cover,
                      alignment: Alignment.topCenter,
                      child: SizedBox(
                        width: 375,
                        height: 812,
                        child: Stack(children: background),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                if (textureAsset != null)
                  Positioned(
                    left: textureRect.left * textureScale,
                    top: textureRect.top * textureScale,
                    width: textureRect.width * textureScale,
                    height: textureRect.height * textureScale,
                    child: FittedBox(
                      fit: BoxFit.fill,
                      child: OnboardingLuminosityTexture(
                        asset: textureAsset!,
                        size: textureRect.size,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                Positioned(
                  top: safe.top,
                  left: safe.left,
                  right: safe.right,
                  bottom: bottom + footerHeight - footerOverlap,
                  child: LayoutBuilder(
                    builder: (context, viewport) => SingleChildScrollView(
                      key: ValueKey('onboarding-content-$pageIndex'),
                      child: Center(
                        child: ConstrainedBox(
                          constraints: BoxConstraints(
                            minHeight: viewport.maxHeight,
                            maxWidth: contentWidth,
                          ),
                          child: _FlexibleContent(
                            weights: spaceWeights,
                            children: [header, visualBuilder(contentWidth)],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                Positioned(
                  left: safe.left,
                  right: safe.right,
                  bottom: bottom,
                  child: Center(
                    child: SizedBox(
                      width: math.min(availableWidth * 291 / 375, 466),
                      child: Column(
                        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          Semantics(
                            label: '引导，第 ${pageIndex + 1} 页，共 $pageCount 页',
                            child: pageCount == 2
                                ? Image.asset(
                                    'assets/onboarding/${pageIndex == 0 ? 'indicator' : 'world_indicator'}.png',
                                    width: 30,
                                    height: indicatorHeight,
                                    fit: BoxFit.fill,
                                    excludeFromSemantics: true,
                                  )
                                : SizedBox(
                                    height: indicatorHeight,
                                    child: Row(
                                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                                      children: [
                                        for (var i = 0; i < pageCount; i++)
                                          Container(
                                            width: 11,
                                            height: 11,
                                            margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                              horizontal: 4,
                                            ),
                                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                              shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                              color: i == pageIndex
                                                  ? const Color(0xFF8CC6FF)
                                                  : Colors.white24,
                                            ),
                                          ),
                                      ],
                                    ),
                                  ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: indicatorGap),
                          SizedBox(
                            width: double.infinity,
                            height: buttonHeight,
                            child: FilledButton(
                              onPressed: busy ? null : onNext,
                              style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                                overlayColor: Colors.transparent,
                                backgroundColor: const Color(0xFFF44BE6),
                                disabledBackgroundColor: const Color(
                                  0xFFF44BE6,
                                ),
                                foregroundColor: Colors.white,
                                shape: const StadiumBorder(),
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                  horizontal: 16,
                                ),
                              ),
                              child: Row(
                                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                                children: [
                                  if (busy)
                                    const SizedBox(
                                      width: 22,
                                      height: 22,
                                      child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                                        strokeWidth: 2,
                                        color: Colors.white,
                                      ),
                                    )
                                  else
                                    Image.asset(
                                      'assets/onboarding/next_icon.png',
                                      width: 22,
                                      height: 22,
                                      excludeFromSemantics: true,
                                    ),
                                  const SizedBox(width: 7),
                                  Flexible(
                                    child: Text(
                                      nextLabel,
                                      style: const TextStyle(
                                        fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
                                        letterSpacing: 1,
                                        fontSize: 16,
                                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                if (error != null)
                  Positioned(
                    left: 18,
                    right: 18,
                    bottom: math.max(4, safe.bottom),
                    child: Semantics(
                      liveRegion: true,
                      child: Text(
                        error!,
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: const TextStyle(
                          color: Colors.white,
                          fontSize: 12,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
              ],
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
/// Wraps naturally for narrow screens and accessibility text sizes.
class OnboardingHeader extends StatelessWidget {
  const OnboardingHeader({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    required this.subtitle,
    required this.description,
    required this.left,
    required this.right,
    required this.titleGap,
    required this.descriptionGap,
    required this.descriptionSize,
    required this.descriptionLineHeight,
  });
  final String title, subtitle, description;
  final double left,
      right,
      titleGap,
      descriptionGap,
      descriptionSize,
      descriptionLineHeight;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    const heading = TextStyle(
      fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
      fontSize: 36,
      height: 1.25,
      fontWeight: FontWeight.w500,
      letterSpacing: 0,
      color: Colors.white,
    );
    return Padding(
      padding: EdgeInsets.only(left: left, right: right),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(title, style: heading),
          SizedBox(height: titleGap),
          ShaderMask(
            blendMode: BlendMode.srcIn,
            shaderCallback: (bounds) => const LinearGradient(
              colors: [Color(0xFF7DD5FF), Color(0xFFFF85D6)],
            ).createShader(Rect.fromLTWH(0, 0, 216, bounds.height)),
            child: Text(subtitle, style: heading),
          ),
          SizedBox(height: descriptionGap),
          Text(
            description,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
              fontSize: descriptionSize,
              height: descriptionLineHeight / descriptionSize,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
              letterSpacing: 0,
              color: const Color(0xF5FFFFFF),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
/// Measures actual wrapped content once, then allocates only surplus space.
/// A scroll view supplies unbounded height; when content is taller than the
/// viewport the minimum gaps remain and the scroll view handles the remainder.
class _FlexibleContent extends MultiChildRenderObjectWidget {
  const _FlexibleContent({required this.weights, required super.children});
  final ({int top, int middle, int bottom}) weights;
  @override
  RenderObject createRenderObject(BuildContext context) =>
      _RenderFlexibleContent(weights);
  @override
  void updateRenderObject(
    BuildContext context,
    covariant _RenderFlexibleContent renderObject,
  ) {
    renderObject.weights = weights;
  }
}
class _RenderFlexibleContent extends RenderBox
    with
        ContainerRenderObjectMixin<RenderBox, _OnboardingParentData>,
        RenderBoxContainerDefaultsMixin<RenderBox, _OnboardingParentData> {
  _RenderFlexibleContent(this._weights);
  ({int top, int middle, int bottom}) _weights;
  set weights(({int top, int middle, int bottom}) value) {
    if (_weights == value) return;
    _weights = value;
    markNeedsLayout();
  }
  @override
  void setupParentData(RenderBox child) {
    if (child.parentData is! _OnboardingParentData) {
      child.parentData = _OnboardingParentData();
    }
  }
  @override
  void performLayout() {
    assert(childCount == 2);
    final header = firstChild!;
    final visual = lastChild!;
    final childConstraints = BoxConstraints.tightFor(
      width: constraints.maxWidth,
    );
    header.layout(childConstraints, parentUsesSize: true);
    visual.layout(childConstraints, parentUsesSize: true);
    final naturalHeight = 16 + header.size.height + 12 + visual.size.height;
    size = constraints.constrain(Size(constraints.maxWidth, naturalHeight));
    final spare = math.max(0.0, size.height - naturalHeight);
    final totalWeight = _weights.top + _weights.middle + _weights.bottom;
    final headerTop = 16 + spare * _weights.top / totalWeight;
    (header.parentData! as _OnboardingParentData).offset = Offset(0, headerTop);
    (visual.parentData! as _OnboardingParentData).offset = Offset(
      0,
      headerTop +
          header.size.height +
          12 +
          spare * _weights.middle / totalWeight,
    );
  }
  @override
  void paint(PaintingContext context, Offset offset) =>
      defaultPaint(context, offset);
  @override
  bool hitTestChildren(BoxHitTestResult result, {required Offset position}) =>
      defaultHitTestChildren(result, position: position);
}
class _OnboardingParentData extends ContainerBoxParentData<RenderBox> {}
import 'dart:ui' as ui;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
// Figma uses luminosity blending here; a normal PNG draw shifts dots to pink.
class OnboardingLuminosityTexture extends StatefulWidget {
  const OnboardingLuminosityTexture({
    super.key,
    this.asset = 'dots',
    this.size = const Size(346.34, 272.31),
  });
  final String asset;
  final Size size;
  @override
  State<OnboardingLuminosityTexture> createState() =>
      OnboardingLuminosityTextureState();
}
class OnboardingLuminosityTextureState
    extends State<OnboardingLuminosityTexture> {
  ImageStream? _stream;
  ImageInfo? _image;
  late final _listener = ImageStreamListener((image, synchronousCall) {
    if (!mounted) {
      image.dispose();
      return;
    }
    setState(() {
      _image?.dispose();
      _image = image;
    });
  });
  @override
  void didChangeDependencies() {
    super.didChangeDependencies();
    _resolveImage();
  }
  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant OnboardingLuminosityTexture oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    if (oldWidget.asset != widget.asset) _resolveImage();
  }
  void _resolveImage() {
    final stream = AssetImage('assets/onboarding/${widget.asset}.png')
        .resolve(createLocalImageConfiguration(context));
    if (stream.key == _stream?.key) return;
    _stream?.removeListener(_listener);
    _stream = stream..addListener(_listener);
  }
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _stream?.removeListener(_listener);
    _image?.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) =>
      CustomPaint(size: widget.size, painter: _TexturePainter(_image?.image));
}
class _TexturePainter extends CustomPainter {
  const _TexturePainter(this.image);
  final ui.Image? image;
  @override
  void paint(Canvas canvas, Size size) {
    final texture = image;
    if (texture == null) return;
    canvas.drawImageRect(
      texture,
      Rect.fromLTWH(0, 0, texture.width.toDouble(), texture.height.toDouble()),
      Offset.zero & size,
      Paint()..blendMode = BlendMode.luminosity,
    );
  }
  @override
  bool shouldRepaint(covariant _TexturePainter oldDelegate) =>
      image != oldDelegate.image;
}
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'dart:ui';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'onboarding_scaffold.dart';
/// First introduction screen, Figma 271:1557. Progress belongs to the caller.
class WelcomePage extends StatefulWidget {
  const WelcomePage({
    super.key,
    required this.onNext,
    this.busy = false,
    this.error,
  });
  final VoidCallback onNext;
  final bool busy;
  final String? error;
  static const assetNames = [
    'dots',
    'rings',
    'avatar_purple',
    'avatar_boy',
    'avatar_pink',
    'star_blue',
    'star_small',
    'star_pink',
    'indicator',
    'next_icon',
  ];
  @override
  State<WelcomePage> createState() => _WelcomePageState();
}
class _WelcomePageState extends State<WelcomePage>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late final _motionController = AnimationController(
    vsync: this,
    duration: const Duration(seconds: 234),
  );
  @override
  void didChangeDependencies() {
    super.didChangeDependencies();
    if (MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)) {
      _motionController.stop();
      _motionController.value = 0;
    } else if (!_motionController.isAnimating) {
      _motionController.repeat();
    }
  }
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _motionController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }
  Widget _motion(String name, Widget child) {
    if (name != 'rings' && !name.startsWith('avatar_')) return child;
    return AnimatedBuilder(
      animation: _motionController,
      child: child,
      builder: (context, child) {
        final elapsed = _motionController.value * 234;
        final phase = elapsed / 6 * math.pi * 2;
        if (name == 'rings') {
          final breath = (1 - math.cos(phase)) / 2;
          return Opacity(
            opacity: 1 - breath * 0.15,
            child: Transform.scale(scale: 1 + breath * 0.04, child: child),
          );
        }
        if (MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)) return child!;
        final motion = switch (name) {
          'avatar_boy' => (period: 3.0, phase: 2.1, amplitude: 10.0),
          'avatar_pink' => (period: 2.6, phase: 4.2, amplitude: 8.0),
          _ => (period: 3.6, phase: 0.0, amplitude: 12.0),
        };
        final angle = elapsed / motion.period * math.pi * 2;
        final scale = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width.clamp(0, 375) / 375;
        return Transform.translate(
          offset: Offset(
            math.sin(angle + motion.phase) * 4 * scale,
            math.sin(angle + motion.phase) * motion.amplitude * scale,
          ),
          child: Transform.rotate(
            angle: math.sin(angle + motion.phase + math.pi / 3) * math.pi / 60,
            child: child,
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
  Widget _image(String name, double width, double height) => _motion(
    name,
    RepaintBoundary(
      child: Image.asset(
        'assets/onboarding/$name.png',
        width: width,
        height: height,
        fit: BoxFit.fill,
        excludeFromSemantics: true,
      ),
    ),
  );
  Widget _bubble(double scale) => Transform.rotate(
    angle: -4.89 * math.pi / 180,
    child: ClipRRect(
      borderRadius: const BorderRadius.only(
        topLeft: Radius.circular(16),
        topRight: Radius.circular(16),
        bottomLeft: Radius.circular(5),
        bottomRight: Radius.circular(16),
      ),
      child: BackdropFilter(
        filter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 2, sigmaY: 2),
        child: ColoredBox(
          color: const Color.fromRGBO(255, 255, 255, 0.19),
          child: Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
              horizontal: 22 * scale,
              vertical: 16 * scale,
            ),
            child: const Text(
              '来啦~欢迎小宝加入',
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: TextStyle(
                fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
                letterSpacing: 0,
                fontSize: 16,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w400,
                height: 1,
                color: Colors.white,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
  Widget _visual(double width) {
    final scale = width / 375;
    final largeText = MediaQuery.textScalerOf(context).scale(16) > 20;
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        SizedBox(
          height: 240 * scale,
          child: Stack(
            clipBehavior: Clip.none,
            children: [
              for (final item in [
                (name: 'avatar_purple', x: 19.0, y: 35.0, size: 63.0),
                (name: 'avatar_boy', x: 278.0, y: 147.0, size: 53.0),
                (name: 'avatar_pink', x: 94.0, y: 177.0, size: 40.0),
                (name: 'star_blue', x: 321.0, y: 0.0, size: 16.0),
                (name: 'star_small', x: 53.0, y: 224.0, size: 10.0),
                (name: 'star_pink', x: 37.0, y: 208.0, size: 16.0),
              ])
                Positioned(
                  left: item.x * scale,
                  top: item.y * scale,
                  child: _image(
                    item.name,
                    item.size * scale,
                    item.size * scale,
                  ),
                ),
              if (!largeText)
                Positioned(
                  left: 95.718 * scale,
                  top: 74.584 * scale,
                  width: 180 * scale,
                  child: _bubble(scale),
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        if (largeText)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 12, 24, 12),
            child: _bubble(1),
          ),
      ],
    );
  }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => OnboardingScaffold(
    pageIndex: 0,
    onNext: widget.onNext,
    busy: widget.busy,
    error: widget.error,
    spaceWeights: (top: 8200, middle: 2200, bottom: 8448),
    background: [
      Positioned(
        left: 28.66,
        top: 0,
        width: 346.34,
        height: 272.31,
        child: Image.asset('assets/design/welcome_dots.png', fit: BoxFit.fill),
      ),
      Positioned(left: 0, top: 166, child: _image('rings', 375, 545)),
    ],
    header: const OnboardingHeader(
      title: '遇见懂你的TA,',
      subtitle: '开启专属陪伴',
      description: 'Pals是一个懂你、关心你的AI伙伴,随时和你\n聊天,记住你的喜好与想法,陪伴你一起成长.',
      left: 18,
      right: 38,
      titleGap: 11,
      descriptionGap: 11,
      descriptionSize: 15,
      descriptionLineHeight: 25,
    ),
    visualBuilder: _visual,
  );
}
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'onboarding_scaffold.dart';
/// Second introduction screen, Figma 271:1590. The conversation is sample copy.
class WorldPage extends StatelessWidget {
  const WorldPage({
    super.key,
    required this.onNext,
    this.busy = false,
    this.error,
  });
  final VoidCallback onNext;
  final bool busy;
  final String? error;
  static const assetNames = [
    'world_ghost',
    'world_dots',
    'world_scene',
    'world_characters',
    'world_indicator',
    'world_badge_icon',
    'next_icon',
  ];
  static Widget _image(String name, double width, double height) => Image.asset(
    'assets/${name == 'world_scene' || name == 'world_characters' ? 'design' : 'onboarding'}/$name.png',
    width: width,
    height: height,
    fit: BoxFit.fill,
    excludeFromSemantics: true,
  );
  Widget _visual(BuildContext context, double width) {
    final cardWidth = width - 48;
    final sceneHeight = cardWidth * 173 / 327;
    final characterWidth = width * 140 / 375;
    final characterHeight = characterWidth * 155.72 / 140;
    final largeText = MediaQuery.textScalerOf(context).scale(13) > 16.25;
    // Preserve the reference overlap normally; large text moves the characters
    // below the transcript so expanded lines remain readable.
    final characterSpace = largeText
        ? characterHeight + 16
        : width * 55.72 / 375;
    return Stack(
      clipBehavior: Clip.none,
      children: [
        Padding(
          padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 0, 24, characterSpace),
          child: DecoratedBox(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: const Color.fromRGBO(28, 34, 45, 0.08),
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
              border: Border.all(color: const Color(0xFF916CFF)),
            ),

      descriptionSize: 14,
      descriptionLineHeight: 27,

    ],
  );
}
import 'dart:async';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'plaza_scenarios.dart';
/// A single story per visit; rebuilds and foregrounding do not redraw it.
class PlazaDialogue extends StatefulWidget {
  const PlazaDialogue({super.key, required this.scenario});
  final PlazaScenario scenario;
  @override
  State<PlazaDialogue> createState() => _PlazaDialogueState();
}
class _PlazaDialogueState extends State<PlazaDialogue>
    with WidgetsBindingObserver {
  late final _lines = widget.scenario.lines;
  final _scroll = ScrollController();
  Timer? _timer;
  int _visible = 1;
  int _elapsed = 0;
  bool _foreground = true;
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addObserver(this);
    _foreground =
        WidgetsBinding.instance.lifecycleState == null ||
        WidgetsBinding.instance.lifecycleState == AppLifecycleState.resumed;
    _timer = Timer.periodic(const Duration(milliseconds: 200), (_) {
      if (!_foreground || !(ModalRoute.of(context)?.isCurrent ?? true)) return;
      _elapsed += 200;
      final delay = (1800 + _lines[_visible - 1].text.runes.length * 90).clamp(
        2600,
        6500,
      );
      if (_elapsed < delay) return;
      _elapsed = 0;
      if (_visible >= _lines.length) {
        _timer?.cancel();
        return;
      }
      final follow = !_scroll.hasClients || _scroll.offset < 40;
      setState(() => _visible++);
      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
        if (!mounted || !_scroll.hasClients || !follow) return;
        if (MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)) {
          _scroll.jumpTo(0);
        } else {
          _scroll.animateTo(
            0,
            duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 400),
            curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
          );
        }
      });
    });
  }
  @override
  void didChangeAppLifecycleState(AppLifecycleState state) {
    _foreground = state == AppLifecycleState.resumed;
  }
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _timer?.cancel();
    _scroll.dispose();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.removeObserver(this);
    super.dispose();
  }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => ShaderMask(
    blendMode: BlendMode.dstIn,
    shaderCallback: (rect) => const LinearGradient(
      begin: Alignment.topCenter,
      end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
      colors: [Colors.transparent, Colors.white, Colors.white],
      stops: [0, .07, 1],
    ).createShader(rect),
    child: SingleChildScrollView(
      key: const ValueKey('plaza-content'),
      controller: _scroll,
      reverse: true,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 12),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(31),
              gradient: const LinearGradient(
                colors: [Color(0x824675FF), Color(0x00FF3ED8)],
              ),
            ),
            child: Row(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                Image.asset(
                  'assets/onboarding/world_badge_icon.png',
                  width: 10,
                  height: 9,
                  excludeFromSemantics: true,
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 4),
                const Flexible(
                  child: Text(
                    '欢迎Viitor进入创作广场',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
                      fontSize: 12,
                      height: 17 / 12,
                      color: Colors.white,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          for (var i = 0; i < _visible; i++)
            TweenAnimationBuilder<double>(
              key: ValueKey('plaza-line-$i'),
              tween: Tween(begin: 0, end: 1),
              duration: MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)
                  ? Duration.zero
                  : const Duration(milliseconds: 450),
              builder: (context, value, child) => ClipRect(
                child: Align(
                  alignment: Alignment.bottomLeft,
                  heightFactor: value,
                  child: Opacity(
                    opacity: value,
                    child: Transform.translate(
                      offset: Offset(0, 12 * (1 - value)),
                      child: child,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 10),
                child: Text.rich(
                  TextSpan(
                    children: [
                      if (_lines[i].speaker.isNotEmpty)
                        TextSpan(
                          text: '${_lines[i].speaker}: ',
                          style: const TextStyle(
                            color: Color(0xFF6ADAFF),
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          ),
                        ),
                      TextSpan(text: _lines[i].text),
                    ],
                  ),
                  style: const TextStyle(
                    fontFamily: 'PingFang SC',
                    fontSize: 14,
                    height: 20 / 14,
                    color: Color(0xDEFFFFFF),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    ),
  );
}
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_svg/flutter_svg.dart';
class PlazaDiscovery {
  const PlazaDiscovery({
    required this.title,
    required this.body,
    required this.category,
    this.image = 'assets/design/discovery_illustration.png',
  });
  final String title, body, category, image;
  // Local preview content, until exploration results are supplied by the API.
  static const samples = [
    PlazaDiscovery(
      title: '听说唱，你更在意押韵还是表达？',
      body: '从格莱美说唱类奖项页面寻找作品，比较不同的节奏与叙事。哪句表达让你记住一首歌？不用会唱，也能聊聊自己的感受。',
      category: '游戏·烹饪经营',
    ),
    PlazaDiscovery(
      title: '猫咪到底在想些什么？',
      body: '从耳朵、尾巴和叫声，观察猫咪表达情绪的小细节。你家的猫咪有哪些让你好奇的习惯？一起分享那些可爱的瞬间。',
      category: '宠物·猫咪',
    ),
    PlazaDiscovery(
      title: '用 AI 创作一段属于秋天的故事',
      body: '从一片落叶、一杯热饮或一场偶遇开始，写下故事的第一句。让想象继续生长，看看我们能一起创造怎样的秋天。',
      category: '创作·故事',
    ),
  ];
}
Future<PlazaDiscovery?> showPlazaDiscoveries(
  BuildContext context, {
  List<PlazaDiscovery> discoveries = PlazaDiscovery.samples,
  int initialPage = 0,
}) {
  assert(discoveries.isNotEmpty);
  return showDialog<PlazaDiscovery>(
    context: context,
    barrierColor: const Color(0xB3000000),
    barrierLabel: '关闭发现',
    builder: (_) => PlazaDiscoveryDialog(
      discoveries: discoveries,
      initialPage: initialPage,
    ),
  );
}
/// Figma 355:10554. Text and controls remain native; ornaments are local assets.
class PlazaDiscoveryDialog extends StatefulWidget {
  const PlazaDiscoveryDialog({
    super.key,
    required this.discoveries,
    this.initialPage = 0,
  }) : assert(discoveries.length > 0);
  final List<PlazaDiscovery> discoveries;
  final int initialPage;
  @override
  State<PlazaDiscoveryDialog> createState() => _PlazaDiscoveryDialogState();
}
class _PlazaDiscoveryDialogState extends State<PlazaDiscoveryDialog> {
  late int _selected = widget.initialPage.clamp(
    0,
    widget.discoveries.length - 1,
  );
  late final _pages = PageController(initialPage: _selected);
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _pages.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Dialog(
    backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
    shadowColor: Colors.transparent,
    elevation: 0,
    insetPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
    child: LayoutBuilder(
      builder: (context, constraints) {
        final width = math.min(375.0, constraints.maxWidth);
        final textScale = MediaQuery.textScalerOf(context).scale(21) / 21;
        final height = math.min(
          constraints.maxHeight,
          509.0 + math.max(0, textScale - 1) * 150,
        );
        return SizedBox(
          width: width,
          height: height,
          child: Column(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: PageView.builder(
                  key: const ValueKey('plaza-discovery-pages'),
                  controller: _pages,
                  itemCount: widget.discoveries.length,
                  onPageChanged: (value) => setState(() => _selected = value),
                  itemBuilder: (context, index) => SingleChildScrollView(
                    child: _DiscoveryCard(
                      discovery: widget.discoveries[index],
                      onOpen: () =>
                          Navigator.pop(context, widget.discoveries[index]),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              SizedBox(
                height: 16,
                child: Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    for (
                      var index = 0;
                      index < widget.discoveries.length;
                      index++
                    )
                      Semantics(
                        button: true,
                        selected: index == _selected,
                        label:
                            '第 ${index + 1} 条发现，共 ${widget.discoveries.length} 条',
                        child: GestureDetector(
                          behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
                          onTap: () => _pages.animateToPage(
                            index,
                            duration: MediaQuery.disableAnimationsOf(context)
                                ? Duration.zero
                                : const Duration(milliseconds: 250),
                            curve: Curves.easeOut,
                          ),
                          child: SizedBox(
                            width: 20,
                            height: 16,
                            child: Center(
                              child: Container(
                                width: 10,
                                height: 10,
                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                  color: index == _selected
                                      ? Colors.white
                                      : Colors.transparent,
                                  border: Border.all(
                                    color: Colors.white,
                                    width: .7,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        );
      },
    ),
  );
}
Widget _decoration(String name) => SvgPicture.asset(
  'assets/design/discovery_$name.svg',
  fit: BoxFit.fill,
  excludeFromSemantics: true,
);

import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'dart:ui';
import '../chat/chat_page.dart';
import 'plaza_dialogue.dart';
import 'plaza_discovery.dart';
import 'plaza_scenarios.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_svg/flutter_svg.dart';
/// Plaza artwork with locally scripted conversations from Figma 340:6678.
class PlazaPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const PlazaPage({
    super.key,
    this.onChat,
    this.onVoice,
    this.initialDiscovery,
  });
  final int? initialDiscovery;
  final VoidCallback? onChat, onVoice;
  @override
  State<PlazaPage> createState() => _PlazaPageState();
}
class _PlazaPageState extends State<PlazaPage> {
  static final _picker = PlazaScenarioPicker();
  late final _scenario = _picker.next();
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    if (widget.initialDiscovery != null) {
      WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
        if (mounted) _showDiscovery();
      });
    }
  }
  Future<void> _showDiscovery() async {
    final selected = await showPlazaDiscoveries(
      context,
      initialPage: widget.initialDiscovery ?? 0,
    );
    if (!mounted || selected == null) return;
    await showModalBottomSheet<void>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      showDragHandle: true,
      builder: (context) => SafeArea(
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(24, 8, 24, 32),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [
              Text(
                selected.title,
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              Text(
                selected.body,
                style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 16, height: 1.6),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
              TextButton(
                onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
                child: const Text('返回广场'),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
  void _chat(BuildContext context, bool voice) {
    final callback = voice ? widget.onVoice : widget.onChat;
    if (callback != null) {
      callback();
      return;
    }
    Navigator.of(context).push(
      MaterialPageRoute<void>(
        builder: (chatContext) => ChatPage(
          returningUser: true,
          startRecordingOnOpen: voice,
          onExit: () => Navigator.pop(chatContext),
          onExplore: () => Navigator.pop(chatContext),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

import 'dart:math';
class PlazaLine {
  const PlazaLine(this.speaker, this.text);
  final String speaker, text;
}
class PlazaScenario {
  const PlazaScenario(this.title, this.script);
  final String title, script;
  List<PlazaLine> get lines => script
      .trim()
      .split('\n')
      .map((line) {
        final split = line.indexOf('：');
    return split < 0 || line.startsWith('✦')
            ? PlazaLine('', line)
            : PlazaLine(line.substring(0, split), line.substring(split + 1));
      })
      .toList(growable: false);
}
// Shuffle bags give every story a turn without repeating at a bag boundary.
class PlazaScenarioPicker {
  PlazaScenarioPicker({Random? random}) : _random = random ?? Random();
  final Random _random;
  final List<PlazaScenario> _bag = [];
  PlazaScenario? _last;
  PlazaScenario next() {
    if (_bag.isEmpty) {
      _bag.addAll(plazaScenarios);
      _bag.shuffle(_random);
      if (_bag.last == _last) {
        final first = _bag.first;
        _bag[0] = _bag.last;
        _bag[_bag.length - 1] = first;
      }
    }
    return _last = _bag.removeLast();
  }
}

  PlazaScenario('喷泉里的许愿币', '''
✦ 顾客看见有人拿着收款码站在喷泉旁。
顾客：你在干什么？
阿团：升级许愿池，现在支持扫码了。
顾客：钱不是进你账户吗？
阿团：对，我人工审核愿望。
顾客：我想一夜暴富。
阿团：已驳回，和管理员的愿望冲突。
顾客：那我希望所有愿望都能实现。
阿团：不支持批量提交。
顾客：你自己的愿望实现了吗？
阿团：快了，我许的是今天遇到一个贵人。
顾客：你别看我，我拿手机是准备报警。
阿团：原来贵人还穿制服。
'''),
  PlazaScenario('花店的离职申请', '''

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
/// Applies the designer's source-image crop inside the 375×812 scene viewport.
/// The caller scales the viewport uniformly; the image is never height-squeezed.
class DesignScene extends StatelessWidget {
  const DesignScene({
    super.key,
    required this.asset,
    required this.sourceRect,
    required this.semanticLabel,
  });
  final String asset, semanticLabel;
  final Rect sourceRect;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => LayoutBuilder(
    builder: (context, c) {
      final scale = c.maxWidth / 375;
      return ClipRect(
        child: Stack(
          children: [
            Positioned(
              left: sourceRect.left * scale,
              top: sourceRect.top * scale,
              width: sourceRect.width * scale,
              height: sourceRect.height * scale,
              child: Image.asset(
                asset,
                fit: BoxFit.fill,
                semanticLabel: semanticLabel,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );
    },
  );
}
